FILE: api/deps.py
========================================================================================
"""FastAPI dependencies — Bearer session token → current user."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status
from fastapi.security import HTTPAuthorizationCredentials, HTTPBearer

from modules.authentication.auth_service import AuthenticationError, resolve_session

bearer_scheme = HTTPBearer(auto_error=False)


def get_session_token(
    creds: HTTPAuthorizationCredentials | None = Depends(bearer_scheme),
) -> str:
    if creds is None or not creds.credentials:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Login first, then Authorize with your JWT session_token.",
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
        )
    return creds.credentials.strip()


def get_current_user(token: str = Depends(get_session_token)) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        return resolve_session(token)
    except AuthenticationError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail=str(exc),
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
        ) from exc

========================================================================================
FILE: api/http_errors.py
========================================================================================
"""Map app errors to HTTP responses (shared by all routers)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import HTTPException, status

from modules.authentication.auth_service import AuthenticationError
from utils.errors import AppError, AuthError, NotFoundError, PermissionDeniedError, ValidationError
from utils.logger import get_logger

log = get_logger("api.http_errors")


def raise_http_error(exc: Exception) -> None:
    """Convert known app errors to HTTPException. Always raises."""
    if isinstance(exc, (AuthError, AuthenticationError)):
        detail = getattr(exc, "message", None) or str(exc)
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail=detail,
        ) from exc
    if isinstance(exc, PermissionDeniedError):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail=exc.message,
        ) from exc
    if isinstance(exc, NotFoundError):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=exc.message,
        ) from exc
    if isinstance(exc, FileNotFoundError):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from exc
    if isinstance(exc, ValidationError):
        detail = getattr(exc, "message", None) or str(exc)
        if "No trained model" in detail:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
                detail=detail,
            ) from exc
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            detail=detail,
        ) from exc
    if isinstance(exc, AppError):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            detail=exc.message,
        ) from exc
    # Avoid opaque FastAPI Internal Server Error for unexpected failures
    log.exception("Unhandled API error: %s", exc)
    raise HTTPException(
        status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
        detail=f"Internal error: {exc}",
    ) from exc

========================================================================================
FILE: api/main.py
========================================================================================
"""FastAPI app — Swagger UI at /docs."""

from __future__ import annotations

from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

from pathlib import Path

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request, status
from fastapi.exceptions import RequestValidationError
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse, JSONResponse

from api.http_errors import raise_http_error
from api.routers import (
    admin_router,
    aia_router,
    auth_router,
    cae_router,
    editor_router,
    journalist_router,
    manager_router,
    publisher_router,
)
from config.env_loader import load_dotenv
from modules.authentication.auth_service import AuthenticationError
from utils.errors import AppError
from utils.logger import get_logger

log = get_logger("api")
STATIC_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "static"

OPENAPI_TAGS = [
    {
        "name": "1. Login",
        "description": "Login, logout, change password, admin reset. Authorize with Bearer token.",
    },
    {
        "name": "2. Journalist tools",
        "description": "AI tools + desk content (news/articles/evidence).",
    },
    {
        "name": "3. Audience Insight Analyst",
        "description": "Heuristic engagement/interest, category/trends, performance loop, story pitches.",
    },
    {
        "name": "4. Content Adaptation (CAE)",
        "description": "Adapt articles for social, print, web, and newsletter; review drafts.",
    },
    {
        "name": "5. Publisher",
        "description": "Publication queue, templates, preview, publish, and schedule.",
    },
    {
        "name": "6. Editor",
        "description": "Pitch review, content review, and assignment.",
    },
    {
        "name": "7. Newsroom Manager",
        "description": "Desk overview plus pitch/content review and assignment.",
    },
    {
        "name": "8. Admin",
        "description": "Data pipeline (ingest/clean/persist) and user/category admin.",
    },
]


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(_app: FastAPI):
    load_dotenv()
    log.info("NewsMind API started. Swagger: /docs")
    yield


def create_app() -> FastAPI:
    application = FastAPI(
        title="NewsMind API",
        summary="Login once, Authorize, then use role tools in Swagger.",
        description=(
            "### Quick start\n"
            "1. Open **1. Login** → `POST /auth/login` → Try it out\n"
            "2. Copy `session_token` (JWT) from the response\n"
            "3. Click **Authorize** → paste token → Authorize\n"
            "4. Use role endpoints (Journalist, AIA, CAE, Publisher, Editor, Manager)\n\n"
            "### Password rules (change/reset)\n"
            "Min 10 characters, with uppercase, lowercase, digit, and special character.\n\n"
            "Models: `artifacts/models/editor_queue_urgency/` (editor review urgency) and "
            "`cae/` (unified content adaptation). Engagement/interest/trend are formulas; "
            "category is manual entry (no category model).\n\n"
            "Simple tester UI (easier than Swagger): **/easy**"
        ),
        version="0.3.0",
        lifespan=lifespan,
        docs_url="/docs",
        redoc_url=None,
        openapi_tags=OPENAPI_TAGS,
        swagger_ui_parameters={
            "docExpansion": "list",
            "defaultModelsExpandDepth": -1,
            "defaultModelExpandDepth": 1,
            "filter": True,
            "tryItOutEnabled": True,
            "persistAuthorization": True,
        },
    )

    application.add_middleware(
        CORSMiddleware,
        allow_origins=["*"],
        allow_credentials=True,
        allow_methods=["*"],
        allow_headers=["*"],
    )

    @application.get("/easy", include_in_schema=False)
    def easy_tester() -> FileResponse:
        return FileResponse(STATIC_DIR / "easy.html")

    @application.exception_handler(RequestValidationError)
    async def validation_exception_handler(
        _request: Request,
        exc: RequestValidationError,
    ) -> JSONResponse:
        errors = []
        for err in exc.errors():
            loc = ".".join(str(part) for part in err.get("loc", ()) if part != "body")
            errors.append({"field": loc or "body", "message": err.get("msg", "Invalid value")})
        return JSONResponse(
            status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
            content={"detail": "Validation failed", "errors": errors},
        )

    @application.exception_handler(HTTPException)
    async def http_exception_handler(_request: Request, exc: HTTPException) -> JSONResponse:
        return JSONResponse(status_code=exc.status_code, content={"detail": exc.detail})

    @application.exception_handler(AppError)
    async def app_error_handler(_request: Request, exc: AppError) -> JSONResponse:
        try:
            raise_http_error(exc)
        except HTTPException as http_exc:
            return JSONResponse(
                status_code=http_exc.status_code,
                content={"detail": http_exc.detail},
            )
        return JSONResponse(status_code=400, content={"detail": str(exc)})

    @application.exception_handler(AuthenticationError)
    async def authentication_error_handler(
        _request: Request,
        exc: AuthenticationError,
    ) -> JSONResponse:
        return JSONResponse(status_code=401, content={"detail": str(exc)})

    @application.exception_handler(Exception)
    async def unhandled_exception_handler(_request: Request, exc: Exception) -> JSONResponse:
        log.exception("Unhandled API exception: %s", exc)
        return JSONResponse(
            status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
            content={"detail": f"Internal error: {exc}"},
        )

    application.include_router(auth_router)
    application.include_router(journalist_router)
    application.include_router(aia_router)
    application.include_router(cae_router)
    application.include_router(publisher_router)
    application.include_router(editor_router)
    application.include_router(manager_router)
    application.include_router(admin_router)
    return application


app = create_app()

FILE: api/routers/admin.py

"""Admin pipeline + user/category management."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query

from api.deps import get_session_token
from api.http_errors import raise_http_error
from api.schemas.admin import (
    AddCategoryRequest,
    AddUserRequest,
    AdminActionResponse,
    CleanRequest,
    IngestFileRequest,
    IngestRequest,
    PersistRequest,
    PipelineRequest,
    ResetPasswordRequest,
)
from api.schemas.journalist import RawIngestListResponse
from config.constants import CAP_ANALYTICS, CAP_MANAGE_USERS
from modules.admin import AdminModule
from services.access import require_capability_session
from services.content.content_service import ContentService
from services.user import UserService
from utils.errors import AuthError, NotFoundError, PermissionDeniedError, ValidationError

router = APIRouter(prefix="/admin", tags=["8. Admin"])
_users = UserService()
_content = ContentService()
_APP_ERRORS = (AuthError, PermissionDeniedError, ValidationError, NotFoundError)


def _admin(token: str) -> AdminModule:
    return AdminModule(session_token=token)


def _ok(result: dict[str, Any], *, message: str = "") -> AdminActionResponse:
    return AdminActionResponse(message=message or str(result.get("message") or "OK"), result=result)


@router.get("/csv-files", response_model=AdminActionResponse, summary="List CSV files in data/raw")
def list_csv_files(token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        return _ok(_admin(token).list_csv_files())
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.get(
    "/raw-ingest",
    response_model=RawIngestListResponse,
    summary="List raw ingest staging rows (ETL)",
)
def list_raw_ingest(
    limit: int = Query(default=20, ge=1, le=500),
    token: str = Depends(get_session_token),
):
    try:
        return _admin(token).list_raw_ingest(limit=limit)
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post("/ingest", response_model=AdminActionResponse, summary="Ingest CSV content")
def ingest(body: IngestRequest, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        return _ok(_admin(token).ingest(only_new=body.only_new))
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post("/ingest-file", response_model=AdminActionResponse, summary="Ingest one CSV file")
def ingest_file(body: IngestFileRequest, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        return _ok(_admin(token).ingest_file(body.source_file))
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post("/clean", response_model=AdminActionResponse, summary="Clean ingested data")
def clean(_body: CleanRequest, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        return _ok(_admin(token).clean())
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post("/ingest-and-clean", response_model=AdminActionResponse, summary="Ingest then clean")
def ingest_and_clean(body: IngestRequest, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        return _ok(_admin(token).ingest_and_clean(only_new=body.only_new))
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post("/persist", response_model=AdminActionResponse, summary="Persist cleaned data")
def persist(_body: PersistRequest, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        return _ok(_admin(token).persist())
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post("/pipeline", response_model=AdminActionResponse, summary="Full ETL pipeline")
def pipeline(body: PipelineRequest, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        return _ok(_admin(token).run_pipeline(only_new=body.only_new))
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post("/users", response_model=AdminActionResponse, summary="Create user account")
def add_user(body: AddUserRequest, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        result = _users.add_user(
            username=body.username,
            password=body.password,
            first_name=body.first_name,
            last_name=body.last_name,
            email=body.email,
            role_code=body.role_code,
            employee_code=body.employee_code,
            session_token=token,
        )
        return AdminActionResponse(message=f"User {result['username']} created.", result=result)
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post("/users/reset-password", response_model=AdminActionResponse, summary="Reset user password")
def reset_user_password(body: ResetPasswordRequest, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        result = _users.reset_password(body.username, body.new_password, session_token=token)
        return AdminActionResponse(
            message=f"Password reset for {body.username}.",
            result=result,
        )
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.get(
    "/users/{username}",
    response_model=AdminActionResponse,
    summary="Look up user by username",
)
def get_user(username: str, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        require_capability_session(CAP_MANAGE_USERS, token)
        result = _users.find_user(username)
        return AdminActionResponse(message="OK", result=result)
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post("/categories", response_model=AdminActionResponse, summary="Add content category")
def add_category(body: AddCategoryRequest, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        # Admin + newsroom manager (+ desks with analytics) may add categories
        require_capability_session(CAP_ANALYTICS, token)
        category_id = _content.add_category(body.name, body.description)
        return AdminActionResponse(
            message="Category created.",
            result={"category_id": category_id, "name": body.name},
        )
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)

FILE: api/routers/aia.py

"""Audience Insight Analyst endpoints."""

from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends

from api.deps import get_session_token
from api.http_errors import raise_http_error
from api.schemas.aia import (
    EngagementResponse,
    InterestResponse,
    MetricsRequest,
    PerformanceAnalyticsResponse,
    StoryPitchListResponse,
    StoryPitchRequest,
    StoryPitchResponse,
    TrendRequest,
    TrendResponse,
)
from config.constants import CAP_ANALYTICS
from services.access import require_capability_session
from services.aia import AIAService
from utils.errors import AuthError, NotFoundError, PermissionDeniedError, ValidationError

router = APIRouter(prefix="/aia", tags=["3. Audience Insight Analyst"])
_service = AIAService()
_APP_ERRORS = (AuthError, PermissionDeniedError, ValidationError, NotFoundError)


def _require_aia(token: str) -> None:
    require_capability_session(CAP_ANALYTICS, token)


@router.post("/analyze-engagement", response_model=EngagementResponse, summary="Analyze engagement")
def analyze_engagement(
    body: MetricsRequest | None = None,
    token: str = Depends(get_session_token),
):
    try:
        _require_aia(token)
        metrics = body or MetricsRequest()
        return _service.analyze_engagement(
            views=metrics.views,
            likes=metrics.likes,
            shares=metrics.shares,
            comments=metrics.comments,
        )
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post("/analyze-interest", response_model=InterestResponse, summary="Analyze interest")
def analyze_interest(
    body: MetricsRequest | None = None,
    token: str = Depends(get_session_token),
):
    try:
        _require_aia(token)
        metrics = body or MetricsRequest()
        return _service.analyze_interest(
            views=metrics.views,
            likes=metrics.likes,
            shares=metrics.shares,
            comments=metrics.comments,
        )
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post("/analyze-trends", response_model=TrendResponse, summary="Analyze trends")

========================================================================================
FILE: api/routers/auth.py
========================================================================================
"""Login, logout, change password, admin reset."""

from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status

from api.deps import get_current_user, get_session_token
from api.http_errors import raise_http_error
from api.schemas.auth import (
    AdminResetPasswordRequest,
    ChangePasswordRequest,
    LoginRequest,
    LoginResponse,
    MessageResponse,
)
from config.constants import CAP_MANAGE_USERS
from modules.authentication.auth_service import (
    AuthenticationError,
    authenticate,
    change_password,
    logout,
)
from services.access import require_capability_session
from services.user import UserService
from utils.errors import AuthError, PermissionDeniedError, ValidationError

router = APIRouter(prefix="/auth", tags=["1. Login"])
_APP_ERRORS = (AuthError, PermissionDeniedError, ValidationError)


@router.post(
    "/login",
    response_model=LoginResponse,
    summary="Login",
    description="Returns a JWT in `session_token`. Click **Authorize**, choose Bearer, paste the token.",
)
def login(body: LoginRequest) -> LoginResponse:
    try:
        payload = authenticate(body.username, body.password)
    except AuthenticationError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail=str(exc)) from exc
    return LoginResponse(
        session_token=str(payload["session_token"]),
        user_id=int(payload["user_id"]),
        username=str(payload["username"]),
        role_code=str(payload["role_code"]),
        capabilities=list(payload.get("capabilities") or []),
    )


@router.post("/logout", response_model=MessageResponse, summary="Logout")
def do_logout(token: str = Depends(get_session_token)) -> MessageResponse:
    logout(token)
    return MessageResponse(message="Logged out.")


@router.post("/change-password", response_model=MessageResponse, summary="Change password")
def do_change_password(
    body: ChangePasswordRequest,
    user: dict = Depends(get_current_user),
):
    try:
        change_password(int(user["user_id"]), body.current_password, body.new_password)
        return MessageResponse(message="Password changed.")
    except ValidationError as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)
    except AuthenticationError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from exc


@router.post(
    "/admin/reset-password",
    response_model=MessageResponse,
    summary="Admin reset password",
)
def do_admin_reset_password(
    body: AdminResetPasswordRequest,
    token: str = Depends(get_session_token),
):
    try:
        require_capability_session(CAP_MANAGE_USERS, token)
        UserService().reset_password(body.username, body.new_password, session_token=token)
        return MessageResponse(message=f"Password reset for {body.username}.")
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)

========================================================================================
FILE: api/routers/cae.py
========================================================================================
"""Content Adaptation Editor endpoints."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, status

from api.deps import get_session_token
from api.http_errors import raise_http_error
from api.schemas.cae import (
    AdaptationCreate,
    AdaptationResponse,
    DraftResponse,
    FinalVersionRequest,
    RemarksRequest,
    RequiredRemarksRequest,
)
from config.constants import CAP_ADAPT
from services.access import require_capability_session
from services.adaptation import AdaptationService, ReviewService
from utils.errors import AuthError, NotFoundError, PermissionDeniedError, ValidationError

router = APIRouter(prefix="/cae", tags=["4. Content Adaptation (CAE)"])
_adapt = AdaptationService()
_review = ReviewService()
_APP_ERRORS = (AuthError, PermissionDeniedError, ValidationError, NotFoundError)


def _user(token: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return require_capability_session(CAP_ADAPT, token)


def _draft_response(row: dict[str, Any]) -> DraftResponse:
    params = row.get("generation_parameters") or {}
    flags = row.get("validation_flags") or {}
    if isinstance(params, str):
        import json

        params = json.loads(params)
    if isinstance(flags, str):
        import json

        flags = json.loads(flags)
    return DraftResponse(
        id=int(row["id"]),
        platform=str(row["platform"]),
        version_number=int(row["version_number"]),
        adapted_title=str(row["adapted_title"]),
        adapted_content=str(row["adapted_content"]),
        model_version=str(row["model_version"]),
        status=str(row["status"]),
        generation_parameters=dict(params),
        validation_flags=dict(flags),
        created_at=row.get("created_at"),
    )


def _adaptation_response(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> AdaptationResponse:
    return AdaptationResponse(
        id=int(payload["id"]),
        original_title=str(payload["original_title"]),
        original_content=str(payload["original_content"]),
        created_by=payload.get("created_by"),
        status=str(payload["status"]),
        created_at=payload.get("created_at"),
        drafts=[_draft_response(d) for d in payload.get("drafts") or []],
    )


@router.post(
    "/adaptations",
    response_model=AdaptationResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
    summary="Create adaptations",
)
def create_adaptation(body: AdaptationCreate, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        user = _user(token)
        result = _adapt.create(
            original_title=body.original_title,
            original_content=body.original_content,
            target_platforms=body.target_platforms,
            created_by=int(user["user_id"]),
        )
        return _adaptation_response(result)
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.get(
    "/adaptations/{request_id}",
    response_model=AdaptationResponse,
    summary="Get adaptation request",
)
def read_adaptation(request_id: int, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        _user(token)
        return _adaptation_response(_adapt.get_request(request_id))
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post(
    "/adaptation-drafts/{draft_id}/approve",
    response_model=DraftResponse,
    summary="Approve draft",
)
def approve_draft(
    draft_id: int,
    body: RemarksRequest | None = None,
    token: str = Depends(get_session_token),
):
    try:
        user = _user(token)
        remarks = body.remarks if body else None
        return _draft_response(_review.approve(draft_id, int(user["user_id"]), remarks))
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post(
    "/adaptation-drafts/{draft_id}/reject",
    response_model=DraftResponse,
    summary="Reject draft",
)
def reject_draft(
    draft_id: int,
    body: RequiredRemarksRequest,
    token: str = Depends(get_session_token),
):
    try:
        user = _user(token)
        return _draft_response(_review.reject(draft_id, int(user["user_id"]), body.remarks))
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post(
    "/adaptation-drafts/{draft_id}/regenerate",
    response_model=DraftResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
    summary="Regenerate draft",
)
def regenerate_draft(
    draft_id: int,
    body: RequiredRemarksRequest,
    token: str = Depends(get_session_token),
):
    try:
        user = _user(token)
        return _draft_response(
            _review.regenerate(draft_id, int(user["user_id"]), body.remarks)
        )
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.put(
    "/adaptation-drafts/{draft_id}/final",
    response_model=DraftResponse,
    summary="Finalize draft",
)
def finalize_draft(
    draft_id: int,
    body: FinalVersionRequest,
    token: str = Depends(get_session_token),
):
    try:
        user = _user(token)
        return _draft_response(
            _review.finalize(
                draft_id,
                int(user["user_id"]),
                body.final_title,
                body.final_content,
                body.remarks,
            )
        )
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.get(
    "/adaptation-drafts/{draft_id}/versions",
    response_model=list[DraftResponse],
    summary="List draft versions",
)
def draft_versions(draft_id: int, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        _user(token)
        return [_draft_response(row) for row in _review.versions(draft_id)]
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)

"""Journalist tools + desk content endpoints."""

from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query

from api.http_errors import raise_http_error
from api.schemas.journalist import (
    ArticleCreateRequest,
    ContentCreateResponse,
    ContentItemResponse,
    ContentListResponse,
    ContentStatusRequest,
    ContentStatusResponse,
    CredibilityRequest,
    CredibilityResponse,
    DuplicatesRequest,
    EvidenceCreateRequest,
    EvidenceCreateResponse,
    NewsCreateRequest,
    PairsResponse,
    RelatedContentRequest,
    RelatedContentResponse,
    ValidateRequest,
    ValidateResponse,
)
from modules.journalist import JournalistModule

router = APIRouter(prefix="/journalist", tags=["2. Journalist tools"])

_APP_ERRORS = (AuthError, PermissionDeniedError, ValidationError, NotFoundError)


def _tools(token: str) -> JournalistModule:
    return JournalistModule(session_token=token)


def _as_dicts(items) -> list[dict]:
    return [item.model_dump() for item in items]


# ── AI tools ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────


@router.post("/duplicates", response_model=PairsResponse, summary="Find duplicates")
def duplicates(body: DuplicatesRequest, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        return _tools(token).duplicates(
            _as_dicts(body.documents),
            threshold=body.threshold,
            limit=body.limit,
        )
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)


@router.post("/validate", response_model=ValidateResponse, summary="Validate article fields")
def validate(body: ValidateRequest, token: str = Depends(get_session_token)):
    try:
        return _tools(token).validate(body.model_dump())
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)

    "/related-content",
    response_model=RelatedContentResponse,
    summary="Related news from published content",
)

    try:
        return _tools(token).related_content(
            title=body.title,
            text=body.text,
            limit=body.limit,
        )
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)

)

    try:
        return _tools(token).credibility(body.content_id, limit=body.limit)
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)

    summary="Deadline risk from time stuck in stage",
)
def deadline_risk(
    submitted_hours: float = Query(default=48.0, ge=1.0, le=720.0),
    rework_hours: float = Query(default=24.0, ge=1.0, le=720.0),
    token: str = Depends(get_session_token),
):
    try:
        _user(token, CAP_ANALYTICS)
        return _service.deadline_risk(
            submitted_hours=submitted_hours,
            rework_hours=rework_hours,
        )
    except _APP_ERRORS as exc:
        raise_http_error(exc)

from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends

from api.deps import get_session_token
from api.http_errors import raise_http_error
from api.schemas.publisher import (
    AnalyticsResponse,
    ArticlesResponse,
    PreviewResponse,

    TemplateAssignRequest,
    TemplatesResponse,

from services.access import require_capability_session
from services.publisher import PublisherService
from utils.errors import AuthError, NotFoundError, PermissionDeniedError, ValidationError

router = APIRouter(prefix="/publisher", tags=["5. Publisher"])
_service = PublisherService()
_APP_ERRORS = (AuthError, PermissionDeniedError, ValidationError, NotFoundError)

    require_capability_session(CAP_PUBLISH, token)

========================================================================================
FILE: api/schemas/__init__.py
========================================================================================
"""Pydantic request/response models for FastAPI routers.

Schemas are **not** business logic. They define:
  - what JSON a client may send (request models)
  - what JSON the API returns (response models)
  - field validation (types, min length, allowed status values via `rules.py`)

Routers import schemas for `response_model=` and body parameters.
Domain work stays in `services/` / `modules/` / `ml/`.
"""

========================================================================================
FILE: api/schemas/admin.py
========================================================================================
"""Admin / pipeline request and response models."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field

from api.schemas.rules import StrongPasswordStr, UsernameStr


class IngestRequest(BaseModel):
    only_new: bool = False


class IngestFileRequest(BaseModel):
    source_file: str = Field(..., min_length=1, description="CSV path relative to data/raw")


class PipelineRequest(BaseModel):
    only_new: bool = False


class CleanRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")


class PersistRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")


class AddUserRequest(BaseModel):
    username: UsernameStr
    password: StrongPasswordStr
    first_name: str = Field(..., min_length=1)
    last_name: str = Field(..., min_length=1)
    email: str = Field(..., min_length=3)
    role_code: str = Field(default="journalist", min_length=1)
    employee_code: str | None = None


class ResetPasswordRequest(BaseModel):
    username: UsernameStr
    new_password: StrongPasswordStr


class AddCategoryRequest(BaseModel):
    name: str = Field(..., min_length=1)
    description: str = ""


class AdminActionResponse(BaseModel):
    message: str = ""
    result: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)

"""Auth request/response bodies."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field

from api.schemas.common import MessageResponse
from api.schemas.rules import LoginPasswordStr, StrongPasswordStr, UsernameStr

__all__ = [
    "LoginRequest",
    "LoginResponse",
    "ChangePasswordRequest",
    "AdminResetPasswordRequest",
    "MessageResponse",
]


class LoginRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(
        json_schema_extra={
            "examples": [
                {"username": "journalist", "password": "Journalist@NewsMind2026!"}
            ]
        }
    )

    username: UsernameStr
    password: LoginPasswordStr


class LoginResponse(BaseModel):
    session_token: str = Field(
        description="JWT access token. Paste into Swagger Authorize as Bearer token."
    )
    user_id: int
    username: str
    role_code: str
    capabilities: list[str] = []


class ChangePasswordRequest(BaseModel):
    current_password: LoginPasswordStr
    new_password: StrongPasswordStr = Field(
        ...,
        description="Min 10 chars with upper, lower, digit, and special character.",
    )


class AdminResetPasswordRequest(BaseModel):
    username: UsernameStr
    new_password: StrongPasswordStr = Field(
        ...,
        description="Min 10 chars with upper, lower, digit, and special character.",
    )

========================================================================================
FILE: api/schemas/cae.py
========================================================================================
"""CAE request/response models."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, field_validator

from config.constants import ADAPTATION_PLATFORMS


class AdaptationCreate(BaseModel):
    original_title: str = Field(..., min_length=5, max_length=500)
    original_content: str = Field(..., min_length=20)
    target_platforms: list[str] = Field(..., min_length=1)

    @field_validator("target_platforms")
    @classmethod
    def validate_platforms(cls, value: list[str]) -> list[str]:
        normalized = [str(v).strip().upper() for v in value]
        if len(set(normalized)) != len(normalized):
            raise ValueError("target_platforms must not contain duplicates")
        bad = [p for p in normalized if p not in ADAPTATION_PLATFORMS]
        if bad:
            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported platforms: {bad}")
        return normalized


class DraftResponse(BaseModel):
    id: int
    platform: str
    version_number: int
    adapted_title: str
    adapted_content: str
    model_version: str
    status: str
    generation_parameters: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    validation_flags: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    created_at: datetime | None = None


class AdaptationResponse(BaseModel):
    id: int
    original_title: str
    original_content: str
    created_by: int | None = None
    status: str
    created_at: datetime | None = None
    drafts: list[DraftResponse] = Field(default_factory=list)


class RemarksRequest(BaseModel):
    remarks: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=2000)


class RequiredRemarksRequest(BaseModel):
    remarks: str = Field(..., min_length=3, max_length=2000)


class FinalVersionRequest(BaseModel):
    final_title: str = Field(..., min_length=5, max_length=500)
    final_content: str = Field(..., min_length=20)
    remarks: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=2000)

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field


class DocumentItem(BaseModel):
    """One article or evidence snippet for search / compare."""

        json_schema_extra={
            "examples": [
                {
                    "id": 1,
                    "title": "Budget vote in parliament",
                    "text": "Parliament held a budget vote today.",
                }
            ]
        }
    )

    id: int | str | None = None
    title: str = ""
    text: str = ""

    message: str


class RankedListResponse(BaseModel):
    actor: str
    results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

"""Journalist endpoint request/response models."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field

from api.schemas.common import DocumentItem
from api.schemas.rules import (
    ArticleTypeStr,
    BodyStr,
    ContentStatusStr,
    SeverityStr,
    SummaryStr,
    TitleStr,
)

__all__ = [
    "DocumentItem",
    "DuplicatesRequest",
    "ValidateRequest",
    "RelatedContentRequest",
    "RelatedContentResponse",
    "CredibilityRequest",
    "PairsResponse",
    "ValidateResponse",
    "CredibilityResponse",
    "EvidenceItemIn",
    "NewsCreateRequest",
    "ArticleCreateRequest",
    "EvidenceCreateRequest",
    "ContentStatusRequest",
    "ContentCreateResponse",
    "ContentItemResponse",
    "ContentListResponse",
    "RawIngestListResponse",
    "EvidenceCreateResponse",
    "ContentStatusResponse",
]


class DuplicatesRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(
        json_schema_extra={
            "examples": [
                {
                    "documents": [
                        {"id": 1, "title": "A", "text": "same story about the city council"},
                        {"id": 2, "title": "B", "text": "same story about the city council"},
                    ],
                    "threshold": 0.5,
                    "limit": 10,
                }
            ]
        }
    )

    documents: list[DocumentItem] = Field(default_factory=list)
    threshold: float = Field(default=0.5, ge=0.0, le=1.0)
    limit: int = Field(default=20, ge=1, le=100)


class ValidateRequest(BaseModel):
    title: str = ""
    text: str = ""
    status: str = "draft"
    category: str | None = None


class RelatedContentRequest(BaseModel):
    """While-typing related news via TF-IDF + cosine on published content."""

    title: str = ""
    text: str = ""
    limit: int = Field(default=8, ge=1, le=50)


class RelatedContentResponse(BaseModel):
    actor: str
    matches: list[dict[str, Any]] = Field(default_factory=list)
    relevancy: str = "none"
    best_score: float = 0.0
    corpus_size: int = 0
    knowledge_base_size: int = 0  # alias: published content count
    source: str = "content"
    method: str = "tfidf_cosine"


class CredibilityRequest(BaseModel):
    """Assess one news/article against the evidence already attached to it."""

    content_id: int = Field(..., gt=0)
    limit: int = Field(default=5, ge=1, le=50)


class PairsResponse(BaseModel):
    actor: str
    pairs: list[dict[str, Any]] = []


class ValidateResponse(BaseModel):
    actor: str
    ok: bool
    errors: list[str] = []
    title: str = ""
    relevancy: str | None = None
    relevancy_score: float | None = None
    relevancy_matches: list[dict[str, Any]] = Field(default_factory=list)
    knowledge_base_size: int | None = None
    source: str | None = None


class CredibilityResponse(BaseModel):
    actor: str
    content_id: int
    evidence_count: int = 0
    claims: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    summary: str = ""


class EvidenceItemIn(BaseModel):
    """Journalist-supplied evidence on news create (not from KB/datasets)."""

    title: TitleStr
    evidence_type: str = Field(default="document", min_length=2, max_length=64)
    source_url: str = Field(default="", max_length=1024)
    text: str = Field(default="", max_length=50_000, description="Optional plain body text")


class NewsCreateRequest(BaseModel):
    title: TitleStr
    body: BodyStr
    summary: SummaryStr = ""
    event_title: str = ""
    event_type: str = Field(default="city", min_length=2, max_length=64)
    event_location: str = Field(default="", max_length=255)
    severity: SeverityStr = "low"
    breaking: bool = False
    status: ContentStatusStr = "draft"
    evidence: list[EvidenceItemIn] = Field(..., min_length=1)


class ArticleCreateRequest(BaseModel):
    title: TitleStr
    body: BodyStr
    summary: SummaryStr = ""
    article_type: ArticleTypeStr = "feature"
    read_time: int = Field(default=3, ge=1, le=120)
    status: ContentStatusStr = "draft"
    evidence: list[EvidenceItemIn] = Field(..., min_length=1)


class EvidenceCreateRequest(BaseModel):
    content_id: int = Field(..., gt=0)
    title: TitleStr
    evidence_type: str = Field(default="document", min_length=2, max_length=64)
    source_url: str = Field(default="", max_length=1024)
    text: str = Field(default="", max_length=50_000)


class ContentStatusRequest(BaseModel):
    status: ContentStatusStr = Field(..., examples=["submitted"])


class ContentCreateResponse(BaseModel):
    actor: str
    content_id: int
    news_id: int | None = None
    article_id: int | None = None
    evidence_ids: list[int] = Field(default_factory=list)


class ContentItemResponse(BaseModel):
    actor: str
    content: dict[str, Any]


class ContentListResponse(BaseModel):
    actor: str
    items: list[dict[str, Any]] = []


class RawIngestListResponse(BaseModel):
    actor: str
    items: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    counts: dict[str, int] = Field(default_factory=dict)


class EvidenceCreateResponse(BaseModel):
    actor: str
    evidence_id: int


class ContentStatusResponse(BaseModel):
    actor: str
    content_id: int
    status: str
    title: str | None = None

class TemplatesResponse(BaseModel):
    templates: list[dict[str, Any]]


class PublicationActionResponse(BaseModel):
    content_id: int
    publication: dict[str, Any]


class PreviewResponse(BaseModel):
    content_id: int
    title: str | None = None
    template: dict[str, Any]
    preview_html: str
    publication_status: str | None = None


class AnalyticsResponse(BaseModel):
    approved_content: int
    publications_by_status: dict[str, int]
    published: int
    scheduled: int
    ready: int
    approved: int

========================================================================================
FILE: api/schemas/rules.py
========================================================================================
"""Shared Pydantic field rules used across API schemas."""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from typing import Annotated

from pydantic import AfterValidator, Field, StringConstraints

from config.constants import ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES, MIN_BODY_LENGTH, MIN_TITLE_LENGTH
from utils.errors import ValidationError as AppValidationError
from utils.validators import (
    ALLOWED_ARTICLE_TYPES,
    ALLOWED_PRIORITIES,
    ALLOWED_SEVERITIES,
    require_password,
)

UsernameStr = Annotated[
    str,
    StringConstraints(strip_whitespace=True, min_length=3, max_length=64),
]

# Login accepts any non-empty password (existing accounts may predate stricter rules).
LoginPasswordStr = Annotated[str, StringConstraints(min_length=1, max_length=128)]

TitleStr = Annotated[
    str,
    StringConstraints(strip_whitespace=True, min_length=MIN_TITLE_LENGTH, max_length=512),
]

BodyStr = Annotated[
    str,
    StringConstraints(strip_whitespace=True, min_length=MIN_BODY_LENGTH, max_length=50_000),
]

SummaryStr = Annotated[str, StringConstraints(strip_whitespace=True, max_length=4000)]


def _strong_password(value: str) -> str:
    try:
        return require_password(value)
    except AppValidationError as exc:
        raise ValueError(exc.message) from exc


StrongPasswordStr = Annotated[str, AfterValidator(_strong_password)]


def _content_status(value: str) -> str:
    text = (value or "").strip().lower()
    if text not in ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES:
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid status. Allowed: {', '.join(ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES)}")
    return text


def _severity(value: str) -> str:
    text = (value or "").strip().lower()
    if text not in ALLOWED_SEVERITIES:
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid severity. Allowed: {', '.join(ALLOWED_SEVERITIES)}")
    return text


def _article_type(value: str) -> str:
    text = (value or "").strip().lower()
    if text not in ALLOWED_ARTICLE_TYPES:
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid article_type. Allowed: {', '.join(ALLOWED_ARTICLE_TYPES)}")
    return text


def _priority(value: str) -> str:
    text = (value or "").strip().lower()
    if text not in ALLOWED_PRIORITIES:
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid priority. Allowed: {', '.join(ALLOWED_PRIORITIES)}")
    return text


ContentStatusStr = Annotated[str, AfterValidator(_content_status)]
SeverityStr = Annotated[str, AfterValidator(_severity)]
ArticleTypeStr = Annotated[str, AfterValidator(_article_type)]
PriorityStr = Annotated[str, AfterValidator(_priority)]

ScoreFloat = Annotated[float, Field(ge=0, le=1000)]
NonNegFloat = Annotated[float, Field(ge=0)]

            { key: "only_new", label: "Only new (true/false)", type: "text", value: "true" },
          ]},
          { id: "ad-users", title: "Add user", method: "POST", path: "/admin/users", role: "admin", fields: [

            { key: "password", label: "Password", type: "text", value: "TempPass@NewsMind2026!" },
            { key: "first_name", label: "First name", type: "text", value: "Admin" },
            { key: "last_name", label: "Last name", type: "text", value: "Created" },
            { key: "email", label: "Email", type: "text", value: "admin.created1@newsmind.local" },
            { key: "role_code", label: "Role code", type: "text", value: "journalist" },
          ]},
          { id: "ad-reset", title: "Reset password", method: "POST", path: "/admin/users/reset-password", role: "admin", fields: [
            { key: "username", label: "Username", type: "text", value: "journalist" },
            { key: "new_password", label: "New password", type: "text", value: "Journalist@NewsMind2026!" },
          ]},
          { id: "ad-user-get", title: "Look up user", method: "GET", path: "/admin/users/{username}", role: "admin", fields: [
            { key: "username", label: "Username", type: "text", value: "journalist", in: "path" },
          ]},
          { id: "ad-cat", title: "Add category", method: "POST", path: "/admin/categories", role: "admin", fields: [
            { key: "name", label: "Category name", type: "text", value: "Local" },
            { key: "description", label: "Description", type: "text", value: "Local desk topics" },
          ]},
        ],
      },
    ];

    const state = {
      token: localStorage.getItem("nm_token") || "",
      user: localStorage.getItem("nm_user") || "",
      role: localStorage.getItem("nm_role") || "",
      currentId: "j-related",
    };

    const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);

    function findEndpoint(id) {
      for (const g of GROUPS) {
        const hit = g.endpoints.find((e) => e.id === id);
        if (hit) return hit;
      }
      return GROUPS[0].endpoints[0];
    }

    function renderNav() {
      const nav = $("nav");
      nav.innerHTML = "";
      for (const g of GROUPS) {
        const h = document.createElement("h3");
        h.textContent = g.name;
        nav.appendChild(h);
        for (const ep of g.endpoints) {
          const wrap = document.createElement("div");
          wrap.className = "ep";
          const btn = document.createElement("button");
          btn.type = "button";
          btn.className = "ghost" + (ep.id === state.currentId ? " active" : "");
          btn.innerHTML = `<span class="method ${ep.method}">${ep.method}</span>${ep.title}`;
          btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
            state.currentId = ep.id;
            renderNav();
            renderForm();
          });
          wrap.appendChild(btn);
          nav.appendChild(wrap);
        }
      }
    }

    function fieldHtml(f) {
      const val = f.value ?? "";
      if (f.type === "textarea") {
        return `<label>${f.label}<textarea data-key="${f.key}" data-in="${f.in || "body"}">${escapeHtml(String(val))}</textarea></label>`;
      }
      return `<label>${f.label}<input data-key="${f.key}" data-in="${f.in || "body"}" type="${f.type === "number" ? "number" : "text"}" value="${escapeAttr(String(val))}" /></label>`;
    }

    function escapeHtml(s) {
      return s.replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;");
    }
    function escapeAttr(s) {
      return s.replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/"/g, "&quot;");
    }

    function renderForm() {
      const ep = findEndpoint(state.currentId);
      const fields = ep.fields || [];
      const pairs = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < fields.length; i += 2) {
        if (fields[i].type === "textarea" || (fields[i + 1] && fields[i + 1].type === "textarea") || !fields[i + 1]) {
          pairs.push(`<div class="fields">${fieldHtml(fields[i])}</div>`);
          if (fields[i + 1]) pairs.push(`<div class="fields">${fieldHtml(fields[i + 1])}</div>`);
        } else {
          pairs.push(`<div class="row2">${fieldHtml(fields[i])}${fieldHtml(fields[i + 1])}</div>`);
        }
      }
      $("formPanel").innerHTML = `
        <h2>${ep.title}</h2>
        <p class="pathline"><span class="method ${ep.method}">${ep.method}</span> ${ep.path}</p>
        <p class="who">Typical role: <strong>${ep.role}</strong> — admin can run every endpoint</p>
        <div class="fields">${pairs.join("") || "<p class='who'>No input fields — just click Run.</p>"}</div>
        <div class="actions"><button type="button" id="runBtn">Run request</button></div>
      `;
      $("runBtn").addEventListener("click", () => runEndpoint(ep));
    }

    function readFields(ep) {
      const pathVals = {};
      const query = {};
      const body = {};
      $("formPanel").querySelectorAll("[data-key]").forEach((el) => {
        const key = el.dataset.key;
        const where = el.dataset.in || "body";
        let val = el.value;
        if (el.type === "number" && val !== "") val = Number(val);
        if (where === "path") pathVals[key] = val;
        else if (where === "query") {
          if (val !== "" && val !== null && val !== undefined) query[key] = val;
        } else if (val !== "" || key === "text") {
          body[key] = val;
        }
      });

      if (body.documents_json !== undefined) {
        try { body.documents = JSON.parse(body.documents_json || "[]"); }
        catch { throw new Error("Documents JSON is invalid"); }
        delete body.documents_json;
      }
      if (body.evidence_json !== undefined) {
        try { body.evidence = JSON.parse(body.evidence_json || "[]"); }
        catch { throw new Error("Evidence JSON is invalid"); }
        delete body.evidence_json;
      }
      if (body.only_new !== undefined && typeof body.only_new === "string") {
        body.only_new = ["1", "true", "yes", "y"].includes(String(body.only_new).toLowerCase());
      }
      if (body.target_platforms !== undefined && typeof body.target_platforms === "string") {
        body.target_platforms = body.target_platforms.split(",").map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
      }

      let path = ep.path;
      Object.entries(pathVals).forEach(([k, v]) => {
        path = path.replace(`{${k}}`, encodeURIComponent(String(v)));
      });
      const qs = new URLSearchParams(query).toString();
      if (qs) path += (path.includes("?") ? "&" : "?") + qs;
      return { path, body };
    }

    async function api(method, path, body) {
      const headers = { "Content-Type": "application/json" };
      if (state.token) headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${state.token}`;
      const res = await fetch(path, {
        method,
        headers,
        body: ["POST", "PUT", "PATCH"].includes(method) ? JSON.stringify(body ?? {}) : undefined,
      });
      const text = await res.text();
      let data;
      try { data = JSON.parse(text); } catch { data = text; }
      return { ok: res.ok, status: res.status, data };
    }

    function showOut(ok, title, data) {
      const meta = $("outMeta");
      meta.className = "meta " + (ok ? "ok" : "bad");
      meta.textContent = title;
      $("output").textContent = typeof data === "string" ? data : JSON.stringify(data, null, 2);
    }

    async function runEndpoint(ep) {
      try {
        if (!state.token && ep.path !== "/auth/login") {
          showOut(false, "Login first", { detail: "Choose a user and click Login" });
          return;
        }
        const { path, body } = readFields(ep);
        const needsBody = ["POST", "PUT", "PATCH"].includes(ep.method);
        const result = await api(ep.method, path, needsBody ? body : undefined);
        showOut(result.ok, `${result.status} ${ep.method} ${path}`, result.data);
      } catch (err) {
        showOut(false, "Request error", { detail: String(err.message || err) });
      }
    }

    $("username").addEventListener("change", () => {
      $("password").value = PASSWORDS[$("username").value] || "";
    });

    $("loginBtn").addEventListener("click", async () => {
      const username = $("username").value;
      const password = $("password").value;
      const result = await api("POST", "/auth/login", { username, password });
      if (!result.ok) {
        showOut(false, `Login failed (${result.status})`, result.data);
        return;
      }
      state.token = result.data.session_token;
      state.user = result.data.username;
      state.role = result.data.role_code;
      localStorage.setItem("nm_token", state.token);
      localStorage.setItem("nm_user", state.user);
      localStorage.setItem("nm_role", state.role);
      $("session").innerHTML = `Logged in as <strong>${state.user}</strong> (${state.role})`;
      showOut(true, "Login OK", result.data);
    });

    $("logoutBtn").addEventListener("click", async () => {
      if (state.token) {
        try { await api("POST", "/auth/logout"); } catch (_) {}
      }
      state.token = state.user = state.role = "";
      localStorage.removeItem("nm_token");
      localStorage.removeItem("nm_user");
      localStorage.removeItem("nm_role");
      $("session").textContent = "Not logged in";
      showOut(true, "Logged out", { message: "Session cleared" });
    });

    $("password").value = PASSWORDS[$("username").value];
    if (state.token) {
      $("session").innerHTML = `Logged in as <strong>${state.user || "?"}</strong> (${state.role || "?"})`;
    }
    renderNav();
    renderForm();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

FILE: app.py

"""
NewsMind console application.

Feature modules: authentication, admin, content
Services: pipeline at services/ root; content/ + user/ grouped
Cleaning: cleaners/ (+ stages/) | Repositories: content + user
Menus are role-based after login.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from getpass import getpass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable, Optional

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from config.constants import (
    ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES,
    ALLOWED_PITCH_STATUSES,
    CAP_ADMIN,
    CAP_CLEAN,
    CAP_INGEST,
    CAP_PERSIST,
    CAP_PIPELINE,
    CAP_PITCH_REVIEW,

    ROLE_ADAPTATION_EDITOR,
    ROLE_AUDIENCE_ANALYST,
    ROLE_EDITOR,
    ROLE_JOURNALIST,
    ROLE_NEWSROOM_MANAGER,
    ROLE_PUBLISHER,
)
from config.env_loader import load_dotenv
from database.connection import check_connection, init_schema

    AuthenticationError,
    authenticate,
    change_password,
    logout,
    resolve_session,
)
from modules.content import ContentModule
from services.permission_service import has_capability

    UserCancelled,

    prompt_nonempty,

load_dotenv()

log = get_logger("app")
admin = AdminModule()
content = ContentModule()
users = UserService()

Action = Callable[[], int]


def _current_user() -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
    try:
        return resolve_session()
    except AuthenticationError:
        return None


def _handle(exc: Exception) -> int:
    if isinstance(exc, UserCancelled):
        raise exc
    if isinstance(exc, AppError):
        error(exc.message)
        return 1
    if isinstance(exc, AuthenticationError):
        error(str(exc))
        return 1
    # Database / driver errors — show the useful message, not a raw stack only
    try:
        from psycopg2 import Error as PgError
    except ImportError:  # pragma: no cover
        PgError = ()  # type: ignore[misc, assignment]
    if PgError and isinstance(exc, PgError):
        log.exception("Database error")
        error(f"Database error: {exc}")
        info("Tip: run menu DB status / python app.py --init-db if schema is outdated.")
        return 1
    if isinstance(exc, (FileNotFoundError, RuntimeError, ValueError)):
        error(str(exc))
        return 1
    log.exception("Unhandled error")
    error(f"Unexpected failure: {exc}")
    return 1


def _run_admin(fn, *, title: str) -> int:
    try:
        result = fn()
    except UserCancelled:
        raise
    except (AuthError, PermissionDeniedError, AppError) as exc:
        return _handle(exc)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)

    header(title)
    kv("Actor", result.get("actor"))
    kv("Role", result.get("role"))
    if result.get("message"):
        info(str(result["message"]))
    if "all_files" in result:
        kv("CSV Files", len(result.get("all_files") or []))
        for name in result.get("all_files") or []:
            mark = " [new/changed]" if name in (result.get("new_or_changed") or []) else ""
            print(f"    - {name}{mark}")
        kv("New Or Changed", len(result.get("new_or_changed") or []))
    if "content_rows" in result:
        kv("Content Rows", result["content_rows"])
        kv("Pitch Rows", result.get("pitch_rows"))
        files = result.get("source_files") or []
        kv("Source Files", len(files))
        for name in files[:20]:
            print(f"    - {name}")
        if len(files) > 20:
            print(f"    ... and {len(files) - 20} more")
        collections = result.get("collections") or {}
        if collections:
            kv("Categories", ", ".join(collections.get("categories") or []) or "none")
            kv("Unique Titles", collections.get("unique_titles"))
        kv("Raw Rows Stored", len(result.get("raw_ingest_ids") or []))
    if "ingest" in result:
        ingest = result["ingest"]
        kv("Ingested Content", ingest.get("content_rows"))
        kv("Ingested Pitches", ingest.get("pitch_rows"))
        kv("Raw Stored", ingest.get("raw_stored"))
        files = ingest.get("source_files") or []
        kv("Source Files", len(files))
        for name in files[:20]:
            print(f"    - {name}")
    if "content_stats" in result:
        stats = result["content_stats"]
        kv("Clean Kept", stats.get("kept"))
        kv("Clean Rejected", stats.get("rejected"))
        kv("Quality Report File", result.get("content_report") or result.get("quality_report"))
        kv("Cleaning Report ID", result.get("cleaning_report_id"))
        kv("Cleaned CSV", result.get("cleaned_csv"))
    if "persisted_content" in result:
        kv("Persisted Content", result.get("persisted_content"))
        kv("Persisted Pitches", result.get("persisted_pitches"))
        kv("Rejected Raw Stored", result.get("rejected_raw_stored"))
    success(f"{title} completed.")
    blank()
    return 0


# ── Auth ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def do_login(username: str = "", password: str = "") -> int:
    try:
        username = username or prompt_nonempty("Username")
        password = password or getpass("Password: ")
        if not password:
            raise ValidationError("Password cannot be empty.")
        payload = authenticate(username, password)
    except (AuthenticationError, ValidationError, AppError) as exc:
        return _handle(exc)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    success("Login successful.")
    show_user(payload)
    return 0


def do_logout() -> int:
    logout()
    success("Logged out.")
    blank()
    return 0


def do_change_password() -> int:
    header("Change Password")
    try:
        user = resolve_session()
        current = getpass("Current password: ")
        new_password = getpass("New password: ")
        confirm = getpass("Confirm new password: ")
        if new_password != confirm:
            raise ValidationError("New password and confirmation do not match.")
        change_password(int(user["user_id"]), current, new_password)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    success("Password changed. Use the new password next login.")
    blank()
    return 0


def do_admin_reset_password() -> int:
    header("Reset User Password (Admin)")
    try:
        username = prompt_nonempty("Username to reset")
        new_password = getpass("New password: ")
        confirm = getpass("Confirm new password: ")
        if new_password != confirm:
            raise ValidationError("New password and confirmation do not match.")
        result = users.reset_password(username, new_password)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    success("Password reset for user.")
    kv("Username", result["username"])
    kv("User ID", result["user_id"])
    blank()
    return 0


# ── Admin / pipeline ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def do_list_csv_files() -> int:
    return _run_admin(admin.list_csv_files, title="CSV Files In data/raw")


def do_ingest() -> int:
    return _run_admin(lambda: admin.ingest(only_new=False), title="Ingest All CSV Files")


def do_ingest_new() -> int:
    return _run_admin(lambda: admin.ingest(only_new=True), title="Ingest New/Changed CSV Files")


def do_ingest_one() -> int:
    header("Ingest One CSV File")
    try:
        listing = admin.list_csv_files()
        files = listing.get("all_files") or []
        if not files:
            raise ValidationError("No CSV files found under data/raw.")
        info(f"{len(files)} CSV file(s) available.")
        for idx, name in enumerate(files, start=1):
            mark = " [new/changed]" if name in (listing.get("new_or_changed") or []) else ""
            print(f"  {idx}) {name}{mark}")
        choice = prompt_nonempty("File number or relative path")
        if choice.isdigit():
            n = int(choice)
            if n < 1 or n > len(files):
                raise ValidationError("File number out of range.")
            source = files[n - 1]
        else:
            source = choice
        return _run_admin(lambda: admin.ingest_file(source), title=f"Ingest File — {source}")
    except (AuthError, PermissionDeniedError, AppError, UserCancelled) as exc:
        if isinstance(exc, UserCancelled):
            raise
        return _handle(exc)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)


def do_clean() -> int:
    return _run_admin(admin.clean, title="Clean Data")


def do_ingest_and_clean() -> int:
    return _run_admin(lambda: admin.ingest_and_clean(only_new=False), title="Ingest + Clean (All)")


def do_ingest_and_clean_new() -> int:
    return _run_admin(lambda: admin.ingest_and_clean(only_new=True), title="Ingest + Clean (New Only)")


def do_persist() -> int:
    return _run_admin(admin.persist, title="Persist Cleaned Data")


def do_pipeline() -> int:
    return _run_admin(lambda: admin.run_pipeline(only_new=False), title="Full Pipeline (All Files)")


def do_pipeline_new() -> int:
    return _run_admin(lambda: admin.run_pipeline(only_new=True), title="Full Pipeline (New Files Only)")


def do_pipeline_one() -> int:
    header("Pipeline One CSV File")
    try:
        listing = admin.list_csv_files()
        files = listing.get("all_files") or []
        if not files:
            raise ValidationError("No CSV files found under data/raw.")
        for idx, name in enumerate(files, start=1):
            mark = " [new/changed]" if name in (listing.get("new_or_changed") or []) else ""
            print(f"  {idx}) {name}{mark}")
        choice = prompt_nonempty("File number or relative path")
        if choice.isdigit():
            n = int(choice)
            if n < 1 or n > len(files):
                raise ValidationError("File number out of range.")
            source = files[n - 1]
        else:
            source = choice
        return _run_admin(lambda: admin.pipeline_file(source), title=f"Pipeline File — {source}")
    except (AuthError, PermissionDeniedError, AppError, UserCancelled) as exc:
        if isinstance(exc, UserCancelled):
            raise
        return _handle(exc)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)


def do_list_reports() -> int:
    try:
        payload = admin.list_reports(limit=20)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    show_dict_rows(
        "Cleaning Reports (DB)",
        payload.get("reports") or [],
        [
            ("Report ID", "cleaning_report_id"),
            ("Type", "report_type"),
            ("Input", "input_count"),
            ("Kept", "kept_count"),
            ("Rejected", "rejected_count"),
            ("Sources", "source_files"),
            ("Created", "created_at"),
        ],
    )
    return 0


def do_view_report() -> int:
    header("View Cleaning Report")
    try:
        report_id = int(prompt_nonempty("Cleaning report ID"))
        payload = admin.view_report(report_id)
        row = payload["report"]
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    kv("Report ID", row.get("cleaning_report_id"))
    kv("Type", row.get("report_type"))
    kv("Source Files", row.get("source_files"))
    kv("Input", row.get("input_count"))
    kv("Kept", row.get("kept_count"))
    kv("Rejected", row.get("rejected_count"))
    kv("Created", row.get("created_at"))
    report_json = row.get("report_json")
    if report_json is not None:
        if isinstance(report_json, str):
            try:
                report_json = json.loads(report_json)
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                pass
        print("  Report detail:")
        print(json.dumps(report_json, indent=2, default=str)[:4000])
    blank()
    return 0


def do_status() -> int:
    ok = check_connection()
    header("Database Status")
    kv("Connected", "Yes" if ok else "No")
    blank()
    return 0 if ok else 1


def _require_admin_ml() -> dict[str, Any]:
    user = _current_user()
    if user is None:
        raise AuthError("Login required.")
    role = user["role_code"]
    caps = set(user.get("capabilities") or [])
    if role != ROLE_ADMIN and CAP_ADMIN not in caps:
        raise PermissionDeniedError("Admin capability required for ML training.")
    return user



def do_push_urgency_datasets() -> int:
    try:
        _require_admin_ml()
        from ml.training import push_urgency
        from ml.training.ingest_clean import list_dataset_csvs

        files = list_dataset_csvs("urgency")
        header("Push Urgency Datasets (ingest + clean)")
        if not files:
            info("No CSV files found under ml/editor/dataset/")
            blank()
            return 1
        info(f"Auto-detected {len(files)} file(s): " + ", ".join(files))
        n = push_urgency()  # all detected; skips unchanged hashes
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    kv("Rows", n)
    success("Urgency push finished.")
    blank()
    return 0


def do_push_adaptation_datasets() -> int:
    try:
        _require_admin_ml()
        from ml.training import push_adaptation
        from ml.training.ingest_clean import list_dataset_csvs

        files = list_dataset_csvs("adaptation")
        header("Push CAE Datasets (ingest + clean)")
        if not files:
            info("No CSV files found under ml/adaptation/dataset/")
            blank()
            return 1
        info(f"Auto-detected {len(files)} file(s): " + ", ".join(files))
        n = push_adaptation()  # all detected; skips unchanged hashes
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    kv("Rows", n)
    success("CAE push finished.")
    blank()
    return 0


def do_train_urgency() -> int:
    try:
        _require_admin_ml()
        from ml.training import train_urgency_from_db

        # Fast mode for console: full CV search can look "stuck" for many minutes
        info("Training urgency (fast mode). Watch step logs 1/6 … 6/6.")
        metrics = train_urgency_from_db(fast=True)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    header("Train Urgency Model")
    kv("Accuracy", metrics.get("accuracy"))
    kv("Macro F1", metrics.get("macro_f1"))
    kv("Train rows", metrics.get("n_train"))
    kv("Test rows", metrics.get("n_test"))
    success("Urgency training completed.")
    blank()
    return 0


def do_train_cae() -> int:
    try:
        _require_admin_ml()
        from ml.training import train_cae_from_db

        results = train_cae_from_db()
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    header("Train Unified CAE Model")
    block = results.get("content_adaptation") or {}
    test = (block.get("metrics") or {}).get("test") or {}
    kv("Overall F1", test.get("f1"))
    kv("Overall accuracy", test.get("accuracy"))
    for platform, stats in (block.get("per_platform") or {}).items():
        f1 = stats.get("f1")
        acc = stats.get("accuracy")
        kv(
            platform,
            f"F1={f1:.4f} acc={acc:.4f}" if f1 is not None and acc is not None else "see console output",
        )
    success("Unified CAE training completed.")
    blank()
    return 0


def do_train_all_models() -> int:
    try:
        _require_admin_ml()
        from ml.training import train_all

        info("Training all models (urgency uses fast mode). Step logs will print below.")
        summary = train_all(fast=True)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    header("Train All Models (urgency + CAE + KB)")
    urg = summary.get("editor_queue_urgency") or {}
    kv("Urgency accuracy", urg.get("accuracy"))
    kv("Urgency macro F1", urg.get("macro_f1"))
    cae = summary.get("cae_adaptation") or {}
    for platform, stats in cae.items():
        f1 = stats.get("test_f1")
        kv(platform, f"test F1={f1:.4f}" if f1 is not None else "see console output")
    success("All model training completed.")
    blank()
    return 0


def do_init_db() -> int:
    try:
        init_schema()
        ok = check_connection()
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    header("Initialize Database")
    kv("Schema", "Applied")
    kv("Connected", "Yes" if ok else "No")
    success("Database ready.")
    blank()
    return 0 if ok else 1

def do_add_user() -> int:
    header("Add User")
    try:
        result = users.add_user(
            username=prompt_nonempty("Username"),
            password=getpass("Password: "),
            first_name=prompt_nonempty("First name"),
            last_name=prompt_nonempty("Last name"),
            email=prompt_nonempty("Email"),
            role_code=prompt_optional("Role code", "journalist"),
        )
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    success("User created.")
    kv("User ID", result["user_id"])
    kv("Username", result["username"])
    kv("Role", result["role_code"])
    blank()
    return 0


def do_add_category() -> int:
    header("Add Category")
    try:
        cid = content.add_category(
            prompt_nonempty("Category name"),
            prompt_optional("Description"),
        )
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    success("Category created.")
    kv("Category ID", cid)
    blank()
    return 0


def do_add_pitch() -> int:
    header("Add Story Pitch")
    try:
        status = prompt_choice("Status", ALLOWED_PITCH_STATUSES) or "submitted"
        status = require_pitch_status(status)
        pid = content.add_pitch(
            prompt_nonempty("Title"),
            prompt_nonempty("Summary"),
            status=status,
        )
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    success("Story pitch created.")
    kv("Story Pitch ID", pid)
    blank()
    return 0


def do_add_news() -> int:
    header("Add News")
    try:
        status = prompt_choice("Status", ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES) or "draft"
        evidence: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        info("At least one evidence item is required.")
        while True:
            evidence.append(
                {
                    "title": prompt_nonempty("Evidence title"),
                    "evidence_type": prompt_optional("Evidence type", "document") or "document",
                    "source_url": prompt_optional("Evidence source URL"),
                    "text": prompt_optional("Evidence text (optional)"),
                }
            )
            more = prompt_optional("Add another evidence? (y/N)", "n").lower()
            if more != "y":
                break
        result = content.add_news(
            title=prompt_nonempty("Title"),
            body=prompt_nonempty("Body"),
            summary=prompt_optional("Summary"),
            event_location=prompt_optional("Event location"),
            severity=prompt_optional("Severity", "low"),
            breaking=prompt_optional("Breaking news? (y/N)", "n").lower() == "y",
            status=status,
            evidence=evidence,
        )
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    success("News created.")
    kv("Content ID", result["content_id"])
    kv("News ID", result["news_id"])
    kv("Evidence IDs", ", ".join(str(i) for i in result.get("evidence_ids") or []))
    blank()
    return 0


def do_add_article() -> int:
    header("Add Article")
    try:
        status = prompt_choice("Status", ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES) or "draft"
        evidence: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        info("At least one evidence item is required.")
        while True:
            evidence.append(
                {
                    "title": prompt_nonempty("Evidence title"),
                    "evidence_type": prompt_optional("Evidence type", "document") or "document",
                    "source_url": prompt_optional("Evidence source URL"),
                    "text": prompt_optional("Evidence text (optional)"),
                }
            )
            more = prompt_optional("Add another evidence? (y/N)", "n").lower()
            if more not in {"y", "yes"}:
                break
        result = content.add_article(
            title=prompt_nonempty("Title"),
            body=prompt_nonempty("Body"),
            summary=prompt_optional("Summary"),
            status=status,
            evidence=evidence,
        )
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return _handle(exc)
    success("Article created.")
    kv("Content ID", result["content_id"])
    kv("Article ID", result["article_id"])
    kv("Evidence IDs", result.get("evidence_ids"))
    blank()
    return 0

        row = content.get_content(prompt_nonempty("Content ID"))
