"""NewsroomManager DAO — table newsroom_manager."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional

from dao.base_dao import BaseDAO
from database.connection import db_cursor
from entities.newsroom_manager import NewsroomManager
from utils.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError
from utils.validators import require_desk_status, require_optional_str, require_positive_id


class NewsroomManagerDAO(BaseDAO):
    table = "newsroom_manager"

    def create(self, manager: NewsroomManager) -> int:
        user_id = require_positive_id(manager.user_id, "User ID")
        status = require_desk_status(manager.status or "active")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO newsroom_manager (user_id, managed_region, status)
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s)
                ON CONFLICT (user_id) DO UPDATE
                SET managed_region = EXCLUDED.managed_region, status = EXCLUDED.status
                RETURNING newsroom_manager_id
                """,
                (
                    user_id,
                    require_optional_str(manager.managed_region, "Managed region", max_len=128),
                    status,
                ),
            )
            return int(cur.fetchone()["newsroom_manager_id"])

    def ensure_for_user(self, user_id: int) -> int:
        return self.create(NewsroomManager(user_id=user_id, managed_region="HQ"))

    def read(self, newsroom_manager_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.get_by_id(
            "newsroom_manager_id",
            require_positive_id(newsroom_manager_id, "Newsroom manager ID"),
        )

    def require(self, newsroom_manager_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = self.read(newsroom_manager_id)
        if not row:
            raise NotFoundError(f"Newsroom manager ID {newsroom_manager_id} was not found.")
        return row

    def read_by_user_id(self, user_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        user_id = require_positive_id(user_id, "User ID")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute("SELECT * FROM newsroom_manager WHERE user_id = %s", (user_id,))
            row = cur.fetchone()
            return dict(row) if row else None

    def update(self, newsroom_manager_id: int, **fields: Any) -> int:
        self.require(newsroom_manager_id)
        allowed = {"managed_region", "status"}
        cols = {k: v for k, v in fields.items() if k in allowed}
        if "status" in cols:
            cols["status"] = require_desk_status(cols["status"])
        if not cols:
            raise ValidationError("No valid fields to update.")
        sets = ", ".join(f"{k} = %s" for k in cols)
        return int(
            self.execute(
                f"UPDATE newsroom_manager SET {sets}, updated_at = NOW() WHERE newsroom_manager_id = %s",
                (*cols.values(), newsroom_manager_id),
            )
        )

    def delete(self, newsroom_manager_id: int) -> int:
        self.require(newsroom_manager_id)
        return self.delete_by_id("newsroom_manager_id", newsroom_manager_id)

FILE: dao/publication_dao.py
========================================================================================
"""Publisher publication queue — table publication."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional

from dao.base_dao import BaseDAO
from database.connection import db_cursor
from utils.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError
from utils.validators import require_optional_str, require_positive_id

ALLOWED_PUBLICATION_STATUSES = ("ready", "approved", "scheduled", "published")


class PublicationDAO(BaseDAO):
    table = "publication"

    def ensure_for_content(self, content_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        content_id = require_positive_id(content_id, "Content ID")
        existing = self.read_by_content_id(content_id)
        if existing:
            return existing
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO publication (content_id, status)
                VALUES (%s, 'ready')
                ON CONFLICT (content_id) DO UPDATE
                SET updated_at = NOW()
                RETURNING *
                """,
                (content_id,),
            )
            return dict(cur.fetchone())

    def read(self, publication_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.get_by_id(
            "publication_id",
            require_positive_id(publication_id, "Publication ID"),
        )

    def require(self, publication_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = self.read(publication_id)
        if not row:
            raise NotFoundError(f"Publication ID {publication_id} was not found.")
        return row

    def read_by_content_id(self, content_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        content_id = require_positive_id(content_id, "Content ID")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute("SELECT * FROM publication WHERE content_id = %s", (content_id,))
            row = cur.fetchone()
            return dict(row) if row else None

    def list_ready_articles(self, limit: int = 100) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT
                    c.content_id,
                    c.title,
                    c.summary,
                    c.content_body,
                    c.content_kind,
                    c.status AS content_status,
                    p.publication_id,
                    p.template_code,
                    p.status AS publication_status,
                    p.scheduled_at,
                    p.published_at
                FROM content c
                LEFT JOIN publication p ON p.content_id = c.content_id
                WHERE c.status = 'approved'
                  AND (p.publication_id IS NULL OR p.status IN ('ready', 'approved', 'scheduled'))
                ORDER BY c.content_id DESC
                LIMIT %s
                """,
                (max(1, min(int(limit), 500)),),
            )
            return [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall() or []]

    def update_fields(self, publication_id: int, **fields: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
        self.require(publication_id)
        allowed = {"template_code", "status", "scheduled_at", "published_at"}
        cols = {k: v for k, v in fields.items() if k in allowed}
        if "status" in cols:
            status = str(cols["status"]).strip().lower()
            if status not in ALLOWED_PUBLICATION_STATUSES:
                raise ValidationError(
                    f"Invalid publication status. Allowed: {', '.join(ALLOWED_PUBLICATION_STATUSES)}"
                )
            cols["status"] = status
        if "template_code" in cols and cols["template_code"] is not None:
            cols["template_code"] = require_optional_str(
                cols["template_code"], "Template code", max_len=64
            )
        if not cols:
            raise ValidationError("No valid fields to update.")
        sets = ", ".join(f"{k} = %s" for k in cols)
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                f"""
                UPDATE publication
                SET {sets}, updated_at = NOW()
                WHERE publication_id = %s
                RETURNING *
                """,
                (*cols.values(), publication_id),
            )
            return dict(cur.fetchone())

    def analytics(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT status, COUNT(*) AS count
                FROM publication
                GROUP BY status
                """
            )
            by_status = {str(r["status"]): int(r["count"]) for r in cur.fetchall() or []}
            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT COUNT(*) AS count
                FROM content
                WHERE status = 'approved'
                """
            )
            approved_content = int(cur.fetchone()["count"])
        return {
            "approved_content": approved_content,
            "publications_by_status": by_status,
            "published": by_status.get("published", 0),
            "scheduled": by_status.get("scheduled", 0),
            "ready": by_status.get("ready", 0),
            "approved": by_status.get("approved", 0),
        }

"""Publisher DAO — table publisher."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional

from dao.base_dao import BaseDAO
from database.connection import db_cursor
from entities.publisher import Publisher
from utils.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError
from utils.validators import require_desk_status, require_optional_str, require_positive_id


class PublisherDAO(BaseDAO):
    table = "publisher"

    def create(self, publisher: Publisher) -> int:
        user_id = require_positive_id(publisher.user_id, "User ID")
        status = require_desk_status(publisher.status or "active")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO publisher (user_id, publishing_group, status)
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s)
                ON CONFLICT (user_id) DO UPDATE
                SET publishing_group = EXCLUDED.publishing_group, status = EXCLUDED.status
                RETURNING publisher_id
                """,
                (
                    user_id,
                    require_optional_str(publisher.publishing_group, "Publishing group", max_len=128),
                    status,
                ),
            )
            return int(cur.fetchone()["publisher_id"])

    def ensure_for_user(self, user_id: int) -> int:
        return self.create(Publisher(user_id=user_id, publishing_group="Primary"))

    def read(self, publisher_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.get_by_id("publisher_id", require_positive_id(publisher_id, "Publisher ID"))

    def require(self, publisher_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = self.read(publisher_id)
        if not row:
            raise NotFoundError(f"Publisher ID {publisher_id} was not found.")
        return row

    def read_by_user_id(self, user_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        user_id = require_positive_id(user_id, "User ID")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute("SELECT * FROM publisher WHERE user_id = %s", (user_id,))
            row = cur.fetchone()
            return dict(row) if row else None

    def update(self, publisher_id: int, **fields: Any) -> int:
        self.require(publisher_id)
        allowed = {"publishing_group", "status"}
        cols = {k: v for k, v in fields.items() if k in allowed}
        if "status" in cols:
            cols["status"] = require_desk_status(cols["status"])
        if not cols:
            raise ValidationError("No valid fields to update.")
        sets = ", ".join(f"{k} = %s" for k in cols)
        return int(
            self.execute(
                f"UPDATE publisher SET {sets}, updated_at = NOW() WHERE publisher_id = %s",
                (*cols.values(), publisher_id),
            )
        )

    def delete(self, publisher_id: int) -> int:
        self.require(publisher_id)
        return self.delete_by_id("publisher_id", publisher_id)

FILE: dao/raw_ingest_dao.py

"""Raw ingest DAO — table raw_ingest (CSV rows before/after cleaning)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional

from dao.base_dao import BaseDAO
from database.connection import db_cursor


class RawIngestDAO(BaseDAO):
    table = "raw_ingest"

    def create(
        self,
        *,
        source_file: str,
        title: str = "",
        raw_text: str = "",
        category: str = "",
        published_date: str = "",
        source_type: str = "",
        cleaning_status: str = "pending",
        reject_reason: str | None = None,
        content_id: int | None = None,
    ) -> int:
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO raw_ingest (
                    source_file, title, raw_text, category, published_date,
                    source_type, cleaning_status, reject_reason, content_id
                )
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                RETURNING raw_ingest_id
                """,
                (
                    source_file,
                    title or None,
                    raw_text or None,
                    category or None,
                    published_date or None,
                    source_type or None,
                    cleaning_status,
                    reject_reason,
                    content_id,
                ),
            )
            return int(cur.fetchone()["raw_ingest_id"])

    def create_many(self, rows: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[int]:
        """Bulk insert in batches (one connection per batch)."""
        from config.runtime import db_insert_batch
        from utils.progress import progress

        if not rows:
            return []
        batch_size = db_insert_batch()
        ids: list[int] = []
        total_batches = (len(rows) + batch_size - 1) // batch_size
        sql = """
            INSERT INTO raw_ingest (
                source_file, title, raw_text, category, published_date,
                source_type, cleaning_status, reject_reason, content_id
            )
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
            RETURNING raw_ingest_id
            """
        for batch_no, start in enumerate(range(0, len(rows), batch_size), start=1):
            batch = rows[start : start + batch_size]
            progress(f"DB insert batch {batch_no}/{total_batches} ({len(batch)} row(s))...")
            with db_cursor() as cur:
                for row in batch:
                    cur.execute(
                        sql,
                        (
                            str(row.get("source_file") or row.get("source_type") or "csv"),
                            str(row.get("title") or "") or None,
                            str(row.get("raw_text") or row.get("text") or row.get("_raw_text") or "") or None,
                            str(row.get("category") or "") or None,
                            str(row.get("date") or row.get("published_date") or "") or None,
                            str(row.get("source_type") or "") or None,
                            str(row.get("cleaning_status") or "pending"),
                            row.get("reject_reason") or row.get("_reject_reason"),
                            row.get("content_id"),
                        ),
                    )
                    ids.append(int(cur.fetchone()["raw_ingest_id"]))
        return ids

    def read(self, raw_ingest_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.get_by_id("raw_ingest_id", raw_ingest_id)

    def update_status(
        self,
        raw_ingest_id: int,
        cleaning_status: str,
        *,
        reject_reason: str | None = None,
        content_id: int | None = None,
    ) -> int:
        return int(
            self.execute(
                """
                UPDATE raw_ingest
                SET cleaning_status = %s,
                    reject_reason = COALESCE(%s, reject_reason),
                    content_id = COALESCE(%s, content_id)
                WHERE raw_ingest_id = %s
                """,
                (cleaning_status, reject_reason, content_id, raw_ingest_id),
            )
        )

    def list_recent(self, limit: int = 20) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        rows = self.execute(
            """
            SELECT raw_ingest_id, source_file, title, category, cleaning_status,
                   reject_reason, content_id, ingested_at
            FROM raw_ingest
            ORDER BY raw_ingest_id DESC
            LIMIT %s
            """,
            (limit,),
            fetch="all",
        )
        return [dict(r) for r in rows or []]

    def count_by_status(self) -> dict[str, int]:
        rows = self.execute(
            """
            SELECT cleaning_status, COUNT(*) AS cnt
            FROM raw_ingest
            GROUP BY cleaning_status
            """,
            fetch="all",
        )
        return {str(r["cleaning_status"]): int(r["cnt"]) for r in rows or []}

"""Role DAO — table role."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional

from dao.base_dao import BaseDAO
from database.connection import db_cursor
from entities.role import Role
from utils.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError
from utils.validators import require_nonempty, require_optional_str, require_positive_id, require_role_code


class RoleDAO(BaseDAO):
    table = "role"

    def create(self, role: Role) -> int:
        code = require_role_code(role.role_code)
        name = require_nonempty(role.role_name, "Role name")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO role (role_code, role_name, role_description, is_active)
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s)
                ON CONFLICT (role_code) DO UPDATE
                SET role_name = EXCLUDED.role_name,
                    role_description = EXCLUDED.role_description,
                    is_active = EXCLUDED.is_active
                RETURNING role_id
                """,
                (
                    code,
                    name,
                    require_optional_str(role.role_description, "Role description"),
                    bool(role.is_active),
                ),
            )
            return int(cur.fetchone()["role_id"])

    def get_id_by_code(self, role_code: str) -> Optional[int]:
        code = require_role_code(role_code)
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute("SELECT role_id FROM role WHERE role_code = %s", (code,))
            row = cur.fetchone()
            return int(row["role_id"]) if row else None

    def read(self, role_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.get_by_id("role_id", require_positive_id(role_id, "Role ID"))

    def require(self, role_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = self.read(role_id)
        if not row:
            raise NotFoundError(f"Role ID {role_id} was not found.")
        return row

    def update(self, role_id: int, **fields: Any) -> int:
        self.require(role_id)
        allowed = {"role_name", "role_description", "is_active"}
        cols = {k: v for k, v in fields.items() if k in allowed}
        if "role_name" in cols:
            cols["role_name"] = require_nonempty(cols["role_name"], "Role name")
        if not cols:
            raise ValidationError("No valid fields to update.")
        sets = ", ".join(f"{k} = %s" for k in cols)
        return int(
            self.execute(
                f"UPDATE role SET {sets}, updated_at = NOW() WHERE role_id = %s",
                (*cols.values(), role_id),
            )
        )

    def delete(self, role_id: int) -> int:
        self.require(role_id)
        return self.delete_by_id("role_id", role_id)

"""StoryPitch DAO — table story_pitch."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional

from dao.base_dao import BaseDAO
from database.connection import db_cursor
from entities.content.story_pitch import StoryPitch
from utils.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError
from utils.validators import (
    require_nonempty,
    require_optional_positive_id,
    require_optional_str,
    require_pitch_status,
    require_positive_id,
    require_priority,
    require_score,
    require_title,
)


class StoryPitchDAO(BaseDAO):
    table = "story_pitch"

    def create(self, pitch: StoryPitch) -> int:
        title = require_title(pitch.title)
        summary = require_nonempty(pitch.summary, "Summary")
        status = require_pitch_status(pitch.status or "submitted")
        priority = require_priority(pitch.priority or "medium")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO story_pitch (
                    audience_insight_id, title, summary, priority, source_type,
                    trend_score, audience_interest_score, coverage_gap_score,
                    status, created_by, approved_by, submitted_at, approved_at
                )
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                RETURNING story_pitch_id
                """,
                (
                    require_optional_positive_id(pitch.audience_insight_id, "Audience insight ID"),
                    title,
                    summary,
                    priority,
                    require_optional_str(pitch.source_type, "Source type", max_len=64),
                    require_score(pitch.trend_score, "Trend score"),
                    require_score(pitch.audience_interest_score, "Audience interest score"),
                    require_score(pitch.coverage_gap_score, "Coverage gap score"),
                    status,
                    require_optional_positive_id(pitch.created_by, "Created by"),
                    require_optional_positive_id(pitch.approved_by, "Approved by"),
                    pitch.submitted_at,
                    pitch.approved_at,
                ),
            )
            return int(cur.fetchone()["story_pitch_id"])

    def read(self, story_pitch_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.get_by_id("story_pitch_id", require_positive_id(story_pitch_id, "Story pitch ID"))

    def require(self, story_pitch_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = self.read(story_pitch_id)
        if not row:
            raise NotFoundError(f"Story pitch ID {story_pitch_id} was not found.")
        return row

    def update(self, story_pitch_id: int, **fields: Any) -> int:
        self.require(story_pitch_id)
        allowed = {
            "title",
            "summary",
            "priority",
            "source_type",
            "trend_score",
            "audience_interest_score",
            "coverage_gap_score",
            "status",
            "approved_by",
            "approved_at",
        }
        cols = {k: v for k, v in fields.items() if k in allowed}
        if "title" in cols:
            cols["title"] = require_title(cols["title"])
        if "summary" in cols:
            cols["summary"] = require_nonempty(cols["summary"], "Summary")
        if "status" in cols:
            cols["status"] = require_pitch_status(str(cols["status"]))
        if "priority" in cols:
            cols["priority"] = require_priority(cols["priority"])
        if not cols:
            raise ValidationError("No valid fields to update.")
        sets = ", ".join(f"{k} = %s" for k in cols)
        return int(
            self.execute(
                f"UPDATE story_pitch SET {sets}, updated_at = NOW() WHERE story_pitch_id = %s",
                (*cols.values(), story_pitch_id),
            )
        )

    def update_status(self, story_pitch_id: int, status: str) -> int:
        return self.update(story_pitch_id, status=require_pitch_status(status))

    def list_by_status(self, status: str, limit: int = 100) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """List pitches for a status, urgent/high first (Editor queue order)."""
        status = require_pitch_status(status)
        rows = self.execute(
            """
            SELECT * FROM story_pitch
            WHERE status = %s
            ORDER BY
                CASE lower(coalesce(priority, 'medium'))
                    WHEN 'urgent' THEN 0
                    WHEN 'high' THEN 1
                    WHEN 'medium' THEN 2
                    WHEN 'low' THEN 3
                    ELSE 4
                END,
                story_pitch_id DESC
            LIMIT %s
            """,
            (status, max(1, min(int(limit), 500))),
            fetch="all",
        )
        return [dict(r) for r in rows or []]

    def delete(self, story_pitch_id: int) -> int:
        self.require(story_pitch_id)
        return self.delete_by_id("story_pitch_id", story_pitch_id)

FILE: dao/user_dao.py

"""User DAO — ERD USER / physical table users."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional

from dao.base_dao import BaseDAO
from database.connection import db_cursor
from entities.user import User
from utils.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError
from utils.validators import (
    require_account_status,
    require_nonempty,
    require_positive_id,
    require_username,
)


class UserDAO(BaseDAO):
    table = "users"

    def create(self, user: User) -> int:
        employee_id = require_positive_id(user.employee_id, "Employee ID")
        role_id = require_positive_id(user.role_id, "Role ID")
        username = require_username(user.username)
        password_hash = require_nonempty(user.password_hash, "Password hash")
        status = require_account_status(user.account_status or "active")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO users (
                    employee_id, role_id, username, password_hash, account_status
                )
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                ON CONFLICT (username) DO UPDATE
                SET password_hash = EXCLUDED.password_hash,
                    account_status = EXCLUDED.account_status,
                    role_id = EXCLUDED.role_id,
                    updated_at = NOW()
                RETURNING user_id
                """,
                (employee_id, role_id, username, password_hash, status),
            )
            return int(cur.fetchone()["user_id"])

    def read(self, user_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.read_by_id(user_id)

    def read_by_id(self, user_id: int) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        user_id = require_positive_id(user_id, "User ID")
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT u.*, e.email, e.first_name, e.last_name, r.role_code, r.role_name
                FROM users u
                JOIN employee e ON e.employee_id = u.employee_id
                JOIN role r ON r.role_id = u.role_id
                WHERE u.user_id = %s
                """,
                (user_id,),
            )
            row = cur.fetchone()
            return dict(row) if row else None

    def require(self, user_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = self.read_by_id(user_id)
        if not row:
            raise NotFoundError(f"User ID {user_id} was not found.")
        return row

    def read_by_username(self, username: str) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        username = require_username(username)
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT u.*, e.email, e.first_name, e.last_name, r.role_code, r.role_name
                FROM users u
                JOIN employee e ON e.employee_id = u.employee_id
                JOIN role r ON r.role_id = u.role_id
                WHERE u.username = %s
                """,
                (username,),
            )
            row = cur.fetchone()
            return dict(row) if row else None

    def update(self, user_id: int, **fields: Any) -> int:
        self.require(user_id)
        allowed = {"role_id", "password_hash", "account_status"}
        cols = {k: v for k, v in fields.items() if k in allowed}
        if "role_id" in cols:
            cols["role_id"] = require_positive_id(cols["role_id"], "Role ID")
        if "password_hash" in cols:
            cols["password_hash"] = require_nonempty(cols["password_hash"], "Password hash")
        if "account_status" in cols:
            cols["account_status"] = require_account_status(cols["account_status"])
        if not cols:
            raise ValidationError("No valid fields to update.")
        sets = ", ".join(f"{k} = %s" for k in cols)
        return int(
            self.execute(
                f"UPDATE users SET {sets}, updated_at = NOW() WHERE user_id = %s",
                (*cols.values(), user_id),
            )
        )

    def update_account_status(self, user_id: int, status: str) -> int:
        return self.update(user_id, account_status=require_account_status(status))

    def update_password_hash(self, user_id: int, password_hash: str) -> int:
        """Set a new password hash and stamp password_changed_at."""
        user_id = require_positive_id(user_id, "User ID")
        password_hash = require_nonempty(password_hash, "Password hash")
        self.require(user_id)
        return int(
            self.execute(
                """
                UPDATE users
                SET password_hash = %s,
                    password_changed_at = NOW(),
                    updated_at = NOW()
                WHERE user_id = %s
                """,
                (password_hash, user_id),
            )
        )

    def record_login_success(self, user_id: int) -> None:
        self.execute(
            "UPDATE users SET last_login = NOW(), updated_at = NOW() WHERE user_id = %s",
            (require_positive_id(user_id, "User ID"),),
        )

    def delete(self, user_id: int) -> int:
        self.require(user_id)
        return self.delete_by_id("user_id", user_id)

========================================================================================
FILE: database/connection.py
========================================================================================
"""PostgreSQL connectivity for the NewsMind console backend."""

from __future__ import annotations

from contextlib import contextmanager
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Generator, Optional

import psycopg2
from psycopg2.extensions import connection as PgConnection
from psycopg2.extras import RealDictCursor

from config.db_config import DatabaseConfig, load_db_config
from utils.logger import get_logger

log = get_logger("db")
ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
SCHEMA_SQL = ROOT / "database" / "newsmind.sql"


def get_connection(cfg: Optional[DatabaseConfig] = None) -> PgConnection:
    cfg = cfg or load_db_config()
    return psycopg2.connect(**cfg.dsn_kwargs())


@contextmanager
def db_cursor(cfg: Optional[DatabaseConfig] = None) -> Generator:
    cfg = cfg or load_db_config()
    conn = get_connection(cfg)
    try:
        with conn:
            with conn.cursor(cursor_factory=RealDictCursor) as cur:
                yield cur
    finally:
        conn.close()


def apply_sql_file(path: Path, cfg: Optional[DatabaseConfig] = None) -> None:
    """Apply a SQL file statement-by-statement."""
    raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    lines: list[str] = []
    for line in raw.splitlines():
        stripped = line.strip()
        if stripped.startswith("--"):
            continue
        lines.append(line)
    text = "\n".join(lines)
    statements = [s.strip() for s in text.split(";") if s.strip()]
    cfg = cfg or load_db_config()
    conn = get_connection(cfg)
    try:
        with conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                for stmt in statements:
                    cur.execute(stmt)
        log.info("Applied SQL file %s (%s statements)", path.name, len(statements))
    finally:
        conn.close()


def require_schema(cfg: Optional[DatabaseConfig] = None) -> None:
    """Fail fast if the DB was not initialized with newsmind.sql."""
    required = ("users", "content", "ml_train_file", "adaptation_requests")
    with db_cursor(cfg) as cur:
        cur.execute(
            """
            SELECT table_name
            FROM information_schema.tables
            WHERE table_schema = 'public'
              AND table_name = ANY(%s)
            """,
            (list(required),),
        )
        found = {str(row["table_name"]) for row in cur.fetchall() or []}
    missing = [name for name in required if name not in found]
    if missing:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Database schema incomplete (missing: "
            + ", ".join(missing)
            + "). Run: python app.py --init-db"
        )


def init_schema(cfg: Optional[DatabaseConfig] = None) -> None:
    """Fresh install from database/newsmind.sql, then seed demo users."""
    cfg = cfg or load_db_config()
    apply_sql_file(SCHEMA_SQL, cfg)
    from database.seed_demo_users import seed_demo_users

    seed_demo_users()


def check_connection(cfg: Optional[DatabaseConfig] = None) -> bool:
    try:
        with db_cursor(cfg) as cur:
            cur.execute("SELECT 1 AS ok")
            row = cur.fetchone()
            return bool(row and row.get("ok") == 1)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        log.error("Database connection failed: %s", exc)
        return False

-- NewsMindAI V2 — single database bootstrap (fresh install; wipes public schema).
-- Applied by: python app.py --init-db
-- Includes: RBAC/desks, content/evidence, CAE/publisher, ml_train_*.
-- Relevancy uses desk-published content only (TF-IDF + cosine); no history CSV corpus.

BEGIN;

CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "pgcrypto";

DROP SCHEMA IF EXISTS public CASCADE;
CREATE SCHEMA public;
GRANT ALL ON SCHEMA public TO PUBLIC;

CREATE TABLE employee (
    employee_id         BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    employee_code       VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    first_name          VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    last_name           VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    email               VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    mobile_number       VARCHAR(32),
    department          VARCHAR(128),
    designation         VARCHAR(128),
    location            VARCHAR(128),
    employment_status   VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
    joining_date        DATE,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE role (
    role_id             BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    role_code           VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    role_name           VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    role_description    TEXT,
    is_active           BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE users (
    user_id             BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    employee_id         BIGINT NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES employee(employee_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    role_id             BIGINT NOT NULL REFERENCES role(role_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    username            VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    password_hash       TEXT NOT NULL,
    account_status      VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
    last_login          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    password_changed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE audience_insight_analyst (
    aia_id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id             BIGINT NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES users(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    focus_region        VARCHAR(128),
    status              VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE editor (
    editor_id           BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id             BIGINT NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES users(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    editorial_desk      VARCHAR(128),

    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

    status              VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',

    trend_score         NUMERIC(8,4) DEFAULT 0,

    approved_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,

);

    published_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,

    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

ALTER TABLE raw_ingest

CREATE INDEX idx_raw_ingest_status ON raw_ingest(cleaning_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_content_status ON content(status);

    validation_flags    JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,

    remarks             TEXT,
    finalized_at        TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

    scheduled_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    published_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE INDEX idx_publication_status ON publication(status);

"""Seed demo desks using usernames + passwords from config.constants.DEMO_PASSWORDS."""

from __future__ import annotations

from config.constants import DEMO_PASSWORDS, ROLE_DISPLAY_NAMES
from dao.audience_insight_analyst_dao import AudienceInsightAnalystDAO
from dao.content_adaptation_editor_dao import ContentAdaptationEditorDAO
from dao.editor_dao import EditorDAO
from dao.employee_dao import EmployeeDAO
from dao.journalist_dao import JournalistDAO
from dao.newsroom_manager_dao import NewsroomManagerDAO
from dao.publisher_dao import PublisherDAO
from dao.role_dao import RoleDAO
from dao.user_dao import UserDAO
from entities.employee import Employee
from entities.user import User
from modules.authentication.security import hash_password
from utils.logger import get_logger

log = get_logger("seed_users")

DEMO_USERS = (
    ("admin", "administrator", "System", "Admin", "admin@newsroom.local"),
    ("analyst", "audience_insight_analyst", "Asha", "Analyst", "analyst@newsroom.local"),
    ("journalist", "journalist", "Jordan", "Journalist", "journalist@newsroom.local"),
    ("editor", "editor", "Elena", "Editor", "editor@newsroom.local"),
    ("adapter", "content_adaptation_editor", "Casey", "Adapter", "adapter@newsroom.local"),
    ("publisher", "publisher", "Priya", "Publisher", "publisher@newsroom.local"),
    ("manager", "newsroom_manager", "Morgan", "Manager", "manager@newsroom.local"),
)


def _ensure_profile(user_id: int, role_code: str) -> None:
    if role_code == "audience_insight_analyst":
        AudienceInsightAnalystDAO().ensure_for_user(user_id)
    elif role_code == "editor":
        EditorDAO().ensure_for_user(user_id)
    elif role_code == "journalist":
        JournalistDAO().ensure_for_user(user_id)
    elif role_code == "content_adaptation_editor":
        ContentAdaptationEditorDAO().ensure_for_user(user_id)
    elif role_code == "publisher":
        PublisherDAO().ensure_for_user(user_id)
    elif role_code == "newsroom_manager":
        NewsroomManagerDAO().ensure_for_user(user_id)


def seed_demo_users() -> int:
    employees = EmployeeDAO()
    users = UserDAO()
    roles = RoleDAO()
    created = 0
    for username, role_code, first, last, email in DEMO_USERS:
        role_id = roles.get_id_by_code(role_code)
        if role_id is None:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Missing role {role_code} — run python app.py --init-db first")
        emp_id = employees.create(
            Employee(
                employee_code=f"NF-{username.upper()}",
                first_name=first,
                last_name=last,
                email=email,
                department="Newsroom",
                designation=ROLE_DISPLAY_NAMES.get(role_code, role_code),
            )
        )
        user_id = users.create(
            User(
                employee_id=emp_id,
                role_id=role_id,
                username=username,
                password_hash=hash_password(DEMO_PASSWORDS[username]),
                account_status="active",
            )
        )
        _ensure_profile(user_id, role_code)
        created += 1
        log.info("Seeded user=%s role=%s", username, role_code)
    return created

========================================================================================
FILE: database/seed_temp_data.sql
========================================================================================
-- NewsMindAI V2 — temporary / probe desk data for local testing.
-- Prerequisite: python app.py --init-db  (roles + demo users must already exist)
--
-- Apply (recommended):
--   python -c "from pathlib import Path; from database.connection import apply_sql_file; apply_sql_file(Path('database/seed_temp_data.sql'))"
-- Or:
--   psql -h localhost -U postgres -d newsmind_console -f database/seed_temp_data.sql
--
-- Safe to re-run: deletes prior rows with these seed titles, then inserts fresh.
-- Does NOT create demo login users (see database/seed_demo_users.py).
--
-- NOTE: avoid DO $$ blocks — apply_sql_file splits on ';' and cannot run them.
-- Leave BEGIN/COMMIT out so database.connection.apply_sql_file wraps one transaction.
-- Published rows use source_type='desk' so related-content / TF-IDF can find them.

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Resolve desk IDs from demo usernames
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TEMP TABLE _seed_ids AS
SELECT
    (SELECT user_id FROM users WHERE username = 'journalist') AS journalist_user_id,
    (SELECT journalist_id FROM journalist j
        JOIN users u ON u.user_id = j.user_id
     WHERE u.username = 'journalist') AS journalist_id,
    (SELECT user_id FROM users WHERE username = 'analyst') AS analyst_user_id,
    (SELECT aia_id FROM audience_insight_analyst a
        JOIN users u ON u.user_id = a.user_id
     WHERE u.username = 'analyst') AS aia_id,
    (SELECT user_id FROM users WHERE username = 'editor') AS editor_user_id,
    (SELECT user_id FROM users WHERE username = 'manager') AS manager_user_id,
    (SELECT user_id FROM users WHERE username = 'publisher') AS publisher_user_id;

SELECT 1 / CASE WHEN journalist_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END AS journalist_ok
FROM _seed_ids;

SELECT 1 / CASE WHEN aia_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END AS analyst_ok
FROM _seed_ids;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Extra categories
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
INSERT INTO category (category_name, description, is_active) VALUES
    ('Transit', 'Transit and infrastructure', TRUE),
    ('Local', 'Local desk topics', TRUE),
    ('Health', 'Public health and hospitals', TRUE),
    ('Education', 'Schools and universities', TRUE),
    ('Technology', 'Tech and digital policy', TRUE)
ON CONFLICT (category_name) DO UPDATE
SET description = EXCLUDED.description,
    is_active = TRUE,
    updated_at = NOW();

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Title registry (cleanup + inserts share this list)
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TEMP TABLE _seed_titles (
    title VARCHAR(512) PRIMARY KEY
);

INSERT INTO _seed_titles (title) VALUES
    ('City council approves downtown metro expansion plan'),
    ('Metro expansion: what residents need to know'),
    ('Neighborhood groups seek metro construction noise limits'),
    ('Opinion roundup: was the metro vote rushed?'),
    ('University district prepares for metro station groundbreaking'),

    title VARCHAR(512) PRIMARY KEY
);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Clear previous seed rows
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
DELETE FROM publication
WHERE content_id IN (
    SELECT c.content_id FROM content c
    JOIN _seed_titles t ON t.title = c.title
);

DELETE FROM content
WHERE title IN (SELECT title FROM _seed_titles);

DELETE FROM story_pitch
WHERE title IN (SELECT title FROM _seed_pitch_titles);

# --- PostgreSQL (local or hosted) ---

NEWSROOM_DB_PORT=5432
NEWSROOM_DB_NAME=newsmind_console
NEWSROOM_DB_USER=postgres
NEWSROOM_DB_PASSWORD=admin
# For cloud Postgres often: require
NEWSROOM_DB_SSLMODE=prefer
NEWSROOM_DB_CONNECT_TIMEOUT=10

NEWSROOM_DEBUG=false

# --- Auth (JWT HS256) ---
# Required. App will not start auth without this (no hardcoded fallback).
NEWSROOM_JWT_SECRET=replace-with-a-long-random-secret

# --- Pipeline / IO ---
NEWSROOM_CSV_CHUNK_ROWS=2000
NEWSROOM_DB_INSERT_BATCH=200
NEWSROOM_MAX_CSV_MB_WARN=15
NEWSROOM_WRITE_PROCESSED_FILES=false
NEWSROOM_PIPELINE_FILE_BY_FILE=true

========================================================================================
FILE: ml/adaptation/features.py
========================================================================================
"""Sentence features for the unified CAE retention model (TF-IDF + manual + platform)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import re

import numpy as np
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix, hstack
from sklearn.base import BaseEstimator, TransformerMixin
from sklearn.feature_extraction.text import TfidfVectorizer
from sklearn.preprocessing import MaxAbsScaler

from config.constants import ADAPTATION_PLATFORMS

NEGATION = re.compile(r"\b(no|not|never|neither|without)\b", re.I)
UNCERTAINTY = re.compile(
    r"\b(may|might|could|expected|likely|possible|estimated)\b", re.I
)
ATTRIBUTION = re.compile(r"\b(said|stated|reported|announced|according)\b", re.I)
CONDITION = re.compile(
    r"\b(if|unless|subject to|provided|depending|after approval)\b", re.I
)
CONTRAST = re.compile(r"\b(but|however|although|despite|while)\b", re.I)
NUMBER = re.compile(r"\b\d[\d,]*(?:\.\d+)?\b")
PERCENT = re.compile(r"(?:\d+(?:\.\d+)?\s*%|\d+(?:\.\d+)?\s+percent)", re.I)
CURRENCY = re.compile(
    r"(?:₹|\$|£|€)\s*\d|\b\d+(?:\.\d+)?\s*(?:crore|lakh|million|billion)\b", re.I
)
DATE = re.compile(
    r"\b(?:Jan(?:uary)?|Feb(?:ruary)?|Mar(?:ch)?|Apr(?:il)?|May|Jun(?:e)?|"
    r"Jul(?:y)?|Aug(?:ust)?|Sep(?:tember)?|Oct(?:ober)?|Nov(?:ember)?|"
    r"Dec(?:ember)?)\s+\d{1,2}\b",
    re.I,
)


def numeric_features(records: list[dict]) -> np.ndarray:
    rows = []
    for r in records:
        s = r["sentence"]
        i = r["position"]
        n = max(r["total_sentences"] - 1, 1)
        platform = str(r.get("platform") or "").upper()
        one_hot = [1.0 if platform == code else 0.0 for code in ADAPTATION_PLATFORMS]
        rows.append(
            [
                i / n,
                len(s.split()),
                int(i == 0),
                int(i == 1),
                bool(NUMBER.search(s)),
                bool(PERCENT.search(s)),
                bool(CURRENCY.search(s)),
                bool(DATE.search(s)),
                int('"' in s or "“" in s),
                bool(NEGATION.search(s)),
                bool(UNCERTAINTY.search(s)),
                bool(ATTRIBUTION.search(s)),
                bool(CONDITION.search(s)),
                bool(CONTRAST.search(s)),
                *one_hot,
            ]
        )
    return np.asarray(rows, dtype=float)


class SentenceFeatureExtractor(BaseEstimator, TransformerMixin):
    def __init__(self, max_word_features: int = 30000, max_char_features: int = 20000):
        self.max_word_features = max_word_features
        self.max_char_features = max_char_features
        self.word = TfidfVectorizer(
            lowercase=True,
            stop_words="english",
            ngram_range=(1, 3),
            min_df=1,
            max_df=0.98,
            sublinear_tf=True,
            max_features=max_word_features,
        )
        self.char = TfidfVectorizer(
            analyzer="char_wb",
            ngram_range=(3, 5),
            min_df=1,
            sublinear_tf=True,
            max_features=max_char_features,
        )
        self.scaler = MaxAbsScaler()

    def fit(self, X, y=None):
        contexts = [r["context"] for r in X]
        self.word.fit(contexts)
        self.char.fit(contexts)
        self.scaler.fit(numeric_features(X))
        return self

    def transform(self, X):
        contexts = [r["context"] for r in X]
        a = self.word.transform(contexts)
        b = self.char.transform(contexts)
        c = csr_matrix(self.scaler.transform(numeric_features(X)))
        return hstack([a, b, c], format="csr")

========================================================================================
FILE: ml/adaptation/labeling.py
========================================================================================
"""Build sentence retain/reject labels from paired original ↔ adapted articles."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

import numpy as np
from sklearn.feature_extraction.text import TfidfVectorizer
from sklearn.metrics.pairwise import cosine_similarity

from ml.adaptation.sentences import context_window, split_sentences

STRONG_POSITIVE_THRESHOLD = 0.50
NEGATIVE_THRESHOLD = 0.20
MAX_POSITIVE_SENTENCES = {
    "SOCIAL_MEDIA": 3,
    "EMAIL_NEWSLETTER": 6,
    "PRINT_NEWSPAPER": 9,
    "WEBSITE": 11,
}
MIN_POSITIVE_SENTENCES = {
    "SOCIAL_MEDIA": 1,
    "EMAIL_NEWSLETTER": 2,
    "PRINT_NEWSPAPER": 3,
    "WEBSITE": 4,
}


def _create_similarity_matrix(
    source_sentences: list[str],
    target_sentences: list[str],
) -> np.ndarray:
    corpus = source_sentences + target_sentences
    word_vectorizer = TfidfVectorizer(
        lowercase=True,
        stop_words="english",
        ngram_range=(1, 2),
        sublinear_tf=True,
    )
    character_vectorizer = TfidfVectorizer(
        lowercase=True,
        analyzer="char_wb",
        ngram_range=(3, 5),
        sublinear_tf=True,
    )
    word_matrix = word_vectorizer.fit_transform(corpus)
    character_matrix = character_vectorizer.fit_transform(corpus)
    source_count = len(source_sentences)
    word_similarity = cosine_similarity(word_matrix[:source_count], word_matrix[source_count:])
    character_similarity = cosine_similarity(
        character_matrix[:source_count], character_matrix[source_count:]
    )
    return 0.70 * word_similarity + 0.30 * character_similarity


def _select_positive_indexes(similarity_matrix: np.ndarray, platform: str) -> set[int]:
    source_count, target_count = similarity_matrix.shape
    if source_count == 0 or target_count == 0:
        return set()

    maximum_scores = similarity_matrix.max(axis=1)
    selected_indexes: set[int] = set()
    for target_index in range(target_count):
        selected_indexes.add(int(np.argmax(similarity_matrix[:, target_index])))
    for source_index, score in enumerate(maximum_scores):
        if score >= STRONG_POSITIVE_THRESHOLD:
            selected_indexes.add(source_index)

    minimum_count = min(MIN_POSITIVE_SENTENCES.get(platform, 1), source_count)
    maximum_count = min(MAX_POSITIVE_SENTENCES.get(platform, max(1, target_count)), source_count)
    ranked_indexes = np.argsort(maximum_scores)[::-1].tolist()
    for source_index in ranked_indexes:
        if len(selected_indexes) >= minimum_count:
            break
        selected_indexes.add(int(source_index))
    if len(selected_indexes) > maximum_count:
        selected_indexes = set(
            sorted(selected_indexes, key=lambda i: maximum_scores[i], reverse=True)[
                :maximum_count
            ]
        )
    return selected_indexes


def build_sentence_records(row: dict[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    source_sentences = split_sentences(str(row["original_content"]))
    target_sentences = split_sentences(str(row["correct_adapted_article"]))
    if not source_sentences or not target_sentences:
        return []

    similarity_matrix = _create_similarity_matrix(source_sentences, target_sentences)
    maximum_scores = similarity_matrix.max(axis=1)
    positive_indexes = _select_positive_indexes(
        similarity_matrix, str(row["target_platform"])
    )

    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for position, sentence in enumerate(source_sentences):
        records.append(
            {
                "article_id": str(row["article_id"]),
                "platform": str(row["target_platform"]),
                "headline": str(row["original_title"]),
                "sentence": sentence,
                "context": context_window(source_sentences, position),
                "position": position,
                "total_sentences": len(source_sentences),
                "label": 1 if position in positive_indexes else 0,
                "label_similarity": float(maximum_scores[position]),
            }
        )
    return records

========================================================================================
FILE: ml/adaptation/model_loader.py
========================================================================================
"""Load the unified CAE sentence-retention model from artifacts/models/cae/."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from functools import lru_cache
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
MODEL_DIR = ROOT / "artifacts" / "models" / "cae"


@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
def load_model(_platform: str | None = None) -> tuple[object, dict]:
    """Load the single shared CAE model. Platform is kept for API compatibility."""
    model_path = MODEL_DIR / "model.joblib"
    metadata_path = MODEL_DIR / "metadata.json"
    if not model_path.exists() or not metadata_path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            "No trained CAE model. "
            "Push CSV to DB then train CAE via the Admin console ML TRAINING menu, "
            "ml.training.train_cae_from_db(), or: python -m ml.training --cae"
        )
    import joblib

    return joblib.load(model_path), json.loads(metadata_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def clear_model_cache() -> None:
    load_model.cache_clear()

========================================================================================
FILE: ml/adaptation/platforms.py
========================================================================================
"""Platform-specific adaptation formatting."""

from __future__ import annotations

import re

from config.constants import (
    PLATFORM_EMAIL,
    PLATFORM_PRINT,
    PLATFORM_SOCIAL,
    PLATFORM_WEB,
)


def clean_title(title: str) -> str:
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", title or "").strip().rstrip(".")


def _keywords(text: str, limit: int = 2) -> list[str]:
    stop = {
        "the",
        "and",
        "for",
        "with",
        "from",
        "will",
        "this",
        "that",
        "have",
        "has",
        "was",
        "were",
        "are",
        "its",
        "into",
        "after",
        "before",
        "about",
    }
    words = re.findall(r"[A-Za-z]{4,}", (text or "").lower())
    counts = {w: words.count(w) for w in set(words) if w not in stop}
    return [w.title() for w, _ in sorted(counts.items(), key=lambda x: (-x[1], x[0]))[:limit]]


def format_output(
    platform: str,
    original_title: str,
    selected: list[tuple[int, str, float]],
) -> tuple[str, str]:
    sentences = [item[1] for item in selected]
    title = clean_title(original_title)

    if platform == PLATFORM_SOCIAL:
        content = " ".join(sentences)
        tags = " ".join("#" + k.replace(" ", "") for k in _keywords(content, 2))
        return title, f"{content} {tags}".strip()

    if platform == PLATFORM_EMAIL:
        return title, " ".join(sentences)

    if platform == PLATFORM_PRINT:
        return title, " ".join(sentences)

    # Website — paragraph chunks, no invented summaries
    chunks = []
    for i in range(0, len(sentences), 3):
        chunks.append(" ".join(sentences[i : i + 3]))
    return title, "\n\n".join(chunks)

========================================================================================
FILE: ml/adaptation/predict.py
========================================================================================
"""Select sentences for a target platform using the unified CAE model."""

from __future__ import annotations

from config.constants import PLATFORM_SENTENCE_LIMITS
from ml.adaptation.model_loader import load_model
from ml.adaptation.sentences import split_sentences
from utils.errors import ValidationError

DEPENDENT_STARTERS = ("however", "but", "therefore", "this", "they", "it", "he", "she", "although")


def predict_sentences(
    title: str,
    content: str,
    platform: str,
    threshold_delta: float = 0.0,
    sentence_delta: int = 0,
    prioritize_quotes: bool = False,
    prioritize_numbers: bool = False,
) -> tuple[list[tuple[int, str, float]], dict, float]:
    sentences = split_sentences(content)
    if not sentences:
        raise ValidationError("No valid sentences found in original content.")

    try:
        model, meta = load_model(platform)
    except FileNotFoundError as exc:
        raise ValidationError(str(exc)) from exc

    platform_key = str(platform).upper()
    records = []
    for i, sentence in enumerate(sentences):
        prev = sentences[i - 1] if i > 0 else ""
        nxt = sentences[i + 1] if i + 1 < len(sentences) else ""
        records.append(
            {
                "headline": title,
                "sentence": sentence,
                "context": f"PREVIOUS {prev} CURRENT {sentence} NEXT {nxt}",
                "position": i,
                "total_sentences": len(sentences),
                "platform": platform_key,
            }
        )
    probabilities = [float(p) for p in model.predict_proba(records)[:, 1]]

    platform_thresholds = meta.get("platform_thresholds") or {}
    base = float(
        platform_thresholds.get(platform_key, meta.get("threshold", 0.5))
    )
    threshold = max(0.05, min(0.95, base + threshold_delta))
    model_version = str(meta.get("version", "1.0.0"))

    scored: list[tuple[int, str, float]] = []
    for i, (sentence, prob) in enumerate(zip(sentences, probabilities)):
        boost = 0.0
        if prioritize_quotes and ('"' in sentence or "“" in sentence):
            boost += 0.15
        if prioritize_numbers and any(ch.isdigit() for ch in sentence):
            boost += 0.15
        scored.append((i, sentence, min(1.0, float(prob) + boost)))

    max_count = max(1, int(PLATFORM_SENTENCE_LIMITS.get(platform, 5)) + int(sentence_delta))
    selected = [row for row in scored if row[2] >= threshold]
    if not selected:
        selected = sorted(scored, key=lambda row: row[2], reverse=True)[:1]
    selected = sorted(selected, key=lambda row: row[2], reverse=True)[:max_count]

    indexes = {row[0] for row in selected}
    for i in list(indexes):
        if i > 0 and sentences[i].lower().startswith(DEPENDENT_STARTERS):
            indexes.add(i - 1)

    ordered = [(i, sentences[i], float(probabilities[i])) for i in sorted(indexes)]
    meta = {**meta, "version": model_version, "platform": platform_key}
    return ordered, meta, threshold

========================================================================================
FILE: ml/adaptation/sentences.py
========================================================================================
"""Sentence splitting for adaptation."""

from __future__ import annotations

import re

ABBREVIATION_PATTERN = re.compile(r"\b(?:Mr|Mrs|Ms|Dr|Prof|Inc|Ltd|No)\.$", re.I)
_WS = re.compile(r"\s+")


def normalize_space(text: str) -> str:
    return _WS.sub(" ", text or "").strip()


def split_sentences(text: str) -> list[str]:
    text = normalize_space(text)
    if not text:
        return []
    pieces = re.split(r'(?<=[.!?])\s+(?=[A-Z“"])', text)
    result: list[str] = []
    carry = ""
    for piece in pieces:
        piece = f"{carry} {piece}".strip() if carry else piece.strip()
        if ABBREVIATION_PATTERN.search(piece):
            carry = piece
            continue
        carry = ""
        if len(piece.split()) >= 3:
            result.append(piece)
    if carry:
        result.append(carry)
    return result


def context_window(sentences: list[str], position: int) -> str:
    """PREVIOUS / CURRENT / NEXT string used by CAE feature extractor."""
    prev = sentences[position - 1] if position > 0 else ""
    current = sentences[position] if 0 <= position < len(sentences) else ""
    nxt = sentences[position + 1] if position + 1 < len(sentences) else ""
    return f"PREVIOUS {prev} CURRENT {current} NEXT {nxt}"

========================================================================================
FILE: ml/adaptation/training.py
========================================================================================
"""Train one unified CAE sentence-retention model for all platforms."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

import joblib
import pandas as pd
from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression
from sklearn.metrics import (
    accuracy_score,
    average_precision_score,
    classification_report,
    confusion_matrix,
    precision_recall_fscore_support,
)
from sklearn.model_selection import GroupShuffleSplit
from sklearn.pipeline import Pipeline

from config.constants import ADAPTATION_PLATFORMS
from ml.adaptation.features import SentenceFeatureExtractor
from ml.adaptation.labeling import build_sentence_records
from ml.adaptation.model_loader import clear_model_cache

# Default keep threshold; predict can still apply per-platform overrides.
PLATFORM_THRESHOLDS = {
    "SOCIAL_MEDIA": 0.70,
    "EMAIL_NEWSLETTER": 0.55,
    "PRINT_NEWSPAPER": 0.40,
    "WEBSITE": 0.35,
}
DEFAULT_THRESHOLD = 0.50

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
DEFAULT_OUTPUT = ROOT / "artifacts" / "models"
CAE_FOLDER = "cae"


def grouped_split(sentence_df: pd.DataFrame) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]:
    first_split = GroupShuffleSplit(n_splits=1, test_size=0.30, random_state=42)
    train_indexes, remaining_indexes = next(
        first_split.split(sentence_df, groups=sentence_df["article_id"])
    )
    train_df = sentence_df.iloc[train_indexes].reset_index(drop=True)
    remaining_df = sentence_df.iloc[remaining_indexes].reset_index(drop=True)
    second_split = GroupShuffleSplit(n_splits=1, test_size=0.50, random_state=42)
    validation_indexes, test_indexes = next(
        second_split.split(remaining_df, groups=remaining_df["article_id"])
    )
    validation_df = remaining_df.iloc[validation_indexes].reset_index(drop=True)
    test_df = remaining_df.iloc[test_indexes].reset_index(drop=True)
    return train_df, validation_df, test_df


def evaluate(pipeline: Pipeline, frame: pd.DataFrame, threshold: float) -> dict:
    features = frame.to_dict("records")
    actual = frame["label"].to_numpy()
    probabilities = pipeline.predict_proba(features)[:, 1]
    predicted = (probabilities >= threshold).astype(int)
    precision, recall, f1, _ = precision_recall_fscore_support(
        actual, predicted, average="binary", zero_division=0
    )
    ap = (
        float(average_precision_score(actual, probabilities))
        if len(set(actual)) >= 2
        else 0.0
    )
    return {
        "accuracy": float(accuracy_score(actual, predicted)),
        "precision": float(precision),
        "recall": float(recall),
        "f1": float(f1),
        "average_precision": ap,
        "confusion_matrix": confusion_matrix(actual, predicted, labels=[0, 1]).tolist(),
        "records": int(len(frame)),
    }


def _print_matrix(label: str, split: str, metrics: dict) -> None:
    cm = metrics["confusion_matrix"]
    print()
    print(f"{label} — {split} metrics")
    print("-" * 70)
    print(f"Accuracy: {metrics['accuracy']:.4f}")
    print(f"Precision: {metrics['precision']:.4f}")
    print(f"Recall: {metrics['recall']:.4f}")
    print(f"F1: {metrics['f1']:.4f}")
    print(f"Average precision: {metrics['average_precision']:.4f}")
    print(f"Records: {metrics['records']}")
    print("Confusion matrix [[TN, FP], [FN, TP]]:")
    print(f"  {cm[0]}")
    print(f"  {cm[1]}")


def validate_source_dataset(dataframe: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    required = {
        "article_id",
        "original_title",
        "original_content",
        "target_platform",
        "correct_adapted_article",
    }
    missing = required - set(dataframe.columns)
    if missing:
        raise ValueError(f"Missing columns: {sorted(missing)}")
    cleaned = dataframe.copy()
    for col in required:
        cleaned[col] = cleaned[col].fillna("").astype(str).str.strip()
    empty = cleaned[
        (cleaned["article_id"] == "")
        | (cleaned["original_content"] == "")
        | (cleaned["correct_adapted_article"] == "")
    ]
    if not empty.empty:
        raise ValueError(f"{len(empty)} rows missing required text fields.")
    supported = set(ADAPTATION_PLATFORMS)
    unsupported = set(cleaned["target_platform"].unique()) - supported
    if unsupported:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported platforms: {sorted(unsupported)}")
    print()
    print("Source dataset validation")
    print("-" * 70)
    print(f"Dataset rows: {len(cleaned)}")
    print(f"Unique articles: {cleaned['article_id'].nunique()}")
    print(cleaned["target_platform"].value_counts().to_string())
    return cleaned


def train_unified_model(
    source_df: pd.DataFrame,
    output_root: Path,
    version: str = "1.0.0",
) -> dict:
    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    print("Preparing unified CAE model (all platforms)")
    print("=" * 70)

    sentence_rows: list[dict] = []
    for row in source_df.to_dict("records"):
        sentence_rows.extend(build_sentence_records(row))
    sentence_df = pd.DataFrame(sentence_rows)
    if sentence_df.empty:
        raise ValueError("No sentence records generated for CAE training.")

    label_counts = sentence_df["label"].value_counts().sort_index().to_dict()
    article_count = int(sentence_df["article_id"].nunique())
    print(f"Paired articles: {len(source_df)}")
    print(f"Unique articles: {article_count}")
    print(f"Sentence records: {len(sentence_df)}")
    print(f"Label distribution: {label_counts}")
    print(f"Platforms: {sorted(sentence_df['platform'].unique())}")
    if sentence_df["label"].nunique() < 2:
        raise ValueError(f"Need both classes for CAE: {label_counts}")

    train_df, validation_df, test_df = grouped_split(sentence_df)
    for name, frame in (
        ("training", train_df),
        ("validation", validation_df),
        ("testing", test_df),
    ):
        counts = frame["label"].value_counts().sort_index().to_dict()
        print(f"CAE {name} labels: {counts}")
        if frame["label"].nunique() < 2:
            raise ValueError(f"CAE {name} has one class only: {counts}")

    pipeline = Pipeline(
        steps=[
            ("features", SentenceFeatureExtractor()),
            (
                "classifier",
                LogisticRegression(
                    max_iter=2500,
                    class_weight="balanced",
                    C=1.0,
                    solver="liblinear",
                    random_state=42,
                ),
            ),
        ]
    )
    print("Training unified CAE model...")
    pipeline.fit(train_df.to_dict("records"), train_df["label"].to_numpy())

    threshold = DEFAULT_THRESHOLD
    metrics = {
        "validation": evaluate(pipeline, validation_df, threshold),
        "test": evaluate(pipeline, test_df, threshold),
    }
    _print_matrix("CAE (all platforms)", "validation", metrics["validation"])
    _print_matrix("CAE (all platforms)", "test", metrics["test"])

    # Per-platform test slices (same model, platform-specific threshold)
    per_platform: dict[str, dict] = {}
    for platform in ADAPTATION_PLATFORMS:
        slice_df = test_df[test_df["platform"] == platform]
        if slice_df.empty or slice_df["label"].nunique() < 2:
            continue
        plat_threshold = PLATFORM_THRESHOLDS.get(platform, threshold)
        plat_metrics = evaluate(pipeline, slice_df, plat_threshold)
        per_platform[platform] = plat_metrics
        _print_matrix(platform, "test", plat_metrics)

    test_probs = pipeline.predict_proba(test_df.to_dict("records"))[:, 1]
    test_pred = (test_probs >= threshold).astype(int)
    print()
    print(classification_report(test_df["label"], test_pred, digits=3, zero_division=0))
    print(
        pd.DataFrame(
            confusion_matrix(test_df["label"], test_pred, labels=[0, 1]),
            index=["actual_0", "actual_1"],
            columns=["pred_0", "pred_1"],
        )
    )

    platform_folder = output_root / CAE_FOLDER
    platform_folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    model_path = platform_folder / "model.joblib"
    metadata_path = platform_folder / "metadata.json"
    metrics_path = platform_folder / "metrics.json"
    joblib.dump(pipeline, model_path)

    metadata = {
        "model": "content_adaptation",
        "version": version,
        "algorithm": "LogisticRegression",
        "feature_extractor": "Word TF-IDF + Character TF-IDF + Manual Features + Platform",
        "threshold": threshold,
        "platform_thresholds": PLATFORM_THRESHOLDS,
        "platforms": list(ADAPTATION_PLATFORMS),
        "trained_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "paired_records": int(len(source_df)),
        "unique_articles": article_count,
        "sentence_records": int(len(sentence_df)),
        "train_records": int(len(train_df)),
        "validation_records": int(len(validation_df)),
        "test_records": int(len(test_df)),
        "label_distribution": {str(k): int(v) for k, v in label_counts.items()},
    }
    # Sidecar for runtime: default threshold + per-platform overrides + version only extras needed
    sidecar_metadata = {
        "version": version,
        "threshold": threshold,
        "platform_thresholds": PLATFORM_THRESHOLDS,
    }
    metadata_path.write_text(json.dumps(sidecar_metadata, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    metrics_path.write_text(
        json.dumps({"overall": metrics, "per_platform": per_platform}, indent=2),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    print(f"Model saved: {model_path}")
    clear_model_cache()
    return {
        "metadata": metadata,
        "metrics": metrics,
        "per_platform": per_platform,
    }


def train_all_from_dataframe(
    source_df: pd.DataFrame,
    output_root: Path | None = None,
    version: str = "1.0.0",
) -> dict:
    """Train the single shared CAE model. Name kept for console compatibility."""
    source_df = validate_source_dataset(source_df)
    output_directory = Path(output_root or DEFAULT_OUTPUT)
    output_directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    result = train_unified_model(
        source_df=source_df,
        output_root=output_directory,
        version=version,
    )
    # Keep CAE outputs under artifacts/models/cae/ only (no extra root clutter)
    summary_path = output_directory / CAE_FOLDER / "training_summary.json"
    summary_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    summary = {"content_adaptation": result}
    summary_path.write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    print("Unified CAE model trained successfully")
    print(f"Training summary: {summary_path}")
    print("=" * 70)
    return summary

========================================================================================
FILE: ml/adaptation/validate.py
========================================================================================
"""Fact checks — block invented numbers/currency relative to source."""

from __future__ import annotations

import re


def _tokens(pattern: str, text: str) -> set[str]:
    return set(re.findall(pattern, text or "", re.I))


def validate_facts(source: str, output: str) -> dict:
    source_numbers = _tokens(r"\b\d[\d,.]*\b", source)
    output_numbers = _tokens(r"\b\d[\d,.]*\b", output)
    invented = sorted(output_numbers - source_numbers)

    source_currency = _tokens(r"(?:₹|\$|£|€)\s*\d[\d,.]*", source)
    output_currency = _tokens(r"(?:₹|\$|£|€)\s*\d[\d,.]*", output)
    invented_currency = sorted(output_currency - source_currency)

    return {
        "invented_numbers": invented,
        "invented_currency": invented_currency,
        "passed": not invented and not invented_currency,
    }

"""AIA helpers: engagement / interest / trend formulas."""

from ml.aia.audience_scores import (

========================================================================================
FILE: ml/aia/audience_scores.py
========================================================================================
"""AIA audience scores — formulas only (no category model).

1) Engagement score  — formula from views/likes/shares/comments
2) Audience interest — formula from how intense the interactions are
3) Trend score       — heuristic from headline/body cues
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from typing import Any

TREND_WORDS = re.compile(
    r"\b(breaking|surge|record|viral|trending|boom|crisis|election|finals|"
    r"championship|outbreak|ban|protest|strike|launch)\b",
    re.I,
)


def predict_engagement(
    *,
    views: float = 120,
    likes: float = 25,
    shares: float = 8,
    comments: float = 4,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Simple engagement score from interaction counts."""
    raw = likes * 2.0 + shares * 3.0 + comments * 4.0 + views * 0.05
    score = round(min(100.0, max(0.0, raw / 10.0)), 2)
    return {
        "engagement_score": score,
        "source": "heuristic",
        "raw": round(raw, 4),
        "features_used": ["views", "likes", "shares", "comments"],
    }


def predict_interest(
    *,
    views: float = 120,
    likes: float = 25,
    shares: float = 8,
    comments: float = 4,
    engagement_raw: float | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Simple audience-interest score (interactions per view)."""
    if engagement_raw is None:
        engagement_raw = float(
            predict_engagement(views=views, likes=likes, shares=shares, comments=comments)["raw"]
        )

    intensity = (likes + shares * 2.0 + comments * 2.0) / max(float(views), 1.0)
    score = round(min(100.0, max(0.0, intensity * 100.0)), 2)

    if score >= 70:
        segment = 2  # high interest
    elif score >= 35:
        segment = 1  # medium
    else:
        segment = 0  # low

    return {
        "audience_interest_score": score,
        "source": "heuristic",
        "segment": segment,
        "distance": None,
        "engagement_raw": round(float(engagement_raw), 4),
    }


def predict_trend(headline: str = "", body: str = "") -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Trend score from simple text cues (no category model)."""
    text = f"{headline or ''}. {body or ''}".strip()
    hits = len(TREND_WORDS.findall(text))
    length_boost = min(15.0, len(text.split()) / 20.0)
    score = round(min(100.0, max(20.0, 45.0 + hits * 12.0 + length_boost)), 2)
    return {
        "trend_score": score,
        "source": "heuristic",
        "category": None,
        "category_confidence": None,
    }
