========================================================================================
FILE: ml/common/entities.py
========================================================================================
"""Named-entity helpers.

Uses spaCy as a supporting library with a **blank** English pipeline + EntityRuler
(no trained model download, no model training). Falls back to regex if spaCy
is not installed.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from functools import lru_cache
from typing import Any

_PERSON = re.compile(r"\b([A-Z][a-z]+(?:\s+[A-Z][a-z]+)+)\b")
_DATE = re.compile(
    r"\b(\d{1,2}[/-]\d{1,2}[/-]\d{2,4}|"
    r"(?:Jan|Feb|Mar|Apr|May|Jun|Jul|Aug|Sep|Oct|Nov|Dec)[a-z]*\s+\d{1,2},?\s+\d{4}|"
    r"\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})\b",
    re.IGNORECASE,
)
_ORG = re.compile(
    r"\b([A-Z][A-Za-z]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z]+)*\s+"
    r"(?:Inc|Corp|Company|Ltd|LLC|University|Ministry|Commission|Agency))\b"
)
_LOCATION = re.compile(
    r"\b(?:in|at|from|near)\s+([A-Z][a-z]+(?:\s+[A-Z][a-z]+)?)\b"
)

_EMPTY: dict[str, list[str]] = {
    "people": [],
    "organizations": [],
    "locations": [],
    "dates": [],
    "other": [],
}


def _regex_entities(text: str) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    return {
        "people": list(dict.fromkeys(_PERSON.findall(text))),
        "organizations": list(dict.fromkeys(_ORG.findall(text))),
        "locations": list(dict.fromkeys(_LOCATION.findall(text))),
        "dates": list(dict.fromkeys(_DATE.findall(text))),
        "other": [],
    }


@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
def _spacy_ruler():
    """Blank spaCy English + pattern ruler. Never downloads a model."""
    import spacy
    from spacy.pipeline import EntityRuler

    nlp = spacy.blank("en")
    ruler: EntityRuler = nlp.add_pipe("entity_ruler")
    patterns: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
        {"label": "DATE", "pattern": [{"SHAPE": "dd/dd/dddd"}]},
        {"label": "DATE", "pattern": [{"SHAPE": "dddd-dd-dd"}]},
        {
            "label": "ORG",
            "pattern": [
                {"IS_TITLE": True, "OP": "+"},
                {"LOWER": {"IN": ["inc", "corp", "company", "ltd", "llc", "university", "ministry", "commission", "agency"]}},
            ],
        },
        {
            "label": "PERSON",
            "pattern": [{"IS_TITLE": True}, {"IS_TITLE": True}],
        },
        {
            "label": "GPE",
            "pattern": [
                {"LOWER": {"IN": ["in", "at", "from", "near"]}},
                {"IS_TITLE": True},
            ],
        },
    ]
    ruler.add_patterns(patterns)
    return nlp


def _spacy_entities(text: str) -> dict[str, list[str]] | None:
    try:
        nlp = _spacy_ruler()
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — spaCy missing or pipe setup failed
        return None

    doc = nlp(text)
    result: dict[str, list[str]] = {
        "people": [],
        "organizations": [],
        "locations": [],
        "dates": [],
        "other": [],
    }
    seen: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()
    for ent in doc.ents:
        value = ent.text.strip()
        # EntityRuler GPE pattern includes the preposition — keep the place name only
        if ent.label_ == "GPE" and " " in value:
            value = value.split(None, 1)[-1]
        key = (ent.label_, value.lower())
        if not value or key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        if ent.label_ == "PERSON":
            result["people"].append(value)
        elif ent.label_ == "ORG":
            result["organizations"].append(value)
        elif ent.label_ in {"GPE", "LOC"}:
            result["locations"].append(value)
        elif ent.label_ == "DATE":
            result["dates"].append(value)
        else:
            result["other"].append(value)

    # Merge regex hits so month-name dates and similar still appear
    regex = _regex_entities(text)
    for field in ("people", "organizations", "locations", "dates"):
        merged = list(dict.fromkeys([*result[field], *regex[field]]))
        result[field] = merged
    return result


def extract_entities(text: str) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    """Extract people, orgs, locations, dates. Empty text → empty lists."""
    raw = "" if text is None else str(text).strip()
    if not raw:
        return dict(_EMPTY)

    spacy_result = _spacy_entities(raw)
    if spacy_result is not None:
        return spacy_result
    return _regex_entities(raw)

========================================================================================
FILE: ml/common/similarity.py
========================================================================================
"""TF-IDF + cosine ranking and near-duplicate checks (shared)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections import Counter
from math import log
from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence

import numpy as np

from ml.common.text import normalize, word_ngrams


def _tokens(text: str) -> list[str]:
    return normalize(text).split()


def _cosine(a: np.ndarray, b: np.ndarray) -> float:
    denom = float(np.linalg.norm(a) * np.linalg.norm(b))
    if denom == 0.0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.dot(a, b) / denom)


def _doc_text(doc: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    return str(doc.get("text") or doc.get("content_body") or doc.get("body") or doc.get("title") or "")


def _tfidf_matrix(
    query_tokens: Sequence[str],
    doc_tokens: Sequence[Sequence[str]],
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, list[np.ndarray]]:
    """Build smoothed TF-IDF vectors for query + docs (shared IDF over the corpus)."""
    corpus = [list(query_tokens), *[list(toks) for toks in doc_tokens]]
    vocab_terms = sorted({t for tokens in corpus for t in tokens})
    if not vocab_terms:
        empty = np.zeros(0, dtype=float)
        return empty, [empty for _ in doc_tokens]

    vocab = {term: i for i, term in enumerate(vocab_terms)}
    n_docs = len(doc_tokens)
    df = Counter()
    for tokens in doc_tokens:
        df.update(set(tokens))

    idf = np.zeros(len(vocab), dtype=float)
    for term, idx in vocab.items():
        # smoothed IDF: log((N+1)/(df+1)) + 1
        idf[idx] = log((n_docs + 1) / (df.get(term, 0) + 1)) + 1.0

    def tfidf_vec(tokens: Sequence[str]) -> np.ndarray:
        vec = np.zeros(len(vocab), dtype=float)
        if not tokens:
            return vec
        counts = Counter(tokens)
        length = float(len(tokens))
        for term, count in counts.items():
            idx = vocab.get(term)
            if idx is None:
                continue
            tf = count / length
            vec[idx] = tf * idf[idx]
        return vec

    q_vec = tfidf_vec(query_tokens)
    d_vecs = [tfidf_vec(toks) for toks in doc_tokens]
    return q_vec, d_vecs


def rank(
    query: str,
    documents: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    *,
    limit: int = 10,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Rank documents by TF-IDF + cosine similarity to the query.

    Each document may include id, title, text (or content_body / body).
    Empty corpus → [].
    """
    docs = list(documents or [])
    if not docs or not normalize(query):
        return []

    query_tokens = _tokens(query)
    doc_tokens = [_tokens(_doc_text(d)) for d in docs]
    q_vec, d_vecs = _tfidf_matrix(query_tokens, doc_tokens)
    if q_vec.size == 0:
        return []

    scored: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for doc, d_vec in zip(docs, d_vecs):
        score = _cosine(q_vec, d_vec)
        if score <= 0:
            continue
        scored.append(
            {
                "id": doc.get("id") or doc.get("kb_id") or doc.get("content_id") or doc.get("evidence_id"),
                "title": doc.get("title") or "",
                "score": round(score, 4),
                "source_tag": doc.get("source_tag") or doc.get("category") or "",
            }
        )

    scored.sort(key=lambda row: row["score"], reverse=True)
    return scored[: max(0, int(limit))]


def _minhash_signature(text: str, *, num_hashes: int = 32) -> set[int]:
    """Simple hash-shingle signature for near-duplicate detection."""
    shingles = word_ngrams(text, n=3) or _tokens(text)
    if not shingles:
        return set()
    sig: set[int] = set()
    for i in range(num_hashes):
        best = min(hash((i, s)) & 0xFFFFFFFF for s in shingles)
        sig.add(best)
    return sig


def _jaccard(a: set[int], b: set[int]) -> float:
    if not a and not b:
        return 0.0
    union = a | b
    if not union:
        return 0.0
    return len(a & b) / len(union)


def similar_pairs(
    documents: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    *,
    threshold: float = 0.5,
    limit: int = 20,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Find near-duplicate pairs using MinHash-style Jaccard on shingles."""
    docs = list(documents or [])
    if len(docs) < 2:
        return []

    sigs = [_minhash_signature(_doc_text(d)) for d in docs]
    pairs: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for i in range(len(docs)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(docs)):
            score = _jaccard(sigs[i], sigs[j])
            if score < threshold:
                continue
            pairs.append(
                {
                    "left_id": docs[i].get("id") or docs[i].get("content_id"),
                    "right_id": docs[j].get("id") or docs[j].get("content_id"),
                    "left_title": docs[i].get("title") or "",
                    "right_title": docs[j].get("title") or "",
                    "score": round(score, 4),
                }
            )

    pairs.sort(key=lambda row: row["score"], reverse=True)
    return pairs[: max(0, int(limit))]

========================================================================================
FILE: ml/common/text.py
========================================================================================
"""Simple text cleanup and n-grams."""

from __future__ import annotations

import re

_WS = re.compile(r"\s+")
_NON_WORD = re.compile(r"[^\w\s]", re.UNICODE)


def normalize(text: str) -> str:
    """Lowercase, strip punctuation-ish noise, collapse spaces."""
    raw = "" if text is None else str(text)
    cleaned = _NON_WORD.sub(" ", raw.lower())
    return _WS.sub(" ", cleaned).strip()


def word_ngrams(text: str, n: int = 2) -> list[str]:
    """Return word n-grams from normalized text."""
    tokens = normalize(text).split()
    if n < 1 or len(tokens) < n:
        return tokens[:]
    return [" ".join(tokens[i : i + n]) for i in range(len(tokens) - n + 1)]

========================================================================================
FILE: ml/editor/queue_urgency.py
========================================================================================
"""Editor queue urgency model.

Tells the editor how urgent a story is for the review queue:
  urgent > high > medium > low

This is NOT the same as AIA "pitch importance" (that one is a simple formula).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from functools import lru_cache
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix, hstack

from utils.logger import get_logger

log = get_logger("ml.editor.queue_urgency")

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
MODEL_DIR = ROOT / "artifacts" / "models" / "editor_queue_urgency"
MODEL_PATHS = (
    MODEL_DIR / "model.joblib",
    # legacy flat path (pre-cleanup)
    ROOT / "artifacts" / "models" / "editor_queue_urgency.joblib",
)

# Model training labels → simple desk levels used in the app
LABEL_TO_URGENCY = {
    "very low": "low",
    "low": "low",
    "medium": "medium",
    "high": "high",
    "very high": "urgent",
}


@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
def _load_model() -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    path = next((p for p in MODEL_PATHS if p.exists()), None)
    if path is None:
        log.warning("Urgency model not found under artifacts/models/editor_queue_urgency/")
        return None
    try:
        import joblib

        bundle = joblib.load(path)
        if not {"model", "tfidf", "labels", "urg_regex"}.issubset(bundle):
            log.warning("Urgency model file is missing required keys: %s", path)
            return None
        log.info("Loaded editor queue urgency model from %s", path)
        return bundle
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        log.warning("Could not load urgency model: %s", exc)
        return None


def model_available() -> bool:
    return _load_model() is not None


def clear_cache() -> None:
    _load_model.cache_clear()


def _extra_features(texts: list[str], urg_regex: str) -> np.ndarray:
    """Simple extra signals: length, urgent words, digits, ALLCAPS runs."""
    rows = []
    urg = re.compile(urg_regex, re.I)
    digit = re.compile(r"\d")
    caps = re.compile(r"[A-Z]{4,}")
    for text in texts:
        raw = text or ""
        rows.append(
            [
                len(raw) / 500.0,
                len(urg.findall(raw)),
                1 if digit.search(raw) else 0,
                len(caps.findall(raw)),
            ]
        )
    return np.asarray(rows, dtype=float)


def predict_editor_queue_urgency(headline: str, body: str = "") -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Return how urgent this story is for the editor queue."""
    bundle = _load_model()
    text = f"{headline or ''}. {body or ''}".strip()

    if bundle is None:
        return {
            "urgency": "medium",
            "model_label": None,
            "confidence": None,
            "probabilities": {},
            "source": "fallback",
        }

    labels: list[str] = list(bundle["labels"])
    features = hstack(
        [
            bundle["tfidf"].transform([text]),
            csr_matrix(_extra_features([text], str(bundle["urg_regex"]))),
        ]
    ).tocsr()

    probs = bundle["model"].predict_proba(features)[0]
    best = int(np.argmax(probs))
    model_label = labels[best]

    return {
        "urgency": LABEL_TO_URGENCY.get(model_label, "medium"),
        "model_label": model_label,
        "confidence": round(float(probs[best]), 4),
        "probabilities": {label: round(float(p), 4) for label, p in zip(labels, probs)},
        "source": "model",
    }

from ml.knowledge.base import (

========================================================================================
FILE: ml/knowledge/base.py
========================================================================================
"""Journalist relevancy: TF-IDF + cosine over desk-published ``content`` only.

Corpus = rows in ``content`` with ``status = 'published'`` that were created by
the newsroom desk (not CSV history seeds). No history CSV push.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from ml.common import rank
from utils.logger import get_logger

log = get_logger("ml.knowledge")
SEED_PREFIX = "seed/"


def _drop_legacy_knowledge_base() -> None:
    from database.connection import db_cursor

    with db_cursor() as cur:
        cur.execute("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS knowledge_base CASCADE")
        # Clear old history-seed rows if any remain
        cur.execute("DELETE FROM content WHERE source_type LIKE %s", (f"{SEED_PREFIX}%",))


def cleanup_legacy_relevancy_sources() -> None:
    """One-shot cleanup: drop knowledge_base table + seed CSV rows."""
    _drop_legacy_knowledge_base()


def load_published_documents(*, limit: int = 2000) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Desk-published content only (excludes source_type seed/…)."""
    try:
        from database.connection import db_cursor, require_schema

        _drop_legacy_knowledge_base()
        require_schema()
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT
                    content_id AS id,
                    title,
                    COALESCE(content_body, raw_body, summary, '') AS text,
                    source_type AS category
                FROM content
                WHERE status = 'published'
                  AND (source_type IS NULL OR source_type NOT LIKE %s)
                ORDER BY content_id DESC
                LIMIT %s
                """,
                (f"{SEED_PREFIX}%", limit),
            )
            return [dict(r) for r in (cur.fetchall() or [])]
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        log.warning("published content load failed: %s", exc)
        return []


def score_content_relevancy(
    title: str,
    body: str = "",
    *,
    limit: int = 8,
    min_score: float = 0.08,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """TF-IDF + cosine against desk-published content only."""
    query = f"{title or ''}. {body or ''}".strip()
    docs = load_published_documents()
    hits = [h for h in rank(query, docs, limit=limit) if float(h.get("score") or 0) >= min_score]
    best = float(hits[0]["score"]) if hits else 0.0
    if best >= 0.35:
        label = "high"
    elif best >= 0.15:
        label = "medium"
    elif best > 0:
        label = "low"
    else:
        label = "none"
    size = len(docs)
    return {
        "relevancy": label,
        "best_score": round(best, 4),
        "matches": hits,
        "method": "tfidf_cosine",
        "corpus_size": size,
        "knowledge_base_size": size,
        "source": "content",
    }

"""Console model training: CSV → ingest+clean → ``ml_train_*`` → artifacts.

Newsroom ``content`` is never used for training. ML CSVs live under
``ml/editor/dataset`` and ``ml/adaptation/dataset``.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


def _ensure_schema() -> None:
    from database.connection import require_schema

    require_schema()


def push_urgency(csv_path: Path | str | None = None, *, force: bool = False) -> int:
    from ml.training.ingest_clean import push_all_new, push_urgency_file

    _ensure_schema()
    if csv_path is None:
        results = push_all_new("urgency", force=force)
        if not results:
            result = push_urgency_file(force=force)
            return int(result.get("rows") or 0)
        return sum(int(r.get("rows") or 0) for r in results)
    return int(push_urgency_file(csv_path, force=force).get("rows") or 0)


def push_adaptation(csv_path: Path | str | None = None, *, force: bool = False) -> int:
    from ml.training.ingest_clean import push_adaptation_file, push_all_new

    _ensure_schema()
    if csv_path is None:
        results = push_all_new("adaptation", force=force)
        if not results:
            result = push_adaptation_file(force=force)
            return int(result.get("rows") or 0)
        return sum(int(r.get("rows") or 0) for r in results)
    return int(push_adaptation_file(csv_path, force=force).get("rows") or 0)


def _load_urgency_from_db() -> pd.DataFrame:
    from database.connection import db_cursor

    with db_cursor() as cur:
        cur.execute("SELECT headline, body, source, priority FROM ml_train_urgency")
        rows = cur.fetchall() or []
    return pd.DataFrame([dict(r) for r in rows])


def _load_adaptation_from_db() -> pd.DataFrame:
    from database.connection import db_cursor

    with db_cursor() as cur:
        cur.execute(
            """
            SELECT article_id, original_title, original_content,
                   target_platform, correct_adapted_article
            FROM ml_train_adaptation
            """
        )
        rows = cur.fetchall() or []
    return pd.DataFrame([dict(r) for r in rows])


def train_urgency_from_db(*, fast: bool = False) -> dict:
    """Train urgency using cleaned rows from DB."""
    import re
    import sys
    import time

    import joblib
    import numpy as np
    from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix, hstack
    from sklearn.feature_extraction.text import TfidfVectorizer
    from sklearn.metrics import (
        accuracy_score,
        classification_report,
        confusion_matrix,
        f1_score,
    )
    from sklearn.model_selection import RandomizedSearchCV, StratifiedKFold, train_test_split
    from xgboost import XGBClassifier

    from utils.progress import progress

    LABELS = ["very low", "low", "medium", "high", "very high"]
    URG_WORDS = (
        r"urgent|breaking|critical|immediate|emergency|severe|ban|sanction|"
        r"recall|bankrupt|attack|fire|halt|shutdown|breach"
    )
    SEED = 42

    def _step(msg: str) -> None:
        progress(msg)
        sys.stdout.flush()

    df = _load_urgency_from_db()
    if df.empty:
        _step("Urgency table empty — pushing detected CSVs (ingest+clean)...")
        push_urgency()
        df = _load_urgency_from_db()
    if df.empty:
        raise RuntimeError("No urgency training rows after push.")

    _step(f"1/6 Loaded {len(df)} cleaned rows from ml_train_urgency")
    texts = (df["headline"].fillna("") + ". " + df["body"].fillna("")).to_numpy(dtype=object)
    y = df["priority"].map({label: i for i, label in enumerate(LABELS)}).to_numpy(dtype=int)
    texts_train, texts_test, y_train, y_test = train_test_split(
        texts, y, test_size=0.2, stratify=y, random_state=SEED
    )
    _step(f"2/6 Split train/test — train={len(y_train)} test={len(y_test)}")

    def numeric_features(series_texts):
        s = pd.Series(list(series_texts))
        return np.c_[
            s.str.len().values / 500.0,
            s.str.count(URG_WORDS, flags=re.I).values,
            s.str.contains(r"\d").astype(int).values,
            s.str.count(r"[A-Z]{4,}").values,
        ]

    def featurize(tfidf, series_texts, *, fit=False):
        matrix = tfidf.fit_transform(series_texts) if fit else tfidf.transform(series_texts)
        return hstack([matrix, csr_matrix(numeric_features(series_texts))]).tocsr()

    _step("3/6 Building TF-IDF features (this can take ~10–30s)...")
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    tfidf = TfidfVectorizer(ngram_range=(1, 2), max_features=5000, min_df=2, sublinear_tf=True)
    X_train = featurize(tfidf, texts_train, fit=True)
    X_test = featurize(tfidf, texts_test)
    _step(f"   Features ready in {time.perf_counter() - t0:.1f}s — shape={X_train.shape}")

    base = XGBClassifier(
        objective="multi:softprob",
        num_class=5,
        tree_method="hist",
        eval_metric="mlogloss",
        random_state=SEED,
        n_jobs=-1,
    )
    if fast:
        _step("4/6 Fitting XGBoost (fast mode, no CV search)...")
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        model = base.set_params(n_estimators=200, max_depth=5, learning_rate=0.1)
        model.fit(X_train, y_train)
        _step(f"   Fit done in {time.perf_counter() - t0:.1f}s")
        print("Best params:", {"n_estimators": 200, "max_depth": 5, "learning_rate": 0.1})
        print("CV macro F1: (skipped — fast mode)")
    else:
        _step(
            "4/6 Hyperparameter search (8 trials × 3-fold CV) — "
            "often 2–10+ minutes; please wait..."
        )
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        search = RandomizedSearchCV(
            base,
            {
                "n_estimators": [200, 400],
                "max_depth": [3, 5, 7],
                "learning_rate": [0.05, 0.1, 0.2],
                "subsample": [0.8, 1.0],
                "colsample_bytree": [0.5, 0.8],
            },
            n_iter=8,
            scoring="f1_macro",
            cv=StratifiedKFold(3, shuffle=True, random_state=SEED),
            random_state=SEED,
            n_jobs=1,
            verbose=1,
        )
        search.fit(X_train, y_train)
        model = search.best_estimator_
        _step(f"   Search done in {time.perf_counter() - t0:.1f}s")
        print("Best params:", search.best_params_)
        print("CV macro F1:", round(float(search.best_score_), 4))

    _step("5/6 Evaluating on test set...")
    pred = model.predict(X_test)
    print("Accuracy:", round(float(accuracy_score(y_test, pred)), 4))
    print("Macro F1:", round(float(f1_score(y_test, pred, average="macro")), 4))
    print("Within 1 level:", round(float(np.mean(np.abs(pred - y_test) <= 1)), 4))
    print(classification_report(y_test, pred, target_names=LABELS, digits=3))
    print(pd.DataFrame(confusion_matrix(y_test, pred), index=LABELS, columns=LABELS))

    _step("6/6 Saving model artifact...")
    model_dir = ROOT / "artifacts" / "models" / "editor_queue_urgency"
    model_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    art = model_dir / "model.joblib"
    metrics_path = model_dir / "metrics.json"
    payload_metrics = {
        "accuracy": float(accuracy_score(y_test, pred)),
        "macro_f1": float(f1_score(y_test, pred, average="macro")),
        "n_train": int(len(y_train)),
        "n_test": int(len(y_test)),
    }
    joblib.dump(
        {
            "model": model,
            "model_name": "XGBoost",
            "tfidf": tfidf,
            "labels": LABELS,
            "urg_regex": URG_WORDS,
        },
        art,
    )
    metrics_path.write_text(json.dumps(payload_metrics, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    _step(f"Saved {art}")
    _step(f"Saved {metrics_path}")
    return payload_metrics


def train_cae_from_db() -> dict:
    import sys

    from ml.adaptation.training import train_all_from_dataframe
    from utils.progress import progress

    df = _load_adaptation_from_db()
    if df.empty:
        progress("CAE table empty — pushing detected CSVs (ingest+clean)...")
        sys.stdout.flush()
        push_adaptation()
        df = _load_adaptation_from_db()
    if df.empty:
        raise RuntimeError("No adaptation training rows after push.")
    progress(f"Training unified CAE from DB ({len(df)} rows) — building features + fit...")
    sys.stdout.flush()
    return train_all_from_dataframe(df)


def _cae_summary(cae_results: dict) -> dict:
    block = cae_results.get("content_adaptation") or cae_results
    metrics = (block.get("metrics") or {}).get("test") or {}
    per = block.get("per_platform") or {}
    out: dict = {
        "unified": {
            "test_f1": metrics.get("f1"),
            "test_accuracy": metrics.get("accuracy"),
        }
    }
    for platform, result in per.items():
        out[platform] = {
            "test_f1": result.get("f1"),
            "test_accuracy": result.get("accuracy"),
        }
    return out


def _write_train_summary(summary: dict) -> None:
    if not summary:
        return
    out = ROOT / "artifacts" / "models" / "training_summary.json"
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out.write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print()
    print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))
    print("wrote", out)


def train_all(*, fast: bool = False) -> dict:
    """Push (ingest+clean) urgency + CAE datasets; train urgency and CAE."""
    push_urgency()
    push_adaptation()
    summary: dict = {
        "editor_queue_urgency": train_urgency_from_db(fast=fast),
        "cae_adaptation": _cae_summary(train_cae_from_db()),
    }
    _write_train_summary(summary)
    return summary


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="NewsMind trainer (ML CSV → ingest+clean → DB → train)"
    )
    parser.add_argument("--all", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--urgency", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--cae", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--push-only", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--force", action="store_true", help="Re-push even if file hash unchanged")
    parser.add_argument("--file", default="", help="Specific CSV filename under the dataset folder")
    parser.add_argument("--fast", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    if not any([args.all, args.urgency, args.cae, args.push_only]):
        parser.print_help()
        raise SystemExit(1)

    file_arg = args.file or None

    if args.all:
        if file_arg:
            raise SystemExit("--file cannot be combined with --all")
        train_all(fast=args.fast)
        return

    summary: dict = {}
    if args.push_only or args.urgency:
        push_urgency(file_arg, force=args.force)
    if args.push_only or args.cae:
        push_adaptation(file_arg, force=args.force)

    if args.push_only:
        print("Push complete.")
        return

    if args.urgency:
        summary["editor_queue_urgency"] = train_urgency_from_db(fast=args.fast)
    if args.cae:
        summary["cae_adaptation"] = _cae_summary(train_cae_from_db())

    _write_train_summary(summary)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

========================================================================================
FILE: ml/training/ingest_clean.py
========================================================================================
"""Ingest + clean ML training CSVs before writing ``ml_train_*`` staging tables."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal

import pandas as pd

from cleaners.orchestrator import CleaningOrchestrator
from utils.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
URGENCY_DIR = ROOT / "ml" / "editor" / "dataset"
ADAPTATION_DIR = ROOT / "ml" / "adaptation" / "dataset"
DatasetKind = Literal["urgency", "adaptation"]


def list_dataset_csvs(kind: DatasetKind) -> list[str]:
    root = URGENCY_DIR if kind == "urgency" else ADAPTATION_DIR
    if not root.exists():
        return []
    return sorted(p.name for p in root.glob("*.csv") if p.is_file())


def resolve_dataset_csv(kind: DatasetKind, source_file: str | Path | None = None) -> Path:
    root = URGENCY_DIR if kind == "urgency" else ADAPTATION_DIR
    if source_file is None or str(source_file).strip() == "":
        defaults = {
            "urgency": root / "synthetic_news.csv",
            "adaptation": root / "newsmind_paired_examples.csv",
        }
        path = defaults[kind]
    else:
        name = Path(str(source_file)).name
        path = (root / name).resolve()
        if root.resolve() not in path.parents and path != root.resolve():
            raise ValidationError(f"CSV must be inside {root.as_posix()}")
    if not path.exists() or not path.is_file():
        raise NotFoundError(f"Training CSV not found: {path.name} (expected under {root})")
    return path


def file_hash(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.md5()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _already_pushed(cur, rel: str, digest: str) -> bool:
    cur.execute(
        "SELECT file_hash FROM ml_train_file WHERE source_file = %s",
        (rel,),
    )
    row = cur.fetchone()
    return bool(row and str(row.get("file_hash") or "") == digest)


def _upsert_file_row(cur, *, rel: str, digest: str, kind: str, row_count: int) -> None:
    cur.execute(
        """
        INSERT INTO ml_train_file (source_file, file_hash, dataset_kind, row_count, loaded_at)
        VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, NOW())
        ON CONFLICT (source_file) DO UPDATE
        SET file_hash = EXCLUDED.file_hash,
            dataset_kind = EXCLUDED.dataset_kind,
            row_count = EXCLUDED.row_count,
            loaded_at = NOW()
        """,
        (rel, digest, kind, row_count),
    )


def _read_csv_records(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    df = pd.read_csv(path)
    df.columns = [str(c).strip().lower() for c in df.columns]
    return df.to_dict(orient="records")


def _urgency_to_clean_rows(raw: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for i, row in enumerate(raw):
        headline = str(row.get("headline") or row.get("title") or "").strip()
        body = str(row.get("body") or row.get("text") or "").strip()
        priority = str(row.get("priority") or row.get("label") or "").strip().lower()
        source = str(row.get("source") or row.get("source_dataset") or "").strip()
        if not headline and not body:
            continue
        out.append(
            {
                "title": headline or body[:80],
                "text": body or headline,
                "content_body": body or headline,
                "headline": headline or body[:80],
                "body": body,
                "priority": priority,
                "source": source,
                "status": "draft",
                "content_kind": "news",
                "_row": i,
            }
        )
    return out


def _adaptation_to_clean_rows(raw: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for i, row in enumerate(raw):
        title = str(row.get("original_title") or row.get("title") or "").strip()
        body = str(row.get("original_content") or row.get("text") or row.get("body") or "").strip()
        adapted = str(row.get("correct_adapted_article") or "").strip()
        platform = str(row.get("target_platform") or "").strip()
        article_id = str(row.get("article_id") or f"row-{i}").strip()
        if not title or not body or not adapted or not platform:
            continue
        out.append(
            {
                "title": title,
                "text": body,
                "content_body": body,
                "article_id": article_id,
                "original_title": title,
                "original_content": body,
                "target_platform": platform,
                "correct_adapted_article": adapted,
                "status": "draft",
                "content_kind": "article",
                "_row": i,
            }
        )
    return out


def _clean_records(records: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    if not records:
        return []
    result = CleaningOrchestrator().clean_content(records, write_files=False)
    return list(result.get("kept") or [])


def push_urgency_file(csv_path: Path | str | None = None, *, force: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
    from database.connection import db_cursor, require_schema

    require_schema()
    path = resolve_dataset_csv("urgency", csv_path)
    rel = f"ml/editor/dataset/{path.name}"
    digest = file_hash(path)
    with db_cursor() as cur:
        if not force and _already_pushed(cur, rel, digest):
            cur.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM ml_train_urgency WHERE source_file = %s", (rel,))
            n = int((cur.fetchone() or {}).get("n") or 0)
            print(f"Skip urgency push (unchanged): {rel} ({n} rows)")
            return {"skipped": True, "source_file": rel, "rows": n}

        raw = _read_csv_records(path)
        cleaned = _clean_records(_urgency_to_clean_rows(raw))
        cur.execute("DELETE FROM ml_train_urgency WHERE source_file = %s", (rel,))
        kept = 0
        for row in cleaned:
            priority = str(row.get("priority") or "").strip().lower()
            if priority not in {"very low", "low", "medium", "high", "very high"}:
                continue
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO ml_train_urgency (source_file, headline, body, source, priority)
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                """,
                (
                    rel,
                    str(row.get("headline") or row.get("title") or ""),
                    str(row.get("body") or row.get("text") or ""),
                    str(row.get("source") or ""),
                    priority,
                ),
            )
            kept += 1
        _upsert_file_row(cur, rel=rel, digest=digest, kind="urgency", row_count=kept)
    print(f"Pushed {kept} cleaned urgency rows → ml_train_urgency ({rel})")
    return {"skipped": False, "source_file": rel, "rows": kept}


def push_adaptation_file(csv_path: Path | str | None = None, *, force: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
    from database.connection import db_cursor, require_schema

    require_schema()
    path = resolve_dataset_csv("adaptation", csv_path)
    rel = f"ml/adaptation/dataset/{path.name}"
    digest = file_hash(path)
    with db_cursor() as cur:
        if not force and _already_pushed(cur, rel, digest):
            cur.execute(
                "SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM ml_train_adaptation WHERE source_file = %s",
                (rel,),
            )
            n = int((cur.fetchone() or {}).get("n") or 0)
            print(f"Skip adaptation push (unchanged): {rel} ({n} rows)")
            return {"skipped": True, "source_file": rel, "rows": n}

        raw = _read_csv_records(path)
        cleaned = _clean_records(_adaptation_to_clean_rows(raw))
        cur.execute("DELETE FROM ml_train_adaptation WHERE source_file = %s", (rel,))
        kept = 0
        for row in cleaned:
            cur.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO ml_train_adaptation
                    (source_file, article_id, original_title, original_content,
                     target_platform, correct_adapted_article)
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                ON CONFLICT (source_file, article_id, target_platform) DO UPDATE
                SET original_title = EXCLUDED.original_title,
                    original_content = EXCLUDED.original_content,
                    correct_adapted_article = EXCLUDED.correct_adapted_article,
                    loaded_at = NOW()
                """,
                (
                    rel,
                    str(row.get("article_id") or ""),
                    str(row.get("original_title") or row.get("title") or ""),
                    str(row.get("original_content") or row.get("text") or ""),
                    str(row.get("target_platform") or ""),
                    str(row.get("correct_adapted_article") or ""),
                ),
            )
            kept += 1
        _upsert_file_row(cur, rel=rel, digest=digest, kind="adaptation", row_count=kept)
    print(f"Pushed {kept} cleaned adaptation rows → ml_train_adaptation ({rel})")
    return {"skipped": False, "source_file": rel, "rows": kept}


def push_all_new(kind: DatasetKind, *, force: bool = False) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    results = []
    for name in list_dataset_csvs(kind):
        if kind == "urgency":
            results.append(push_urgency_file(name, force=force))
        else:
            results.append(push_adaptation_file(name, force=force))
    return results

  admin/            ingest → clean → persist
  content/          desk content operations
"""

from modules.admin.admin_module import AdminModule

========================================================================================
FILE: modules/admin/admin_module.py
========================================================================================
"""Admin / pipeline feature module."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Optional

from cleaners.orchestrator import CleaningOrchestrator
from config.constants import CAP_CLEAN, CAP_INGEST, CAP_PERSIST, CAP_PIPELINE
from config.runtime import pipeline_file_by_file
from services.access import require_capability_session
from services.ingestion_service import IngestionService
from services.persistence_service import PersistenceService
from services.report_service import ReportService
from utils.logger import get_logger
from utils.progress import progress

log = get_logger("admin")


class AdminModule:
    def __init__(
        self,
        ingestion: IngestionService | None = None,
        cleaning: CleaningOrchestrator | None = None,
        persistence: PersistenceService | None = None,
        reports: ReportService | None = None,
        session_token: str | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.ingestion = ingestion or IngestionService()
        self.cleaning = cleaning or CleaningOrchestrator()
        self.persistence = persistence or PersistenceService()
        self.reports = reports or ReportService()
        self.session_token = session_token

    def _cap(self, capability: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return require_capability_session(capability, self.session_token)

    def list_raw_ingest(self, limit: int = 20) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """ETL staging rows — admin pipeline only (not journalist desk)."""
        from services.content.content_service import ContentService

        user = self._cap(CAP_INGEST)
        content = ContentService()
        return {
            "actor": user["username"],
            "role": user.get("role_name") or user["role_code"],
            "items": content.list_raw_ingest(limit=limit),
            "counts": content.raw_ingest_counts(),
        }

    def list_csv_files(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._cap(CAP_INGEST)
        progress("Scanning data/raw for CSV files...")
        files = self.ingestion.list_csv_files()
        new_files = self.ingestion.list_new_or_changed_files()
        progress(
            f"Found {len(files)} CSV file(s); {len(new_files)} new or changed."
        )
        return {
            "actor": user["username"],
            "role": user.get("role_name") or user["role_code"],
            "all_files": files,
            "new_or_changed": new_files,
            "registered": self.ingestion.registered_files(),
        }

    def ingest(self, *, only_new: bool = False, store_raw: bool = True) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._cap(CAP_INGEST)
        mode = "new/changed only" if only_new else "all CSV files"
        progress(f"Ingest started ({mode})...")
        payload = self.ingestion.ingest_all_content(only_new=only_new)
        pitches = self.ingestion.ingest_pitches() if not only_new else []
        raw_ids: list[int] = []
        if store_raw and payload["records"]:
            progress(f"Storing {len(payload['records'])} raw row(s) in database...")
            raw_ids = self.persistence.persist_raw_records(
                payload["records"], cleaning_status="pending"
            )
            progress(f"Raw ingest stored: {len(raw_ids)} row(s).")
        progress(
            f"Ingest finished: {len(payload['records'])} content row(s), "
            f"{len(pitches)} pitch row(s), {len(payload.get('source_files') or [])} file(s)."
        )
        return {
            "actor": user["username"],
            "role": user.get("role_name") or user["role_code"],
            "content_rows": len(payload["records"]),
            "pitch_rows": len(pitches),
            "source_files": payload.get("source_files") or [],
            "collections": payload["collections"],
            "raw_ingest_ids": raw_ids,
            "message": payload.get("message"),
            "records": payload["records"],
            "pitches": pitches,
        }

    def ingest_file(self, source_file: str, *, store_raw: bool = True) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._cap(CAP_INGEST)
        progress(f"Ingesting file: {source_file}")
        payload = self.ingestion.ingest_one(source_file)
        raw_ids: list[int] = []
        if store_raw and payload["records"]:
            progress(f"Storing {len(payload['records'])} raw row(s) in database...")
            raw_ids = self.persistence.persist_raw_records(
                payload["records"], cleaning_status="pending"
            )
            progress(f"Raw ingest stored: {len(raw_ids)} row(s).")
        progress(
            f"File ingest finished: {source_file} → "
            f"{payload.get('usable_row_count', len(payload['records']))} usable row(s)."
        )
        return {
            "actor": user["username"],
            "role": user.get("role_name") or user["role_code"],
            "content_rows": len(payload["records"]),
            "pitch_rows": 0,
            "source_files": payload["source_files"],
            "collections": payload["collections"],
            "raw_ingest_ids": raw_ids,
            "records": payload["records"],
            "pitches": [],
        }

    def clean(
        self,
        records: Optional[list[dict[str, Any]]] = None,
        pitches: Optional[list[dict[str, Any]]] = None,
        *,
        source_files: list[str] | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._cap(CAP_CLEAN)
        if records is None:
            progress("Loading CSV data for cleaning...")
            loaded = self.ingestion.ingest_all_content(only_new=False)
            records = loaded["records"]
            source_files = source_files or loaded.get("source_files")
        if pitches is None:
            pitches = self.ingestion.ingest_pitches()

        progress(f"Cleaning {len(records)} content row(s)...")
        content_result = self.cleaning.clean_content(records)
        progress(
            f"Content clean done: kept={content_result['stats'].get('kept')} "
            f"rejected={content_result['stats'].get('rejected')}"
        )
        progress(f"Cleaning {len(pitches)} pitch row(s)...")
        pitch_result = self.cleaning.clean_pitches(pitches)
        progress(
            f"Pitch clean done: kept={pitch_result['stats'].get('kept')} "
            f"rejected={pitch_result['stats'].get('rejected')}"
        )
        progress("Saving cleaning report to database...")
        report_id = self.reports.save_cleaning_report(
            report_type="content",
            result=content_result,
            source_files=source_files or [],
            created_by=user.get("user_id"),
        )
        pitch_report_id = None
        if pitches:
            pitch_report_id = self.reports.save_cleaning_report(
                report_type="pitch",
                result=pitch_result,
                source_files=source_files or ["pitches.csv"],
                created_by=user.get("user_id"),
            )
        progress(f"Cleaning report saved (id={report_id}).")
        return {
            "actor": user["username"],
            "role": user.get("role_name") or user["role_code"],
            "content_stats": content_result["stats"],
            "pitch_stats": pitch_result["stats"],
            "content_report": content_result.get("report_path"),
            "cleaned_csv": content_result.get("cleaned_csv"),
            "cleaning_report_id": report_id,
            "pitch_report_id": pitch_report_id,
            "content": content_result,
            "pitches": pitch_result,
        }

    def ingest_and_clean(self, *, only_new: bool = False, store_raw: bool = True) -> dict[str, Any]:
        self._cap(CAP_CLEAN)
        loaded = self.ingest(only_new=only_new, store_raw=store_raw)
        cleaned = self.clean(
            records=loaded["records"],
            pitches=loaded["pitches"],
            source_files=loaded.get("source_files"),
        )
        return {
            "actor": cleaned["actor"],
            "role": cleaned["role"],
            "ingest": {
                "content_rows": loaded["content_rows"],
                "pitch_rows": loaded["pitch_rows"],
                "source_files": loaded["source_files"],
                "collections": loaded["collections"],
                "raw_stored": len(loaded.get("raw_ingest_ids") or []),
                "message": loaded.get("message"),
            },
            "content_stats": cleaned["content_stats"],
            "pitch_stats": cleaned["pitch_stats"],
            "content_report": cleaned["content_report"],
            "cleaned_csv": cleaned["cleaned_csv"],
            "cleaning_report_id": cleaned["cleaning_report_id"],
            "content": cleaned["content"],
            "pitches": cleaned["pitches"],
        }

    def pipeline_file(self, source_file: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        self._cap(CAP_PIPELINE)
        progress(f"Pipeline for file: {source_file}")
        loaded = self.ingest_file(source_file, store_raw=True)
        cleaned = self.clean(
            records=loaded["records"],
            pitches=[],
            source_files=loaded["source_files"],
        )
        persisted = self.persist(cleaned)
        progress(
            f"Pipeline finished for {source_file}: "
            f"persisted={persisted['persisted_content']} "
            f"rejected_raw={persisted['rejected_raw_stored']}"
        )
        return {
            "actor": persisted["actor"],
            "role": persisted["role"],
            "source_files": loaded["source_files"],
            "ingest": {
                "content_rows": loaded["content_rows"],
                "source_files": loaded["source_files"],
                "collections": loaded["collections"],
            },
            "content_stats": cleaned["content_stats"],
            "cleaning_report_id": cleaned["cleaning_report_id"],
            "persisted_content": persisted["persisted_content"],
            "rejected_raw_stored": persisted["rejected_raw_stored"],
        }

    def persist(self, cleaned: Optional[dict[str, Any]] = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._cap(CAP_PERSIST)
        if cleaned is None:
            cleaned = self.ingest_and_clean(store_raw=True)
        kept = cleaned["content"]["kept"]
        rejected = cleaned["content"]["rejected"]
        pitches_kept = cleaned["pitches"]["kept"]
        progress(f"Persisting {len(kept)} cleaned content row(s)...")
        content_ids = self.persistence.persist_content(kept)
        progress(f"Persisting {len(rejected)} rejected row(s) to raw_ingest...")
        rejected_ids = self.persistence.persist_rejected(rejected)
        progress(f"Persisting {len(pitches_kept)} pitch row(s)...")
        pitch_ids = self.persistence.persist_pitches(pitches_kept)
        progress(
            f"Persist finished: content={len(content_ids)} "
            f"pitches={len(pitch_ids)} rejected_raw={len(rejected_ids)}"
        )
        return {
            "actor": user["username"],
            "role": user.get("role_name") or user["role_code"],
            "content_ids": content_ids,
            "pitch_ids": pitch_ids,
            "persisted_content": len(content_ids),
            "persisted_pitches": len(pitch_ids),
            "rejected_raw_stored": len(rejected_ids),
            "cleaning_report_id": cleaned.get("cleaning_report_id"),
        }

    def run_pipeline(self, *, only_new: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Full ETL. By default processes one CSV at a time with console progress."""
        self._cap(CAP_PIPELINE)
        if pipeline_file_by_file():
            files = (
                self.ingestion.list_new_or_changed_files()
                if only_new
                else self.ingestion.list_csv_files()
            )
            if only_new and not files:
                user = self._cap(CAP_PIPELINE)
                progress("No new or changed CSV files to process.")
                return {
                    "actor": user["username"],
                    "role": user.get("role_name") or user["role_code"],
                    "ingest": {
                        "content_rows": 0,
                        "pitch_rows": 0,
                        "source_files": [],
                        "message": "No new or changed CSV files to ingest.",
                    },
                    "content_stats": {"input": 0, "kept": 0, "rejected": 0},
                    "pitch_stats": {"input": 0, "kept": 0, "rejected": 0},
                    "quality_report": "",
                    "cleaning_report_id": None,
                    "persisted_content": 0,
                    "persisted_pitches": 0,
                    "rejected_raw_stored": 0,
                    "persisted_content_ids": [],
                    "persisted_pitch_ids": [],
                    "message": "No new or changed CSV files to ingest.",
                }
            totals = {
                "content_rows": 0,
                "kept": 0,
                "rejected": 0,
                "persisted_content": 0,
                "rejected_raw_stored": 0,
                "persisted_pitches": 0,
                "source_files": [],
                "content_ids": [],
                "pitch_ids": [],
                "cleaning_report_id": None,
            }
            last_actor = ""
            last_role = ""
            total_files = len(files)
            progress(f"Full pipeline starting for {total_files} file(s) (one file at a time)...")
            for index, rel in enumerate(files, start=1):
                progress(f"--- File {index}/{total_files}: {rel} ---")
                one = self.pipeline_file(rel)
                last_actor = one.get("actor") or last_actor
                last_role = one.get("role") or last_role
                totals["source_files"].append(rel)
                totals["content_rows"] += int((one.get("ingest") or {}).get("content_rows") or 0)
                stats = one.get("content_stats") or {}
                totals["kept"] += int(stats.get("kept") or 0)
                totals["rejected"] += int(stats.get("rejected") or 0)
                totals["persisted_content"] += int(one.get("persisted_content") or 0)
                totals["rejected_raw_stored"] += int(one.get("rejected_raw_stored") or 0)
                totals["cleaning_report_id"] = one.get("cleaning_report_id") or totals["cleaning_report_id"]
            pitches = self.ingestion.ingest_pitches() if not only_new else []
            pitch_ids: list[int] = []
            pitch_stats = {"input": 0, "kept": 0, "rejected": 0}
            if pitches:
                progress(f"Processing pitches.csv ({len(pitches)} row(s))...")
                pitch_clean = self.cleaning.clean_pitches(pitches)
                pitch_stats = pitch_clean["stats"]
                pitch_ids = self.persistence.persist_pitches(pitch_clean["kept"])
                totals["persisted_pitches"] = len(pitch_ids)
                progress(f"Pitches persisted: {len(pitch_ids)}")
            progress(
                f"Full pipeline complete: files={len(totals['source_files'])} "
                f"persisted_content={totals['persisted_content']} "
                f"rejected_raw={totals['rejected_raw_stored']}"
            )
            return {
                "actor": last_actor,
                "role": last_role,
                "ingest": {
                    "content_rows": totals["content_rows"],
                    "pitch_rows": len(pitches),
                    "source_files": totals["source_files"],
                    "message": f"Processed {len(totals['source_files'])} file(s) one at a time.",
                },
                "content_stats": {
                    "input": totals["content_rows"],
                    "kept": totals["kept"],
                    "rejected": totals["rejected"],
                },
                "pitch_stats": pitch_stats,
                "quality_report": "",
                "cleaning_report_id": totals["cleaning_report_id"],
                "persisted_content": totals["persisted_content"],
                "persisted_pitches": totals["persisted_pitches"],
                "rejected_raw_stored": totals["rejected_raw_stored"],
                "persisted_content_ids": totals["content_ids"],
                "persisted_pitch_ids": pitch_ids,
            }

        progress("Full pipeline started (combined mode)...")
        cleaned = self.ingest_and_clean(only_new=only_new, store_raw=True)
        persisted = self.persist(cleaned)
        progress("Full pipeline complete.")
        return {
            "actor": persisted["actor"],
            "role": persisted["role"],
            "ingest": cleaned["ingest"],
            "content_stats": cleaned["content_stats"],
            "pitch_stats": cleaned["pitch_stats"],
            "quality_report": cleaned["content_report"],
            "cleaning_report_id": cleaned.get("cleaning_report_id"),
            "persisted_content": persisted["persisted_content"],
            "persisted_pitches": persisted["persisted_pitches"],
            "rejected_raw_stored": persisted["rejected_raw_stored"],
            "persisted_content_ids": persisted["content_ids"],
            "persisted_pitch_ids": persisted["pitch_ids"],
        }

    def list_reports(self, limit: int = 15) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._cap(CAP_CLEAN)
        rows = self.reports.list_recent(limit=limit)
        return {
            "actor": user["username"],
            "role": user.get("role_name") or user["role_code"],
            "reports": rows,
        }

    def view_report(self, report_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._cap(CAP_CLEAN)
        row = self.reports.get(report_id)
        return {
            "actor": user["username"],
            "role": user.get("role_name") or user["role_code"],
            "report": row,
        }

========================================================================================
FILE: modules/authentication/auth_service.py
========================================================================================
"""Login / logout / JWT resolve.

Login: **username** + password (email on employee only).
Access tokens are signed JWTs (HS256). User/role are re-loaded from DB on each request.
Logout uses an in-memory jti denylist (process-local) plus clears the CLI token file.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Optional

import jwt

from dao.user_dao import UserDAO
from modules.authentication.security import (
    create_access_token,
    decode_access_token,
    hash_password,
    verify_password,
)
from repository.user_repository import UserRepository
from services.permission_service import capabilities_for_role
from utils.errors import ValidationError
from utils.helpers import project_path
from utils.validators import require_password, require_username

SESSION_FILE = project_path("logs", ".session_token")

# jti values revoked before natural JWT expiry (logout). Process-local.
_REVOKED_JTI: dict[str, datetime] = {}


class AuthenticationError(Exception):
    pass


def _purge_expired_revocations() -> None:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    expired = [jti for jti, exp in _REVOKED_JTI.items() if exp <= now]
    for jti in expired:
        _REVOKED_JTI.pop(jti, None)


def _revoke_jti(jti: str, expires_at: datetime) -> None:
    if expires_at.tzinfo is None:
        expires_at = expires_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    _REVOKED_JTI[jti] = expires_at


def _is_revoked(jti: str) -> bool:
    _purge_expired_revocations()
    exp = _REVOKED_JTI.get(jti)
    if exp is None:
        return False
    if exp <= datetime.now(timezone.utc):
        _REVOKED_JTI.pop(jti, None)
        return False
    return True


def _save_cli_token(user_id: int, token: str, expires_at: datetime) -> None:
    """CLI convenience: last login JWT so resolve_session() works without Bearer."""
    SESSION_FILE.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    SESSION_FILE.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "user_id": user_id,
                "token": token,
                "expires_at": expires_at.isoformat(),
            }
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )


def _load_cli_token() -> Optional[str]:
    if not SESSION_FILE.exists():
        return None
    try:
        data = json.loads(SESSION_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):
        return None
    token = str(data.get("token") or "").strip()
    return token or None


def _clear_cli_token() -> None:
    if SESSION_FILE.exists():
        SESSION_FILE.unlink()


def _user_payload(user: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    role_code = str(user.get("role_code") or "")
    profiles = UserRepository().get_profile_ids(int(user["user_id"]))
    return {
        "user_id": int(user["user_id"]),
        "username": user["username"],
        "email": user.get("email"),
        "full_name": f"{user.get('first_name', '')} {user.get('last_name', '')}".strip(),
        "role_code": role_code,
        "role_name": user.get("role_name"),
        "capabilities": list(capabilities_for_role(role_code)),
        "profiles": profiles,
    }


def authenticate(
    username: str,
    password: str,
    *,
    ip_address: Optional[str] = None,
    user_agent: Optional[str] = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    del ip_address, user_agent  # reserved for future audit
    username = (username or "").strip()
    if not username or not password:
        raise AuthenticationError("Username and password are required.")

    users = UserDAO()
    user = users.read_by_username(username)
    if user is None:
        raise AuthenticationError("Invalid username or password.")

    if user.get("account_status") != "active":
        raise AuthenticationError("This account is inactive.")

    if not verify_password(password, user["password_hash"]):
        raise AuthenticationError("Invalid username or password.")

    users.record_login_success(int(user["user_id"]))
    token = create_access_token(
        user_id=int(user["user_id"]),
        username=str(user["username"]),
        role_code=str(user.get("role_code") or ""),
    )
    claims = decode_access_token(token)
    exp = claims.get("exp")
    if isinstance(exp, (int, float)):
        expires_at = datetime.fromtimestamp(exp, tz=timezone.utc)
    else:
        expires_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    _save_cli_token(int(user["user_id"]), token, expires_at)

    payload = _user_payload(user)
    payload["session_token"] = token  # JWT; field name kept for API compatibility
    return payload


def resolve_session(session_token: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    token = (session_token or "").strip() or (_load_cli_token() or "")
    if not token:
        raise AuthenticationError("Invalid or expired session.")

    try:
        claims = decode_access_token(token)
    except jwt.ExpiredSignatureError as exc:
        raise AuthenticationError("Invalid or expired session.") from exc
    except jwt.PyJWTError as exc:
        raise AuthenticationError("Invalid or expired session.") from exc

    jti = str(claims.get("jti") or "")
    if jti and _is_revoked(jti):
        raise AuthenticationError("Invalid or expired session.")

    user_id = claims.get("user_id") or claims.get("sub")
    try:
        user_id_int = int(user_id)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise AuthenticationError("Invalid or expired session.") from exc

    user = UserDAO().read_by_id(user_id_int)
    if not user or user.get("account_status") != "active":
        raise AuthenticationError("Invalid or expired session.")
    return _user_payload(user)


def logout(session_token: str | None = None) -> None:
    token = (session_token or "").strip()
    if token:
        try:
            claims = decode_access_token(token)
            jti = str(claims.get("jti") or "")
            exp = claims.get("exp")
            if jti and isinstance(exp, (int, float)):
                _revoke_jti(jti, datetime.fromtimestamp(exp, tz=timezone.utc))
        except jwt.PyJWTError:
            pass
        file_token = _load_cli_token()
        if file_token and file_token == token:
            _clear_cli_token()
        return

    # Console logout with no explicit token: revoke CLI JWT if present, clear file.
    file_token = _load_cli_token()
    if file_token:
        try:
            claims = decode_access_token(file_token)
            jti = str(claims.get("jti") or "")
            exp = claims.get("exp")
            if jti and isinstance(exp, (int, float)):
                _revoke_jti(jti, datetime.fromtimestamp(exp, tz=timezone.utc))
        except jwt.PyJWTError:
            pass
    _clear_cli_token()


def change_password(user_id: int, current_password: str, new_password: str) -> None:
    """Logged-in user changes their own password (must know current password)."""
    if not current_password:
        raise ValidationError("Current password cannot be empty.")
    new_password = require_password(new_password)
    if current_password == new_password:
        raise ValidationError("New password must be different from the current password.")

    users = UserDAO()
    user = users.require(int(user_id))
    if not verify_password(current_password, user["password_hash"]):
        raise AuthenticationError("Current password is incorrect.")
    users.update_password_hash(int(user["user_id"]), hash_password(new_password))


def admin_reset_password(username: str, new_password: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Administrator sets a new password for any user (no old password required)."""
    username = require_username(username)
    new_password = require_password(new_password)
    users = UserDAO()
    user = users.read_by_username(username)
    if user is None:
        raise AuthenticationError(f"User '{username}' was not found.")
    users.update_password_hash(int(user["user_id"]), hash_password(new_password))
    return {"user_id": int(user["user_id"]), "username": user["username"]}

========================================================================================
FILE: modules/authentication/security.py
========================================================================================
"""Password hashing (stdlib) + JWT access tokens (PyJWT)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import hmac
import os
import secrets
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Any

import jwt

from config.constants import (
    JWT_ALGORITHM,
    PASSWORD_HASH_ITERATIONS,
    PASSWORD_SALT_BYTES,
    SESSION_TTL_HOURS,
    get_jwt_secret,
)


def hash_password(password: str, salt: bytes | None = None) -> str:
    """Return stored form: salt_hex$digest_hex (single ERD password_hash field)."""
    if not password:
        raise ValueError("Password must not be empty")
    salt_bytes = salt if salt is not None else os.urandom(PASSWORD_SALT_BYTES)
    digest = hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac(
        "sha256",
        password.encode("utf-8"),
        salt_bytes,
        PASSWORD_HASH_ITERATIONS,
    )
    return f"{salt_bytes.hex()}${digest.hex()}"


def verify_password(password: str, password_hash: str) -> bool:
    try:
        salt_hex, digest_hex = password_hash.split("$", 1)
        candidate = hash_password(password, salt=bytes.fromhex(salt_hex))
        return hmac.compare_digest(candidate, password_hash)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return False


def create_access_token(*, user_id: int, username: str, role_code: str = "") -> str:
    """Create a signed JWT access token (HS256)."""
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
        "sub": str(user_id),
        "user_id": int(user_id),
        "username": username,
        "role_code": role_code or "",
        "iat": now,
        "exp": now + timedelta(hours=SESSION_TTL_HOURS),
        "jti": secrets.token_urlsafe(16),
        "type": "access",
    }
    return jwt.encode(payload, get_jwt_secret(), algorithm=JWT_ALGORITHM)


def decode_access_token(token: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Decode and validate a JWT. Raises jwt.PyJWTError on failure."""
    return jwt.decode(
        token,
        get_jwt_secret(),
        algorithms=[JWT_ALGORITHM],
        options={"require": ["exp", "sub", "jti"]},
    )

"""Content desk operations (news/article/pitch/category/list/view)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from services.content import ContentService


class ContentModule:
    def __init__(self, service: ContentService | None = None) -> None:
        self.service = service or ContentService()

    def add_category(self, name: str, description: str = "") -> int:
        return self.service.add_category(name, description)

    def add_pitch(self, title: str, summary: str, status: str = "submitted") -> int:
        return self.service.add_pitch(title, summary, status=status)

    def add_news(self, **kwargs: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.service.add_news(**kwargs)

    def add_article(self, **kwargs: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.service.add_article(**kwargs)

    def list_content(self, limit: int = 10) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.service.list_content(limit=limit)

    def list_news(self, limit: int = 10) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.service.list_news(limit=limit)

    def list_raw(self, limit: int = 15) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.service.list_raw_ingest(limit=limit)

    def raw_counts(self) -> dict[str, int]:
        return self.service.raw_ingest_counts()

    def get_content(self, content_id: int | str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if isinstance(content_id, str):
            return self.service.get_content_by_input(content_id)
        return self.service.get_content(content_id)

    def update_status(self, content_id: int, status: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.service.update_content_status(content_id, status)

========================================================================================
FILE: modules/journalist/journalist_module.py
========================================================================================
"""Journalist tools façade — capability check, then call a service."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence

from config.constants import CAP_JOURNALIST_TOOLS
from dao.journalist_dao import JournalistDAO
from services.access import require_capability_session
from services.content import ContentService
from services.journalist import (
    assess_credibility,
    find_duplicates,
    validate_article,
)
from utils.errors import ValidationError


class JournalistModule:
    def __init__(
        self,
        session_token: str | None = None,
        content: ContentService | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.session_token = session_token
        self.content = content or ContentService()

    def _user(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return require_capability_session(CAP_JOURNALIST_TOOLS, self.session_token)

    def _author_id(self, user: dict[str, Any]) -> int | None:
        """content.author_id FK → journalist.journalist_id (not users.user_id)."""
        profiles = user.get("profiles") or {}
        journalist_id = profiles.get("journalist_id")
        if journalist_id:
            return int(journalist_id)
        return JournalistDAO().ensure_for_user(int(user["user_id"]))

    def duplicates(
        self,
        documents: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]] | None = None,
        *,
        threshold: float = 0.5,
        limit: int = 20,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        pairs = find_duplicates(documents, threshold=threshold, limit=limit)
        return {"actor": user["username"], "pairs": pairs}

    def validate(self, record: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        return {"actor": user["username"], **validate_article(record)}

    def credibility(self, content_id: int, *, limit: int = 5) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Evaluate a news/article using only its own attached evidence."""
        user = self._user()
        row = self.content.get_content(int(content_id))
        text = str(row.get("content_body") or row.get("raw_body") or row.get("summary") or "").strip()
        if not text:
            raise ValidationError("Content has no body to assess.")
        attached = self.content.list_evidence(int(content_id))
        if not attached:
            raise ValidationError("Attach evidence before credibility assessment.")
        evidence_docs = [
            {
                "id": item.get("evidence_id"),
                "title": item.get("title") or "",
                "text": item.get("body_text") or item.get("ocr_text") or "",
            }
            for item in attached
        ]
        report = assess_credibility(text, evidence_docs, limit=limit)
        return {
            "actor": user["username"],
            "content_id": int(content_id),
            "evidence_count": len(evidence_docs),
            **report,
        }

    # ── Desk content (parity with console journalist menu) ─────────────────

    def add_news(self, **kwargs: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        kwargs.setdefault("author_id", self._author_id(user))
        created = self.content.add_news(**kwargs)
        return {"actor": user["username"], **created}

    def add_article(self, **kwargs: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        kwargs.setdefault("author_id", self._author_id(user))
        created = self.content.add_article(**kwargs)
        return {"actor": user["username"], **created}

    def list_content(self, limit: int = 20, status: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        return {
            "actor": user["username"],
            "items": self.content.list_content(limit=limit, status=status),
        }

    def related_content(
        self,
        title: str = "",
        text: str = "",
        *,
        limit: int = 8,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        from ml.knowledge import score_content_relevancy

        user = self._user()
        result = score_content_relevancy(title, text, limit=limit)
        size = int(result.get("corpus_size") or result.get("knowledge_base_size") or 0)
        return {
            "actor": user["username"],
            "matches": result.get("matches") or [],
            "relevancy": result.get("relevancy") or "none",
            "best_score": float(result.get("best_score") or 0.0),
            "corpus_size": size,
            "knowledge_base_size": size,
            "source": result.get("source") or "content",
            "method": result.get("method") or "tfidf_cosine",
        }

    def get_content(self, content_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        return {"actor": user["username"], "content": self.content.get_content(content_id)}

    def list_news(self, limit: int = 20) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        return {"actor": user["username"], "items": self.content.list_news(limit=limit)}

    def add_evidence(self, **kwargs: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        if not kwargs.get("content_id"):
            raise ValidationError("content_id is required — evidence must attach to a news/article.")
        evidence_id = self.content.add_evidence(**kwargs)
        return {"actor": user["username"], "evidence_id": evidence_id}

    def list_evidence(self, content_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        items = self.content.list_evidence(content_id)
        return {"actor": user["username"], "content_id": content_id, "items": items}

    def update_content_status(self, content_id: int, status: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        user = self._user()
        updated = self.content.update_content_status(content_id, status)
        return {"actor": user["username"], **updated}

FILE: README.md
========================================================================================
# NewsMind

Console newsroom backend plus FastAPI role APIs (Swagger at `/docs`).

## Architecture

| Layer | Path | Responsibility |
|---|---|---|
| Console CLI | `app.py` | Role menus, human-readable output |
| FastAPI + Swagger | `api/` | Auth, journalist, AIA, CAE, publisher, editor, manager, admin |
| Feature modules | `modules/` | Auth, admin pipeline, content desk, journalist tools |
| Services | `services/` | Pipeline + desks |
| ML helpers | `ml/` | `common` + `aia` + `editor` + `adaptation` + `knowledge` + `training` |
| Cleaners | `cleaners/` | Shared ingest cleaning stages |
| DAO / Entities | `dao/`, `entities/` | Table access + domain objects |

## Data vs ML

| Location | Purpose |
|---|---|
| Location | Push target | Used for |
|---|---|---|
| `data/raw/` | **`content`** via admin ingest | Desk workflow |
| `ml/editor/dataset/*.csv` | **`ml_train_urgency`** | Urgency model training |
| `ml/adaptation/dataset/*.csv` | **`ml_train_adaptation`** | CAE model training |
| `artifacts/models/…` | (trained artifacts) | Inference |

**Relevancy:** TF-IDF + cosine over **desk-published** `content` only (after publisher sets `status=published`). No history CSV corpus.

**Evidence flow:** create news/article with evidence → validate → `POST /journalist/credibility` with `content_id`.

**Push commands:** console *Push urgency* / *Push CAE*, or `python -m ml.training --push-only`.

---

## Config

Copy `ENV.example` → `.env`.  
`config/db_config.py` + `config/runtime.py` load it automatically.

Auth uses **JWT** (HS256) access tokens returned as `session_token`.  
`NEWSROOM_JWT_SECRET` is **required** in `.env` (no hardcoded secret in code). Logout revokes the token `jti` in-process until expiry.

Database bootstrap is a single file: `database/newsmind.sql` (`python app.py --init-db`).

Temp desk/probe data (metro stories, evidence, pitches): `database/seed_temp_data.sql` — run after init-db:

```powershell
python -c "from pathlib import Path; from database.connection import apply_sql_file; apply_sql_file(Path('database/seed_temp_data.sql'))"
```

---

## Start

```powershell
cd "G:\NewsMind AI\NewsMindAI V2"
$env:PYTHONPATH = "G:\NewsMind AI\NewsMindAI V2"
python -m pip install -r requirements.txt
python app.py --init-db
python app.py
```

```powershell
$env:PYTHONPATH = "G:\NewsMind AI\NewsMindAI V2"
uvicorn api.main:app --reload
```

Open **http://127.0.0.1:8000/docs** or **http://127.0.0.1:8000/easy**

### Demo logins

| Username | Password |
|---|---|
| `admin` | `Admin@NewsMind2026!` |
| `journalist` | `Journalist@NewsMind2026!` |
| `editor` | `Editor@NewsMind2026!` |
| `publisher` | `Publisher@NewsMind2026!` |
| `manager` | `Manager@NewsMind2026!` |
| `analyst` | `Analyst@NewsMind2026!` |
| `adapter` | `Adapter@NewsMind2026!` |

### ML training

```powershell
python -m ml.training --push-only
python -m ml.training --all --fast
```

Admin console **ML TRAINING** auto-detects every `*.csv` under the dataset folders, pushes new/changed files (hash skip), then trains.

"""Content-side repository: content, article, news, evidence, pitches, raw ingest."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from dao.article_dao import ArticleDAO
from dao.content_dao import ContentDAO
from dao.evidence_dao import EvidenceDAO
from dao.news_dao import NewsDAO
from dao.raw_ingest_dao import RawIngestDAO
from dao.story_pitch_dao import StoryPitchDAO
from entities.content.article import Article
from entities.content.content import Content
from entities.content.evidence import Evidence
from entities.content.news import News
from entities.content.story_pitch import StoryPitch


class ContentRepository:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.content = ContentDAO()
        self.articles = ArticleDAO()
        self.news = NewsDAO()
        self.evidence = EvidenceDAO()
        self.pitches = StoryPitchDAO()
        self.raw = RawIngestDAO()

    def save_pitch(self, pitch: StoryPitch) -> int:
        return self.pitches.create(pitch)

    def save_content_bundle(self, content: Content, *, event_location: str = "") -> int:
        content_id = self.content.create(content)
        if (content.content_kind or "article") == "news":
            self.news.create(
                News(
                    content_id=content_id,
                    event_title=content.title,
                    event_location=event_location or None,
                )
            )
        else:
            self.articles.create(Article(content_id=content_id, article_type="feature"))
        return content_id

    def save_evidence(self, evidence: Evidence) -> int:
        return self.evidence.create(evidence)

    def get_content(self, content_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.content.require(content_id)

        self.journalists = self.user_repo.journalists

        self.content = self.content_repo.content
        self.articles = self.content_repo.articles
        self.news = self.content_repo.news
        self.evidence = self.content_repo.evidence
