"""Newsroom manager — ops overview, bottlenecks, business analytics."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from dao.content_dao import ContentDAO
from dao.publication_dao import PublicationDAO
from dao.story_pitch_dao import StoryPitchDAO
from database.connection import db_cursor
from services.editor.editor_service import EditorService


class ManagerService:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.editor = EditorService()
        self.pitches = StoryPitchDAO()
        self.content = ContentDAO()
        self.publications = PublicationDAO()

    def overview(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        submitted_pitches = self.pitches.list_by_status("submitted", limit=500)
        submitted_content = self.content.list_by_status("submitted", limit=500)
        approved_content = self.content.list_by_status("approved", limit=500)
        analytics = self.publications.analytics()
        return {
            "submitted_pitches": len(submitted_pitches),
            "submitted_content": len(submitted_content),
            "approved_content": len(approved_content),
            "publication": analytics,
        }

    def list_pitches(self, status: str = "submitted", limit: int = 100) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.editor.list_pitches(status=status, limit=limit)

    def list_content(self, status: str = "submitted", limit: int = 100) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.editor.list_content(status=status, limit=limit)

    def review_queue(
        self,
        *,
        pitch_status: str = "submitted",
        content_status: str = "submitted",
        limit: int = 100,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.editor.review_queue(
            pitch_status=pitch_status,
            content_status=content_status,
            limit=limit,
        )

    def approve_pitch(self, story_pitch_id: int, reviewer_id: int | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.editor.approve_pitch(story_pitch_id, reviewer_id)

    def reject_pitch(self, story_pitch_id: int, remarks: str = "") -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.editor.reject_pitch(story_pitch_id, remarks=remarks)

    def approve_content(self, content_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.editor.approve_content(content_id)

    def reject_content(self, content_id: int, remarks: str = "") -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.editor.reject_content(content_id, remarks=remarks)

    def assign_content(
        self,
        content_id: int,
        assignee_user_id: int,
        note: str = "",
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.editor.assign_content(content_id, assignee_user_id, note=note)

    def bottlenecks(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Where work is piling up across the newsroom pipeline."""
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT status AS stage,
                       COUNT(*) AS item_count,
                       ROUND(AVG(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (NOW() - updated_at)) / 3600.0)::numeric, 2)
                         AS avg_hours_in_stage
                FROM content
                WHERE status NOT IN ('published', 'draft')
                GROUP BY status
                ORDER BY item_count DESC
                """
            )
            content_stages = [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall() or []]

            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT status AS stage,
                       COUNT(*) AS item_count
                FROM story_pitch
                GROUP BY status
                ORDER BY item_count DESC
                """
            )
            pitch_stages = [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall() or []]

            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT status AS stage,
                       COUNT(*) AS item_count
                FROM publication
                GROUP BY status
                ORDER BY item_count DESC
                """
            )
            publication_stages = [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall() or []]

            try:
                cur.execute(
                    """
                    SELECT status AS stage,
                           COUNT(*) AS item_count
                    FROM adaptation_drafts
                    GROUP BY status
                    ORDER BY item_count DESC
                    """
                )
                adaptation_stages = [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall() or []]
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — table may be missing in older DBs
                adaptation_stages = []

        hotspots = []
        for row in content_stages:
            if str(row.get("stage")) in {"submitted", "rejected"} and int(row.get("item_count") or 0) > 0:
                hotspots.append(
                    {
                        "area": "content",
                        "stage": row["stage"],
                        "item_count": int(row["item_count"]),
                        "avg_hours_in_stage": float(row.get("avg_hours_in_stage") or 0),
                        "note": "Editorial backlog — prioritize with /manager/queue",
                    }
                )
        for row in pitch_stages:
            if str(row.get("stage")) == "submitted" and int(row.get("item_count") or 0) > 0:
                hotspots.append(
                    {
                        "area": "story_pitch",
                        "stage": row["stage"],
                        "item_count": int(row["item_count"]),
                        "note": "Pitch review backlog",
                    }
                )

        return {
            "content_stages": content_stages,
            "pitch_stages": pitch_stages,
            "publication_stages": publication_stages,
            "adaptation_stages": adaptation_stages,
            "hotspots": hotspots,
        }

    def business_analytics(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Whole-application business snapshot for the newsroom manager."""
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute("SELECT status, COUNT(*) AS count FROM content GROUP BY status")
            content_by_status = {str(r["status"]): int(r["count"]) for r in cur.fetchall() or []}

            cur.execute("SELECT content_kind, COUNT(*) AS count FROM content GROUP BY content_kind")
            content_by_kind = {str(r["content_kind"]): int(r["count"]) for r in cur.fetchall() or []}

            cur.execute("SELECT status, COUNT(*) AS count FROM story_pitch GROUP BY status")
            pitches_by_status = {str(r["status"]): int(r["count"]) for r in cur.fetchall() or []}

            cur.execute("SELECT priority, COUNT(*) AS count FROM story_pitch GROUP BY priority")
            pitches_by_priority = {str(r["priority"]): int(r["count"]) for r in cur.fetchall() or []}

            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT cleaning_status, COUNT(*) AS count
                FROM raw_ingest
                GROUP BY cleaning_status
                """
            )
            ingest_by_status = {str(r["cleaning_status"]): int(r["count"]) for r in cur.fetchall() or []}

            cur.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM users WHERE account_status = 'active'")
            active_users = int(cur.fetchone()["count"])

            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT r.role_code, COUNT(u.user_id) AS count
                FROM role r
                LEFT JOIN users u ON u.role_id = r.role_id AND u.account_status = 'active'
                GROUP BY r.role_code
                ORDER BY r.role_code
                """
            )
            users_by_role = {str(r["role_code"]): int(r["count"]) for r in cur.fetchall() or []}

            cur.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM evidence")
            evidence_count = int(cur.fetchone()["count"])

            cur.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM ingest_file")
            ingest_files = int(cur.fetchone()["count"])

        publication = self.publications.analytics()
        bottlenecks = self.bottlenecks()
        workload = self.workload()
        deadline_risk = self.deadline_risk()
        resources = self.resources()

        return {
            "pipeline": {
                "raw_ingest": ingest_by_status,
                "content": content_by_status,
                "content_kinds": content_by_kind,
                "story_pitches": pitches_by_status,
                "pitch_priority": pitches_by_priority,
                "publication": publication,
            },
            "ops": {
                "active_users": active_users,
                "users_by_role": users_by_role,
                "evidence_items": evidence_count,
                "ingest_files": ingest_files,
            },
            "bottleneck_hotspots": bottlenecks.get("hotspots") or [],
            "workload_summary": {
                "journalist_count": len(workload.get("journalists") or []),
                "total_open_items": workload.get("total_open_items"),
            },
            "deadline_risk_summary": {
                "at_risk_count": deadline_risk.get("at_risk_count"),
                "thresholds_hours": deadline_risk.get("thresholds_hours"),
            },
            "resource_summary": resources.get("utilization"),
            "totals": {
                "content": sum(content_by_status.values()),
                "story_pitches": sum(pitches_by_status.values()),
                "publications": sum((publication.get("publications_by_status") or {}).values()),
            },
        }

    def workload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Per-journalist open workload + availability."""
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT j.journalist_id, j.user_id, u.username,
                       COALESCE(NULLIF(TRIM(e.first_name || ' ' || e.last_name), ''), u.username)
                         AS full_name,
                       j.primary_specialization, j.secondary_specialization,
                       j.availability_status,
                       COUNT(c.content_id) FILTER (
                         WHERE c.status IN ('draft', 'submitted', 'rework')
                       ) AS open_items,
                       COUNT(c.content_id) FILTER (WHERE c.status = 'rework') AS rework_items,
                       COUNT(c.content_id) FILTER (WHERE c.status = 'submitted') AS submitted_items
                FROM journalist j
                JOIN users u ON u.user_id = j.user_id
                LEFT JOIN employee e ON e.employee_id = u.employee_id
                LEFT JOIN content c ON c.author_id = j.journalist_id
                WHERE j.status = 'active'
                GROUP BY j.journalist_id, j.user_id, u.username, e.first_name, e.last_name,
                         j.primary_specialization, j.secondary_specialization, j.availability_status
                ORDER BY open_items DESC, j.journalist_id
                """
            )
            journalists = [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall() or []]
        total_open = sum(int(j.get("open_items") or 0) for j in journalists)
        return {
            "journalists": journalists,
            "total_open_items": total_open,
            "method": "open content in draft/submitted/rework by author_id",
        }

    def deadline_risk(self, *, submitted_hours: float = 48.0, rework_hours: float = 24.0) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Items stuck too long in stage (proxy for deadline risk)."""
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT content_id, title, status, author_id,
                       ROUND(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (NOW() - COALESCE(submitted_at, updated_at))) / 3600.0)
                         AS hours_in_stage
                FROM content
                WHERE status IN ('submitted', 'rework')
                  AND EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (NOW() - COALESCE(submitted_at, updated_at))) / 3600.0
                      >= CASE WHEN status = 'rework' THEN %s ELSE %s END
                ORDER BY hours_in_stage DESC
                LIMIT 200
                """,
                (rework_hours, submitted_hours),
            )
            content_risk = [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall() or []]

            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT story_pitch_id, title, status, priority, source_type,
                       ROUND(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (NOW() - COALESCE(submitted_at, created_at))) / 3600.0)
                         AS hours_in_stage
                FROM story_pitch
                WHERE status = 'submitted'
                  AND EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (NOW() - COALESCE(submitted_at, created_at))) / 3600.0
                      >= %s
                ORDER BY hours_in_stage DESC
                LIMIT 200
                """,
                (submitted_hours,),
            )
            pitch_risk = [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall() or []]

        return {
            "thresholds_hours": {
                "submitted": submitted_hours,
                "rework": rework_hours,
            },
            "content_at_risk": content_risk,
            "pitches_at_risk": pitch_risk,
            "at_risk_count": len(content_risk) + len(pitch_risk),
            "method": "age in stage vs thresholds (no due_at column)",
        }

    def resources(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Resource utilization across desks + journalist load distribution."""
        workload = self.workload()
        journalists = workload.get("journalists") or []
        with db_cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(
                """
                SELECT r.role_code, r.role_name, COUNT(u.user_id) AS active_users
                FROM role r
                LEFT JOIN users u ON u.role_id = r.role_id AND u.account_status = 'active'
                GROUP BY r.role_code, r.role_name
                ORDER BY r.role_code
                """
            )
            desks = [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall() or []]

        loads = [int(j.get("open_items") or 0) for j in journalists]
        available = sum(
            1 for j in journalists if str(j.get("availability_status") or "") == "available"
        )
        avg_load = round(sum(loads) / len(loads), 2) if loads else 0.0
        overloaded = [j for j in journalists if int(j.get("open_items") or 0) >= max(3, avg_load + 2)]
        underutilized = [
            j
            for j in journalists
            if str(j.get("availability_status") or "") == "available"
            and int(j.get("open_items") or 0) == 0
        ]

        return {
            "desks": desks,
            "utilization": {
                "active_journalists": len(journalists),
                "available_journalists": available,
                "avg_open_load": avg_load,
                "overloaded_count": len(overloaded),
                "underutilized_count": len(underutilized),
            },
            "overloaded": overloaded[:20],
            "underutilized": underutilized[:20],
            "method": "role headcount + journalist open-load distribution",
        }

========================================================================================
FILE: services/permission_service.py
========================================================================================
"""RBAC helpers — role_code → capabilities (your desk model)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Iterable

from config.constants import ROLE_ADMIN, ROLE_CAPABILITIES


class PermissionError(Exception):
    pass


def capabilities_for_role(role_code: str) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    return ROLE_CAPABILITIES.get(role_code, ())


def has_capability(role_code: str, capability: str) -> bool:
    if role_code == ROLE_ADMIN:
        return True
    return capability in capabilities_for_role(role_code)


def require_roles(role_code: str, allowed: Iterable[str]) -> None:
    allowed_set = set(allowed)
    if role_code == ROLE_ADMIN or role_code in allowed_set:
        return
    raise PermissionError(f"Role '{role_code}' is not allowed for this action.")


def require_capability(role_code: str, capability: str) -> None:
    if has_capability(role_code, capability):
        return
    raise PermissionError(f"Role '{role_code}' lacks capability '{capability}'.")

========================================================================================
FILE: services/persistence_service.py
========================================================================================
"""Persist raw ingest rows and cleaned content into Postgres."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from entities.content.content import Content
from entities.content.evidence import Evidence
from entities.content.story_pitch import StoryPitch
from repository.content_repository import ContentRepository
from repository.user_repository import UserRepository
from utils.logger import get_logger
from utils.progress import progress
from utils.validators import require_content_status, require_pitch_status, validate_content_record

log = get_logger("persistence")


class PersistenceService:
    def __init__(
        self,
        content_repo: ContentRepository | None = None,
        user_repo: UserRepository | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.content_repo = content_repo or ContentRepository()
        self.user_repo = user_repo or UserRepository()

    def persist_raw_records(self, records: list[dict[str, Any]], *, cleaning_status: str = "pending") -> list[int]:
        payload = []
        for row in records:
            payload.append(
                {
                    "source_file": str(
                        row.get("_source_file") or row.get("source_dataset") or row.get("source_type") or "csv"
                    )[:256],
                    "title": str(row.get("title") or ""),
                    "raw_text": str(row.get("_raw_text") or row.get("text") or row.get("content_body") or ""),
                    "category": str(row.get("category") or "")[:128],
                    "date": str(row.get("date") or row.get("published_date") or "")[:64],
                    "source_type": str(row.get("source_type") or "")[:128],
                    "cleaning_status": cleaning_status,
                    "reject_reason": row.get("_reject_reason"),
                    "content_id": row.get("content_id"),
                }
            )
        if payload:
            progress(
                f"Writing {len(payload)} raw_ingest row(s) "
                f"(status={cleaning_status})..."
            )
        ids = self.content_repo.raw.create_many(payload)
        log.info("Persisted %s raw ingest rows (status=%s)", len(ids), cleaning_status)
        return ids

    def persist_content(self, records: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[int]:
        self.user_repo.ensure_demo_user()
        ids: list[int] = []
        total = len(records)
        if total:
            progress(f"Saving cleaned content to database ({total} row(s))...")
        for index, row in enumerate(records, start=1):
            if index == 1 or index % 50 == 0 or index == total:
                progress(f"  content row {index}/{total}...")

            errors = validate_content_record(row)
            if errors:
                log.warning("Skip persist content errors=%s title=%s", errors, row.get("title"))
                self.persist_raw_records(
                    [{**row, "_reject_reason": ",".join(errors)}],
                    cleaning_status="rejected",
                )
                continue

            category = str(row.get("category_name") or row.get("category") or "").strip()
            body = str(row.get("content_body") or row.get("text") or "")
            raw_body = str(row.get("_raw_text") or row.get("text") or body)
            summary = str(row.get("summary") or "").strip()
            if not summary and category:
                summary = f"[{category}] {body[:200]}"
            elif not summary:
                summary = body[:240] or None

            status = require_content_status(str(row.get("status") or "draft"))
            content = Content(
                title=str(row["title"]),
                content_body=body,
                raw_body=raw_body,
                summary=summary,
                status=status,
                content_kind=str(row.get("content_kind") or "article"),
                source_type=str(row.get("source_type") or row.get("source_dataset") or "pipeline"),
            )
            content_id = self.content_repo.save_content_bundle(
                content,
                event_location=str(row.get("event_location") or category or ""),
            )
            ids.append(content_id)
            source = str(row.get("source_type") or row.get("source_dataset") or "pipeline")
            self.content_repo.save_evidence(
                Evidence(
                    content_id=content_id,
                    title=f"Ingest note for {content.title[:40]}",
                    evidence_type="ingest_log",
                    body_text=f"Persisted from source={source}",
                )
            )
            self.persist_raw_records(
                [{**row, "content_id": content_id}],
                cleaning_status="kept",
            )
        log.info("Persisted %s cleaned content rows", len(ids))
        return ids

    def persist_rejected(self, records: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[int]:
        return self.persist_raw_records(records, cleaning_status="rejected")

    def persist_pitches(self, records: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[int]:
        ids: list[int] = []
        for row in records:
            status = require_pitch_status(str(row.get("status") or "submitted"))
            title = str(row.get("title") or "").strip()
            summary = str(row.get("summary") or row.get("text") or title or "Pipeline pitch")[:500]
            pitch = StoryPitch(
                title=title,
                summary=summary,
                priority=str(row.get("priority") or "medium"),
                trend_score=float(row.get("trend_score") or 0),
                audience_interest_score=float(row.get("audience_interest_score") or 0),
                coverage_gap_score=float(row.get("coverage_gap_score") or 0),
                status=status,
                source_type=str(row.get("source_type") or "pipeline"),
            )
            ids.append(self.content_repo.save_pitch(pitch))
        log.info("Persisted %s pitch rows", len(ids))
        return ids

FILE: services/publisher/publisher_service.py

"""Publication queue — approve, template, preview, publish, schedule."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any

from config.constants import PUBLICATION_TEMPLATES
from dao.content_dao import ContentDAO
from dao.publication_dao import PublicationDAO
from utils.errors import ValidationError
from utils.validators import require_positive_id


class PublisherService:
    def __init__(
        self,
        publications: PublicationDAO | None = None,
        content: ContentDAO | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.publications = publications or PublicationDAO()
        self.content = content or ContentDAO()

    def list_articles(self, limit: int = 100) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"articles": self.publications.list_ready_articles(limit=limit)}

    def templates(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"templates": list(PUBLICATION_TEMPLATES)}

    def _pub_for_content(self, content_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        content_id = require_positive_id(content_id, "Content ID")
        row = self.content.require(content_id)
        if str(row.get("status")) != "approved":
            raise ValidationError("Only approved content can enter the publication queue.")
        return self.publications.ensure_for_content(content_id)

    def approve(self, content_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        pub = self._pub_for_content(content_id)
        updated = self.publications.update_fields(int(pub["publication_id"]), status="approved")
        return {"content_id": content_id, "publication": updated}

    def assign_template(self, content_id: int, template_code: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        codes = {t["code"] for t in PUBLICATION_TEMPLATES}
        if template_code not in codes:
            raise ValidationError(
                f"Unknown template '{template_code}'. Allowed: {', '.join(sorted(codes))}"
            )
        pub = self._pub_for_content(content_id)
        updated = self.publications.update_fields(
            int(pub["publication_id"]),
            template_code=template_code,
        )
        return {"content_id": content_id, "publication": updated}

    def preview(self, content_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        content = self.content.require(require_positive_id(content_id, "Content ID"))
        pub = self.publications.read_by_content_id(content_id) or {}
        template_code = pub.get("template_code") or "web_standard"
        template = next(
            (t for t in PUBLICATION_TEMPLATES if t["code"] == template_code),
            PUBLICATION_TEMPLATES[0],
        )
        body = content.get("content_body") or content.get("raw_body") or ""
        return {
            "content_id": content_id,
            "title": content.get("title"),
            "template": template,
            "preview_html": (
                f"<article data-template='{template['code']}'>"
                f"<h1>{content.get('title') or ''}</h1>"
                f"<p>{body}</p>"
                f"</article>"
            ),
            "publication_status": pub.get("status"),
        }

    def publish(self, content_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        pub = self._pub_for_content(content_id)
        if not pub.get("template_code"):
            raise ValidationError("Assign a template before publishing.")
        updated = self.publications.update_fields(
            int(pub["publication_id"]),
            status="published",
            published_at=datetime.utcnow(),
            scheduled_at=None,
        )
        self.content.update_status(content_id, "published")
        return {"content_id": content_id, "publication": updated}

    def schedule(self, content_id: int, scheduled_at: datetime) -> dict[str, Any]:
        compare_at = scheduled_at.replace(tzinfo=None) if scheduled_at.tzinfo else scheduled_at
        if compare_at <= datetime.utcnow():
            raise ValidationError("scheduled_at must be in the future.")
        pub = self._pub_for_content(content_id)
        if not pub.get("template_code"):
            raise ValidationError("Assign a template before scheduling.")
        updated = self.publications.update_fields(
            int(pub["publication_id"]),
            status="scheduled",
            scheduled_at=scheduled_at,
        )
        return {"content_id": content_id, "publication": updated}

    def analytics(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.publications.analytics()

FILE: services/report_service.py

"""Persist and read cleaning quality reports from DB."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from dao.cleaning_report_dao import CleaningReportDAO
from utils.errors import NotFoundError
from utils.validators import require_positive_id


class ReportService:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.dao = CleaningReportDAO()

    def save_cleaning_report(
        self,
        *,
        report_type: str,
        result: dict[str, Any],
        source_files: list[str] | str | None = None,
        created_by: int | None = None,
    ) -> int:
        if isinstance(source_files, list):
            source_text = ", ".join(source_files)
        else:
            source_text = source_files or ""
        stats = result.get("stats") or {}
        report = result.get("report") or {}
        return self.dao.create(
            report_type=report_type,
            report=report,
            input_count=int(stats.get("input") or 0),
            kept_count=int(stats.get("kept") or 0),
            rejected_count=int(stats.get("rejected") or 0),
            source_files=source_text,
            created_by=created_by,
        )

    def list_recent(self, limit: int = 15) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.dao.list_recent(limit=limit)

    def get(self, report_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = self.dao.read(require_positive_id(report_id, "Report ID"))
        if not row:
            raise NotFoundError(f"Cleaning report ID {report_id} was not found.")
        return row

FILE: services/user/user_service.py

"""User domain service — employee/user/desk creation with validation."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from config.constants import CAP_MANAGE_USERS, ROLE_ADMIN, ROLE_NEWSROOM_MANAGER
from entities.employee import Employee
from entities.user import User
from modules.authentication.auth_service import admin_reset_password
from modules.authentication.security import hash_password
from repository.user_repository import UserRepository
from services.access import require_capability_session
from utils.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError
from utils.validators import (
    require_email,
    require_nonempty,
    require_password,
    require_role_code,
    require_username,
)


class UserService:
    def __init__(self, repo: UserRepository | None = None) -> None:
        self.repo = repo or UserRepository()

    def add_user(
        self,
        *,
        username: str,
        password: str,
        first_name: str,
        last_name: str,
        email: str,
        role_code: str = "journalist",
        employee_code: str | None = None,
        session_token: str | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        actor = require_capability_session(CAP_MANAGE_USERS, session_token)
        username = require_username(username)
        password = require_password(password)
        first_name = require_nonempty(first_name, "First name")
        last_name = require_nonempty(last_name, "Last name")
        email = require_email(email)
        role_code = require_role_code(role_code)
        if actor["role_code"] == ROLE_NEWSROOM_MANAGER and role_code == ROLE_ADMIN:
            raise ValidationError("Newsroom managers cannot create administrator accounts.")

        if self.repo.find_by_username(username):
            raise ValidationError(f"Username '{username}' already exists.")

        role_id = self.repo.role_id_by_code(role_code)
        if role_id is None:
            raise ValidationError(f"Unknown role code: {role_code}")

        code = (employee_code or f"EMP-{username.upper()}").strip()
        emp_id = self.repo.create_employee(
            Employee(
                employee_code=code,
                first_name=first_name,
                last_name=last_name,
                email=email,
                department="Newsroom",
                designation=role_code,
            )
        )
        user_id = self.repo.create_user(
            User(
                employee_id=emp_id,
                role_id=role_id,
                username=username,
                password_hash=hash_password(password),
                account_status="active",
            )
        )
        self.repo.ensure_desk(user_id, role_code)
        return {"user_id": user_id, "username": username, "role_code": role_code}

    def find_user(self, username: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        username = require_username(username)
        user = self.repo.find_by_username(username)
        if not user:
            raise NotFoundError(f"User '{username}' was not found.")
        return user

    def reset_password(
        self,
        username: str,
        new_password: str,
        *,
        session_token: str | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Password reset for another account (manage_users capability)."""
        require_capability_session(CAP_MANAGE_USERS, session_token)
        return admin_reset_password(username, new_password)

========================================================================================
FILE: tests/conftest.py

"""Ensure this project's packages win over the parent NewsMind AI tree."""

from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PARENT = ROOT.parent.resolve()

cleaned: list[str] = []
for entry in sys.path:
    try:
        resolved = Path(entry).resolve()
    except OSError:
        cleaned.append(entry)
        continue
    if resolved == PARENT:
        continue
    cleaned.append(entry)
sys.path[:] = cleaned
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def db_ready():
    """Integration tests need a live Postgres schema; skip if unavailable."""
    from database.connection import check_connection, init_schema

    if not check_connection():
        pytest.skip("PostgreSQL not available")
    try:
        init_schema()
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        pytest.skip(f"init_schema failed: {exc}")
    return True

FILE: tests/test_api_aia.py

"""Integration: AIA (Audience Insight Analyst) API."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from api.main import app
from config.constants import DEMO_PASSWORDS
from modules.authentication import logout


@pytest.fixture
def client(db_ready):
    logout()
    with TestClient(app) as test_client:
        yield test_client
    logout()


def _login(client: TestClient, username: str) -> str:
    response = client.post(
        "/auth/login",
        json={"username": username, "password": DEMO_PASSWORDS[username]},
    )
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    return response.json()["session_token"]


def test_aia_paths_in_openapi(client):
    paths = client.get("/openapi.json").json()["paths"]
    assert "/aia/analyze-engagement" in paths
    assert "/aia/predict-category" not in paths
    assert "/aia/generate-story-pitch" in paths
    assert "/aia/story-pitches" in paths
    assert "/auth/change-password" in paths


def test_journalist_forbidden_on_aia(client):
    token = _login(client, "journalist")
    response = client.post(
        "/aia/analyze-engagement",
        headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"},
    )
    assert response.status_code == 403


def test_analyst_can_generate_and_list_pitch(client):
    token = _login(client, "analyst")
    headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"}

    engagement = client.post(
        "/aia/analyze-engagement",
        headers=headers,
        json={"views": 200, "likes": 40, "shares": 12, "comments": 6},
    )
    assert engagement.status_code == 200, engagement.text
    assert engagement.json()["engagement_score"] >= 0
    assert engagement.json()["source"] in {"heuristic", "fallback", "model"}

    scores = client.post(
        "/aia/analyze-trends",
        headers=headers,
        json={"title": "New technology tools reshape newsrooms", "summary": "AI tools expand."},
    )
    assert scores.status_code == 200
    assert "trend_score" in scores.json()

    # Priority model is Editor-owned; AIA uses formula for pitch priority.
    # Category is manual entry (no category model endpoint).
    created = client.post(
        "/aia/generate-story-pitch",
        headers=headers,
        json={
            "title": "AIA API pitch for civic coverage",
            "summary": "Audience interest is rising around local council budgets this week.",
            "category": "City",
            "trend_score": 90,
            "audience_interest_score": 90,
            "coverage_gap_score": 80,
        },
    )
    assert created.status_code == 200, created.text
    body = created.json()
    assert body["story_pitch_id"] > 0
    assert body["pitch_importance"] == "high"
    assert body.get("importance_source") == "formula"
    assert body.get("source_type") == "AI_GENERATED"
    assert body.get("created_by_desk") == "AIA"

    listed = client.get("/aia/story-pitches", headers=headers)
    assert listed.status_code == 200
    assert any(
        row.get("story_pitch_id") == body["story_pitch_id"]
        and row.get("created_by_desk") == "AIA"
        for row in listed.json().get("pitches") or []
    )

    performance = client.get("/aia/performance", headers=headers)
    assert performance.status_code == 200, performance.text
    assert "insights" in performance.json()
    assert "publication_by_status" in performance.json()

"""Auth password validation endpoints."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from api.main import app
from config.constants import DEMO_PASSWORDS
from modules.authentication import logout


@pytest.fixture
def client(db_ready):
    logout()
    with TestClient(app) as test_client:
        yield test_client
    logout()


def _login(client: TestClient, username: str) -> str:
    response = client.post(
        "/auth/login",
        json={"username": username, "password": DEMO_PASSWORDS[username]},
    )
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    return response.json()["session_token"]


def test_weak_password_rejected_on_change(client):
    token = _login(client, "journalist")
    response = client.post(
        "/auth/change-password",
        headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"},
        json={"current_password": DEMO_PASSWORDS["journalist"], "new_password": "short"},
    )
    assert response.status_code == 422
    body = response.json()
    assert body["detail"] == "Validation failed"
    assert body["errors"]


def test_admin_reset_requires_strong_password(client):
    token = _login(client, "admin")
    weak = client.post(
        "/auth/admin/reset-password",
        headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"},
        json={"username": "publisher", "new_password": "weakpass"},
    )
    assert weak.status_code == 422

    strong = client.post(
        "/auth/admin/reset-password",
        headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"},
        json={"username": "publisher", "new_password": "PublisherReset@2026!!"},
    )
    assert strong.status_code == 200, strong.text

    # restore demo password
    restore = client.post(
        "/auth/admin/reset-password",
        headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"},
        json={"username": "publisher", "new_password": DEMO_PASSWORDS["publisher"]},
    )
    assert restore.status_code == 200

"""Integration: CAE, publisher, editor, manager APIs."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timedelta

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from api.main import app
from config.constants import DEMO_PASSWORDS
from modules.authentication import logout

SAMPLE_ARTICLE = (
    "The city council approved a new housing plan covering 10 wards. "
    "Officials said funding will begin next quarter after a public review. "
    "Residents welcomed the announcement during a town hall meeting. "
    "However, some activists asked for clearer timelines and budget figures. "
    "The mayor confirmed that construction may start once permits are issued."
)

_DEMO_EVIDENCE = [
    {
        "title": "Source notes for article",
        "evidence_type": "document",
        "source_url": "https://example.com/source",
        "text": "Attached source for this article only",
    }
]


@pytest.fixture
def client(db_ready):
    logout()
    with TestClient(app) as test_client:
        yield test_client
    logout()


def _login(client: TestClient, username: str) -> str:
    response = client.post(
        "/auth/login",
        json={"username": username, "password": DEMO_PASSWORDS[username]},
    )
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    return response.json()["session_token"]


def test_openapi_includes_new_desks(client):
    paths = client.get("/openapi.json").json()["paths"]
    assert "/cae/adaptations" in paths
    assert "/publisher/articles" in paths
    assert "/editor/pitches" in paths
    assert "/editor/queue" in paths
    assert "/editor/queue/score" in paths
    assert "/editor/event-groups" not in paths
    assert "/editor/pitches/duplicates" in paths
    assert "/editor/assign/suggest" in paths
    assert "/editor/content/{content_id}/rework" in paths
    assert "/manager/overview" in paths
    assert "/manager/queue" in paths
    assert "/manager/bottlenecks" in paths
    assert "/manager/analytics" in paths
    assert "/manager/workload" in paths
    assert "/manager/deadline-risk" in paths
    assert "/manager/resources" in paths
    assert "/manager/event-groups" not in paths
    assert "/aia/performance" in paths
    assert "/aia/predict-priority" not in paths
    assert "/journalist/suggest-category" not in paths
    assert "/journalist/related-content" in paths
    assert "/admin/users" in paths
    assert "/admin/ingest" in paths


def test_cae_create_approve_and_versions(client):
    from pathlib import Path

    model_path = (
        Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "artifacts" / "models" / "cae" / "model.joblib"
    )
    if not model_path.exists():
        pytest.skip("CAE model not trained yet (artifacts/models/cae empty)")

    token = _login(client, "adapter")
    headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"}

    created = client.post(
        "/cae/adaptations",
        headers=headers,
        json={
            "original_title": "City housing plan advances",
            "original_content": SAMPLE_ARTICLE,
            "target_platforms": ["SOCIAL_MEDIA", "WEBSITE"],
        },
    )
    assert created.status_code == 201, created.text
    body = created.json()
    assert body["status"] == "COMPLETED"
    assert len(body["drafts"]) == 2
    draft_id = body["drafts"][0]["id"]

    approved = client.post(
        f"/cae/adaptation-drafts/{draft_id}/approve",
        headers=headers,
        json={"remarks": "Looks good"},
    )
    assert approved.status_code == 200, approved.text
    assert approved.json()["status"] == "APPROVED"

    versions = client.get(
        f"/cae/adaptation-drafts/{draft_id}/versions",
        headers=headers,
    )
    assert versions.status_code == 200
    assert len(versions.json()) >= 1


def test_editor_priority_queue_and_score(client, db_ready):
    editor_token = _login(client, "editor")
    headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {editor_token}"}

    scored = client.post(
        "/editor/queue/score",
        headers=headers,
        json={
            "title": "BREAKING plant fire halts production in Asia",
            "summary": "Emergency shutdown after a critical factory fire.",
        },
    )
    assert scored.status_code == 200, scored.text
    assert scored.json()["urgency"] in {"low", "medium", "high", "urgent"}
    assert scored.json()["source"] in {"model", "fallback"}

    ContentService().add_article(
        title="Low-key bakery contest coverage for weekend",
        body="A local bakery contest drew a small crowd downtown.",
        summary="Community feature",
        status="submitted",
        evidence=_DEMO_EVIDENCE,
    )
    ContentService().add_article(
        title="BREAKING emergency bridge collapse blocks interstate traffic",
        body="Rescue crews responded after a critical bridge failure overnight.",
        summary="Urgent transport incident",
        status="submitted",
        evidence=_DEMO_EVIDENCE,
    )

    queue = client.get("/editor/queue", headers=headers)
    assert queue.status_code == 200, queue.text
    body = queue.json()
    assert body["content_count"] >= 2
    urgencies = [str(t.get("urgency") or "medium") for t in body["tasks"] if t.get("task_type") == "content"]
    rank = {"urgent": 0, "high": 1, "medium": 2, "low": 3}
    assert urgencies == sorted(urgencies, key=lambda p: rank.get(p, 4))


def test_editor_and_publisher_flow(client, db_ready):
    editor_token = _login(client, "editor")
    publisher_token = _login(client, "publisher")
    editor_headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {editor_token}"}
    publisher_headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {publisher_token}"}

    created = ContentService().add_article(
        title="Ready for publication desk review",
        body=SAMPLE_ARTICLE,
        summary="Housing plan story",
        status="submitted",
        evidence=_DEMO_EVIDENCE,
    )
    content_id = int(created["content_id"])

    listed = client.get("/editor/content?status=submitted", headers=editor_headers)
    assert listed.status_code == 200
    assert any(row.get("content_id") == content_id for row in listed.json()["items"])
    assert any("editor_queue_urgency" in row for row in listed.json()["items"])

    rework = client.put(
        f"/editor/content/{content_id}/rework",
        headers=editor_headers,
        json={"remarks": "Add more sources"},
    )
    assert rework.status_code == 200, rework.text
    assert rework.json()["status"] == "rework"

    journalist_token = _login(client, "journalist")
    j_headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {journalist_token}"}
    rework_list = client.get("/journalist/content?status=rework", headers=j_headers)
    assert rework_list.status_code == 200
    # May or may not include this id if author differs; status filter must work
    assert "items" in rework_list.json()

    resubmit = client.put(
        f"/journalist/content/{content_id}/status",
        headers=j_headers,
        json={"status": "submitted"},
    )
    assert resubmit.status_code == 200, resubmit.text
    assert resubmit.json()["status"] == "submitted"

    approved = client.put(
        f"/editor/content/{content_id}/approve",
        headers=editor_headers,
    )
    assert approved.status_code == 200, approved.text
    assert approved.json()["status"] == "approved"

    queue = client.get("/publisher/articles", headers=publisher_headers)
    assert queue.status_code == 200
    assert any(row.get("content_id") == content_id for row in queue.json()["articles"])

    templated = client.put(
        f"/publisher/articles/{content_id}/template",
        headers=publisher_headers,
        json={"template_code": "web_standard"},
    )
    assert templated.status_code == 200, templated.text

    preview = client.get(
        f"/publisher/articles/{content_id}/preview",
        headers=publisher_headers,
    )
    assert preview.status_code == 200
    assert "preview_html" in preview.json()

    published = client.post(
        f"/publisher/articles/{content_id}/publish",
        headers=publisher_headers,
    )
    assert published.status_code == 200, published.text
    assert published.json()["publication"]["status"] == "published"

def test_publisher_schedule_requires_future(client, db_ready):
    editor_token = _login(client, "editor")
    publisher_token = _login(client, "publisher")
    editor_headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {editor_token}"}
    publisher_headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {publisher_token}"}

    created = ContentService().add_article(
        title="Schedule me for next week desk",
        body=SAMPLE_ARTICLE,
        status="submitted",
        evidence=_DEMO_EVIDENCE,
    )
    content_id = int(created["content_id"])
    client.put(f"/editor/content/{content_id}/approve", headers=editor_headers)
    client.put(
        f"/publisher/articles/{content_id}/template",
        headers=publisher_headers,
        json={"template_code": "newsletter"},
    )

    when = (datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(days=2)).isoformat()
    scheduled = client.post(
        f"/publisher/articles/{content_id}/schedule",
        headers=publisher_headers,
        json={"scheduled_at": when},
    )
    assert scheduled.status_code == 200, scheduled.text
    assert scheduled.json()["publication"]["status"] == "scheduled"

    assert logout_resp.status_code == 200


def test_journalist_desk_content_endpoints(client):
    token = _login(client, "journalist")
    headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"}
    body_text = (
        "The city council approved a housing plan covering several wards after a public review. "
        "Officials said funding will begin next quarter."
    )

    news = client.post(
        "/journalist/news",
        headers=headers,
        json={
            "title": "Housing plan advances in council",
            "body": body_text,
            "summary": "Council housing update",
            "status": "draft",
            "evidence": [
                {
                    "title": "Council memo",
                    "evidence_type": "link",
                    "source_url": "https://example.com/housing",
                    "text": "Funding note",
                }
            ],
        },
    )
    assert news.status_code == 200, news.text
    news_body = news.json()
    content_id = news_body["content_id"]
    assert news_body["news_id"] > 0
    assert news_body.get("evidence_ids")

    article = client.post(
        "/journalist/articles",
        headers=headers,
        json={
            "title": "Feature on civic housing reform",
            "body": body_text,
            "article_type": "feature",
            "status": "draft",
            "evidence": [
                {
                    "title": "Interview notes",
                    "evidence_type": "document",
                    "source_url": "",
                    "text": "Official confirmed timeline",
                }
            ],
        },
    )
    assert article.status_code == 200, article.text
    assert article.json()["article_id"] > 0
    assert article.json().get("evidence_ids")
    article_id_content = article.json()["content_id"]
    listed_evidence = client.get(
        f"/journalist/content/{article_id_content}/evidence",
        headers=headers,
    )
    assert listed_evidence.status_code == 200, listed_evidence.text
    assert len(listed_evidence.json()["items"]) >= 1

    listed = client.get("/journalist/content?limit=20", headers=headers)
    assert listed.status_code == 200
    assert any(row.get("content_id") == content_id for row in listed.json()["items"])

    viewed = client.get(f"/journalist/content/{content_id}", headers=headers)
    assert viewed.status_code == 200
    assert viewed.json()["content"]["content_id"] == content_id

    submitted = client.put(
        f"/journalist/content/{content_id}/status",
        headers=headers,
        json={"status": "submitted"},
    )
    assert submitted.status_code == 200
    assert submitted.json()["status"] == "submitted"

    evidence = client.post(
        "/journalist/evidence",
        headers=headers,
        json={
            "content_id": content_id,
            "title": "Council meeting minutes evidence",
            "evidence_type": "document",
        },
    )
    assert evidence.status_code == 200, evidence.text
    assert evidence.json()["evidence_id"] > 0

    news_list = client.get("/journalist/news", headers=headers)
    assert news_list.status_code == 200
    assert isinstance(news_list.json()["items"], list)

    assert client.get("/journalist/raw-ingest", headers=headers).status_code == 404

    paths = client.get("/openapi.json").json()["paths"]
    assert "/journalist/raw-ingest" not in paths
    assert "/admin/raw-ingest" in paths
    for path in (
        "/journalist/news",
        "/journalist/articles",
        "/journalist/content",
        "/journalist/content/{content_id}",
        "/journalist/content/{content_id}/status",
        "/journalist/evidence",
        "/journalist/content/{content_id}/evidence",
    ):
        assert path in paths

FILE: tests/test_integration_pipeline.py
========================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cleaners.orchestrator import CleaningOrchestrator
from config.constants import DEMO_PASSWORDS
from modules.admin import AdminModule
from modules.authentication import authenticate, logout
from services.persistence_service import PersistenceService


def test_clean_and_persist_inline_fixture(db_ready, tmp_path):
    rows = [
        {
            "title": "Integration fixture: civic coverage follow-up",
            "text": (
                "Inline test body long enough for cleaner schema checks so the persistence "
                "layer can store a cleaned content row through DAO."
            ),
            "_raw_text": "raw inline body before clean",
            "category": "City",
            "date": "2026-11-02",
            "status": "draft",
            "content_kind": "article",
            "source_type": "test",
        }
    ]
    pitches = [
        {
            "title": "Integration pitch: coverage gap on civic response",
            "summary": "Test pitch summary with enough detail for editorial follow-up assignment.",
            "text": "Pitch body with enough characters for schema cleaner when record_type is pitch.",
            "status": "submitted",
            "source_type": "test",
            "trend_score": 0.5,
            "audience_interest_score": 0.5,
            "coverage_gap_score": 0.5,
        }
    ]
    cleaned_content = CleaningOrchestrator(processed_dir=tmp_path).clean_content(rows)
    cleaned_pitches = CleaningOrchestrator(processed_dir=tmp_path).clean_pitches(pitches)
    assert cleaned_content["stats"]["kept"] == 1

    persist = PersistenceService()
    content_ids = persist.persist_content(cleaned_content["kept"])
    pitch_ids = persist.persist_pitches(cleaned_pitches["kept"])
    assert len(content_ids) == 1
    assert len(pitch_ids) == 1


def test_admin_pipeline_persists_raw_and_clean(db_ready):
    authenticate("admin", DEMO_PASSWORDS["admin"])
    try:
        admin = AdminModule()
        files = admin.list_csv_files()["all_files"]
        assert files
        # Single-file path keeps CI bounded while still exercising DB persist
        loaded = admin.ingest_file(files[0], store_raw=True)
        cleaned = admin.clean(
            records=loaded["records"][:40],
            pitches=[],
            source_files=loaded["source_files"],
        )
        result = admin.persist(cleaned)
        assert result["actor"] == "admin"
        assert "persisted_content" in result
        assert "rejected_raw_stored" in result
        assert cleaned.get("cleaning_report_id")
    finally:
        logout()

========================================================================================
FILE: tests/test_single_file_ingest.py
========================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from config.constants import DEMO_PASSWORDS
from modules.admin import AdminModule
from modules.authentication import authenticate, logout
from services.ingestion_service import IngestionService
from services.report_service import ReportService
from utils.helpers import project_path


def test_single_file_ingest_registers_hash(db_ready, tmp_path):
    csv_path = tmp_path / "extra_story.csv"
    csv_path.write_text(
        "title,text,category\n"
        "Single file civic update story,"
        "Body long enough for schema cleaner validation during single-file ingest tests,"
        "City\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    svc = IngestionService(raw_dir=tmp_path)
    first = svc.ingest_one("extra_story.csv")
    assert first["usable_row_count"] == 1
    assert first["source_files"] == ["extra_story.csv"]
    assert svc.file_needs_ingest("extra_story.csv") is False
    assert svc.list_new_or_changed_files() == []

    csv_path.write_text(
        "title,text,category\n"
        "Single file civic update story,"
        "Body long enough for schema cleaner validation during single-file ingest tests,"
        "City\n"
        "Another single file follow-up story,"
        "Second body long enough for schema cleaner validation during single-file ingest,"
        "City\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    assert svc.file_needs_ingest("extra_story.csv") is True
    second = svc.ingest_all_content(only_new=True)
    assert second["source_files"] == ["extra_story.csv"]
    assert second["collections"]["list_count"] == 2


def test_admin_pipeline_file_and_report(db_ready, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    raw = project_path("data", "raw")
    files = sorted(p.relative_to(raw).as_posix() for p in raw.rglob("*.csv") if p.is_file())
    if not files:
        pytest.skip("No CSV files in data/raw")
    # Prefer a small-ish top-level sample if present
    target = next((f for f in files if "/" not in f), files[0])

    authenticate("admin", DEMO_PASSWORDS["admin"])
    try:
        admin = AdminModule()
        # Use only_new listing path + single file ingest without full tree persist cost
        loaded = admin.ingest_file(target, store_raw=False)
        assert target in loaded["source_files"]
        cleaned = admin.clean(
            records=loaded["records"][:25],
            pitches=[],
            source_files=loaded["source_files"],
        )
        assert cleaned.get("cleaning_report_id")
        report = ReportService().get(cleaned["cleaning_report_id"])
        assert report["report_type"] == "content"
        assert report["input_count"] == cleaned["content_stats"]["input"]
        listed = admin.list_reports(limit=5)
        assert any(
            r["cleaning_report_id"] == cleaned["cleaning_report_id"]
            for r in listed["reports"]
        )
    finally:
        logout()

========================================================================================
FILE: utils/console_ui.py
========================================================================================
"""Human-readable console formatting (no JSON dumps)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Mapping, Optional, Sequence


def line(char: str = "=", width: int = 56) -> str:
    return char * width


def header(title: str) -> None:
    print()
    print(line("="))
    print(f"  {title}")
    print(line("="))


def success(message: str) -> None:
    print(f"[OK] {message}")


def error(message: str) -> None:
    print(f"[ERROR] {message}")


def info(message: str) -> None:
    print(f"[INFO] {message}")


def kv(label: str, value: Any) -> None:
    text = "-" if value is None or value == "" else value
    print(f"  {label} - {text}")


def blank() -> None:
    print()


def show_user(user: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
    header("Session")
    kv("Username", user.get("username"))
    kv("Full Name", user.get("full_name"))
    kv("Role", user.get("role_name") or user.get("role_code"))
    kv("Role Code", user.get("role_code"))
    caps = user.get("capabilities") or []
    kv("Capabilities", ", ".join(caps) if caps else "none")
    profiles = user.get("profiles") or {}
    if profiles:
        kv("Profiles", ", ".join(f"{k}={v}" for k, v in profiles.items()))
    blank()


def show_dict_rows(title: str, rows: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]], fields: Sequence[tuple[str, str]]) -> None:
    """fields: list of (label, key)."""
    header(title)
    if not rows:
        info("No records found.")
        blank()
        return
    for i, row in enumerate(rows, start=1):
        print(f"  --- Record {i} ---")
        for label, key in fields:
            kv(label, row.get(key))
        print()
    kv("Total", len(rows))
    blank()


def show_stats(title: str, stats: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
    header(title)
    for key, value in stats.items():
        label = str(key).replace("_", " ").title()
        if isinstance(value, Mapping):
            kv(label, ", ".join(f"{k}={v}" for k, v in value.items()))
        else:
            kv(label, value)
    blank()


CANCEL_WORDS = {"q", "quit", "exit", "cancel"}


class UserCancelled(Exception):
    """User asked to cancel the current prompt/action."""


def is_cancel(value: str) -> bool:
    return (value or "").strip().lower() in CANCEL_WORDS


def prompt_nonempty(label: str) -> str:
    while True:
        value = input(f"{label}: ").strip()
        if is_cancel(value):
            raise UserCancelled("Cancelled.")
        if value:
            return value
        error(f"{label} cannot be empty.")


def prompt_optional(label: str, default: str = "") -> str:
    value = input(f"{label}: ").strip()
    if is_cancel(value):
        raise UserCancelled("Cancelled.")
    return value if value else default


def prompt_choice(label: str, allowed: Sequence[str], *, allow_empty: bool = False) -> Optional[str]:
    options = ", ".join(allowed)
    while True:
        value = input(f"{label} [{options}]: ").strip().lower()
        if is_cancel(value):
            raise UserCancelled("Cancelled.")
        if not value:
            if allow_empty:
                return None
            error(f"{label} is required. Choose one of: {options}")
            continue
        if value in {a.lower() for a in allowed}:
            return value
        error(f"Invalid {label.lower()}. Allowed: {options}")

========================================================================================
FILE: utils/csv_fields.py
========================================================================================
"""Normalize any CSV news-like columns into canonical fields.

Works for current samples and unknown future datasets (CSV only).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from utils.helpers import coerce_str

TITLE_ALIASES = (
    "title",
    "headline",
    "headline_text",
    "headlines",
    "news_title",
    "article_title",
    "name",
    "heading",
    "primary_heading",
    "story_title",
    "subject_line",
)
TEXT_ALIASES = (
    "text",
    "content",
    "content_body",
    "body",
    "article",
    "article_body",
    "description",
    "short_description",
    "summary",
    "story",
    "full_text",
    "full_article",
    "article_text",
    "news_text",
    "content_text",
)
CATEGORY_ALIASES = (
    "category",
    "category_name",
    "subject",
    "section",
    "topic",
    "topic_label",
    "tags",
    "label",
    "class",
    "genre",
    "channel",
)
DATE_ALIASES = (
    "date",
    "published_date",
    "publish_date",
    "publication_date",
    "datetime",
    "time",
    "created_at",
    "published_at",
)
STATUS_ALIASES = ("status", "state")
KIND_ALIASES = ("content_kind", "kind", "type", "article_type", "news_type")
SOURCE_ALIASES = ("source_dataset", "source", "dataset", "origin")
SUMMARY_ALIASES = ("summary", "excerpt", "teaser", "abstract")
SKIP_KEYS = {"id", "index", "unnamed: 0", "row_id", "uuid"}


def _first(row: dict[str, Any], aliases: tuple[str, ...]) -> str:
    for key in aliases:
        if key in row and coerce_str(row.get(key)):
            return coerce_str(row.get(key))
    return ""


def _longest_text_value(row: dict[str, Any], *, exclude: set[str] | None = None) -> str:
    exclude = exclude or set()
    best = ""
    for key, value in row.items():
        if key in exclude or key in SKIP_KEYS:
            continue
        text = coerce_str(value)
        if len(text) > len(best):
            best = text
    return best


def normalize_row(row: dict[str, Any], *, source_file: str = "") -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Map arbitrary CSV columns → title/text/category/date/..."""
    raw = {
        str(k).strip().lower(): v
        for k, v in row.items()
        if str(k).strip() and not str(k).lower().startswith("unnamed")
    }
    raw.pop("", None)

    title = _first(raw, TITLE_ALIASES)
    text = _first(raw, TEXT_ALIASES)
    category = (_first(raw, CATEGORY_ALIASES) or "General")[:128]
    if category.count(",") >= 2:
        category = category.split(",", 1)[0].strip()[:128] or "General"
    date_val = _first(raw, DATE_ALIASES)[:64]
    status = (_first(raw, STATUS_ALIASES) or "draft").lower()
    kind = (_first(raw, KIND_ALIASES) or "article").lower()
    if kind not in {"article", "news"}:
        kind = "article"
    source = (_first(raw, SOURCE_ALIASES) or source_file)[:128]
    summary = _first(raw, SUMMARY_ALIASES)

    # Unknown dataset fallback: use longest string field as body/title
    if not text:
        text = _longest_text_value(raw, exclude={*(TITLE_ALIASES), *(CATEGORY_ALIASES), *(DATE_ALIASES)})
    if not title and text:
        title = text[:120].strip()
    if title and not text:
        text = title
        if category.lower() in {"", "general", "unknown"}:
            category = "Headline"

    out = dict(raw)
    out.update(
        {
            "title": title,
            "text": text,
            "content_body": text,
            "category": category,
            "category_name": category,
            "date": date_val,
            "status": status if status else "draft",
            "content_kind": kind,
            "summary": summary,
            "source_dataset": source,
            "source_type": f"csv:{source_file}" if source_file else coerce_str(raw.get("source_type")),
            "_raw_title": title,
            "_raw_text": text,
            "_source_file": source_file,
        }
    )
    return out

FILE: utils/errors.py

"""Application exceptions for the NewsMind console."""

from __future__ import annotations


class AppError(Exception):
    """Base application error with a user-facing message."""

    def __init__(self, message: str) -> None:
        self.message = message
        super().__init__(message)


class ValidationError(AppError):
    """Invalid or missing field values."""


class NotFoundError(AppError):
    """Requested record does not exist."""


class AuthError(AppError):
    """Authentication / session failure."""


class PermissionDeniedError(AppError):
    """Caller lacks required capability."""

========================================================================================
FILE: utils/helpers.py
========================================================================================
"""Small helper utilities."""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterable


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def project_path(*parts: str) -> Path:
    return ROOT.joinpath(*parts)


def coerce_str(value: Any, default: str = "") -> str:
    if value is None:
        return default
    return str(value).strip()


def normalize_key(text: str) -> str:
    text = coerce_str(text).lower()
    text = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", " ", text)
    text = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9\s]", " ", text)
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip()
    return text


def chunked(items: Iterable[Any], size: int) -> list[list[Any]]:
    buf: list[Any] = []
    out: list[list[Any]] = []
    for item in items:
        buf.append(item)
        if len(buf) >= size:
            out.append(buf)
            buf = []
    if buf:
        out.append(buf)
    return out

========================================================================================
FILE: utils/logger.py
========================================================================================
"""Application logging."""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from pathlib import Path

from utils.helpers import project_path


def get_logger(name: str = "newsmind") -> logging.Logger:
    logger = logging.getLogger(name)
    if logger.handlers:
        return logger
    logger.setLevel(logging.INFO)
    fmt = logging.Formatter("%(asctime)s | %(levelname)s | %(name)s | %(message)s")

    log_dir = project_path("logs")
    log_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    file_handler = logging.FileHandler(log_dir / "application.log", encoding="utf-8")
    file_handler.setFormatter(fmt)
    file_handler.setLevel(logging.INFO)
    # Console: show INFO+ so pipeline steps are visible in the terminal
    stream_handler = logging.StreamHandler()
    stream_handler.setFormatter(fmt)
    stream_handler.setLevel(logging.INFO)
    logger.addHandler(file_handler)
    logger.addHandler(stream_handler)
    logger.propagate = False
    return logger

========================================================================================
FILE: utils/progress.py
========================================================================================
"""User-visible progress lines for long pipeline steps (console + log file)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from pathlib import Path

from utils.console_ui import info
from utils.helpers import project_path

_file_log: logging.Logger | None = None


def _file_logger() -> logging.Logger:
    """Write progress to application.log without duplicating on the console."""
    global _file_log
    if _file_log is not None:
        return _file_log
    logger = logging.getLogger("progress")
    if not logger.handlers:
        logger.setLevel(logging.INFO)
        log_dir = project_path("logs")
        log_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        handler = logging.FileHandler(log_dir / "application.log", encoding="utf-8")
        handler.setFormatter(
            logging.Formatter("%(asctime)s | %(levelname)s | %(name)s | %(message)s")
        )
        logger.addHandler(handler)
        logger.propagate = False
    _file_log = logger
    return logger


def progress(message: str) -> None:
    """Show a clear step on the console and mirror it to application.log."""
    text = str(message).strip()
    if not text:
        return
    info(text)
    _file_logger().info("%s", text)

========================================================================================
FILE: utils/validators.py
========================================================================================
"""Field validation used by DAOs, services, and cleaners."""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from typing import Any, Optional

from config.constants import (
    ALLOWED_ACCOUNT_STATUSES,
    ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES,
    ALLOWED_PITCH_STATUSES,
    CONTENT_KINDS,
    MIN_BODY_LENGTH,
    MIN_TITLE_LENGTH,
)
from utils.errors import ValidationError

EMAIL_RE = re.compile(r"^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$")
ALLOWED_SEVERITIES = ("low", "medium", "high", "critical")
ALLOWED_PRIORITIES = ("low", "medium", "high", "urgent")
ALLOWED_ARTICLE_TYPES = ("feature", "analysis", "opinion", "profile", "explainer")
ALLOWED_DESK_STATUSES = ("active", "inactive")
ALLOWED_AVAILABILITY = ("available", "busy", "away", "leave")
ALLOWED_EMPLOYMENT = ("active", "inactive", "terminated")
ALLOWED_CLEANING_STATUS = ("pending", "kept", "rejected", "ingested")


def is_nonempty(value: Any) -> bool:
    return value is not None and str(value).strip() != ""


def require_nonempty(value: Any, label: str) -> str:
    text = "" if value is None else str(value).strip()
    if not text:
        raise ValidationError(f"{label} cannot be empty.")
    return text


def require_optional_str(value: Any, label: str, *, max_len: Optional[int] = None) -> Optional[str]:
    if value is None:
        return None
    text = str(value).strip()
    if not text:
        return None
    if max_len is not None and len(text) > max_len:
        raise ValidationError(f"{label} must be at most {max_len} characters.")
    return text


def is_valid_email(value: str) -> bool:
    return bool(EMAIL_RE.match((value or "").strip()))


def require_email(value: Any) -> str:
    email = require_nonempty(value, "Email")
    if not is_valid_email(email):
        raise ValidationError("A valid email is required.")
    return email


def is_valid_title(value: str) -> bool:
    return len((value or "").strip()) >= MIN_TITLE_LENGTH


def require_title(value: Any) -> str:
    title = require_nonempty(value, "Title")
    if len(title) < MIN_TITLE_LENGTH:
        raise ValidationError(f"Title must be at least {MIN_TITLE_LENGTH} characters.")
    if len(title) > 512:
        raise ValidationError("Title must be at most 512 characters.")
    return title


def is_valid_body(value: str, *, headline_ok: bool = False) -> bool:
    text = (value or "").strip()
    if len(text) >= MIN_BODY_LENGTH:
        return True
    if headline_ok and len(text) >= MIN_TITLE_LENGTH:
        return True
    return False


def require_body(value: Any, *, headline_ok: bool = False) -> str:
    body = require_nonempty(value, "Body")
    if not is_valid_body(body, headline_ok=headline_ok):
        raise ValidationError(f"Body must be at least {MIN_BODY_LENGTH} characters.")
    return body


def require_choice(value: Any, label: str, allowed: tuple[str, ...]) -> str:
    text = require_nonempty(value, label).lower()
    if text not in {a.lower() for a in allowed}:
        raise ValidationError(f"Invalid {label.lower()} '{value}'. Allowed: {', '.join(allowed)}")
    return text


def is_allowed_content_status(value: str) -> bool:
    return (value or "").strip().lower() in ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES


def is_allowed_pitch_status(value: str) -> bool:
    return (value or "").strip().lower() in ALLOWED_PITCH_STATUSES


def is_allowed_account_status(value: str) -> bool:
    return (value or "").strip().lower() in ALLOWED_ACCOUNT_STATUSES


def is_allowed_content_kind(value: str) -> bool:
    return (value or "").strip().lower() in CONTENT_KINDS


def require_content_status(value: str) -> str:
    return require_choice(value if value is not None else "", "Status", ALLOWED_CONTENT_STATUSES)


def require_pitch_status(value: str) -> str:
    return require_choice(value if value is not None else "", "Status", ALLOWED_PITCH_STATUSES)


def require_account_status(value: str) -> str:
    return require_choice(value if value is not None else "", "Account status", ALLOWED_ACCOUNT_STATUSES)


def require_content_kind(value: str) -> str:
    return require_choice(value if value is not None else "", "Content kind", CONTENT_KINDS)


def require_severity(value: Any) -> str:
    return require_choice(value if value is not None else "", "Severity", ALLOWED_SEVERITIES)


def require_priority(value: Any) -> str:
    return require_choice(value if value is not None else "", "Priority", ALLOWED_PRIORITIES)


def require_article_type(value: Any) -> str:
    return require_choice(value if value is not None else "", "Article type", ALLOWED_ARTICLE_TYPES)


def require_desk_status(value: Any) -> str:
    return require_choice(value if value is not None else "", "Status", ALLOWED_DESK_STATUSES)


def require_availability(value: Any) -> str:
    return require_choice(value if value is not None else "", "Availability", ALLOWED_AVAILABILITY)


def require_employment_status(value: Any) -> str:
    return require_choice(value if value is not None else "", "Employment status", ALLOWED_EMPLOYMENT)


def require_cleaning_status(value: Any) -> str:
    return require_choice(value if value is not None else "", "Cleaning status", ALLOWED_CLEANING_STATUS)


def require_positive_id(raw: Any, label: str = "ID") -> int:
    if isinstance(raw, int):
        value = raw
    else:
        text = "" if raw is None else str(raw).strip()
        if not text:
            raise ValidationError(f"{label} cannot be empty.")
        try:
            value = int(text)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise ValidationError(f"{label} must be a number.") from exc
    if value <= 0:
        raise ValidationError(f"{label} must be a positive number.")
    return value


def require_optional_positive_id(raw: Any, label: str = "ID") -> Optional[int]:
    if raw is None or str(raw).strip() == "":
        return None
    return require_positive_id(raw, label)


def require_username(value: Any) -> str:
    username = require_nonempty(value, "Username")
    if len(username) < 3:
        raise ValidationError("Username must be at least 3 characters.")
    if " " in username:
        raise ValidationError("Username cannot contain spaces.")
    return username


_PASSWORD_RULES = (
    (r".{10,}", "at least 10 characters"),
    (r"[A-Z]", "one uppercase letter"),
    (r"[a-z]", "one lowercase letter"),
    (r"\d", "one digit"),
    (r"[^A-Za-z0-9]", "one special character"),
)


def require_password(value: Any) -> str:
    """Strong password policy for change/reset (not applied to login of existing users)."""
    password = "" if value is None else str(value)
    if not password:
        raise ValidationError("Password cannot be empty.")
    if password.strip() != password:
        raise ValidationError("Password cannot start or end with spaces.")
    missing = [label for pattern, label in _PASSWORD_RULES if not re.search(pattern, password)]
    if missing:
        raise ValidationError(
            "Password must include: " + ", ".join(missing) + "."
        )
    if len(password) > 128:
        raise ValidationError("Password must be at most 128 characters.")
    return password


def require_role_code(value: Any) -> str:
    code = require_nonempty(value, "Role code").lower()
    if " " in code:
        raise ValidationError("Role code cannot contain spaces.")
    return code


def require_category_name(value: Any) -> str:
    name = require_nonempty(value, "Category name")
    if len(name) > 128:
        raise ValidationError("Category name must be at most 128 characters.")
    return name


def require_score(value: Any, label: str) -> float:
    try:
        score = float(value if value is not None else 0)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ValidationError(f"{label} must be a number.") from exc
    if score < 0 or score > 1_000:
        raise ValidationError(f"{label} must be between 0 and 1000.")
    return score


def require_read_time(value: Any) -> Optional[int]:
    if value is None or str(value).strip() == "":
        return None
    try:
        minutes = int(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ValidationError("Estimated read time must be a whole number.") from exc
    if minutes < 1 or minutes > 240:
        raise ValidationError("Estimated read time must be between 1 and 240 minutes.")
    return minutes


def validate_content_record(record: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
    errors: list[str] = []
    title = record.get("title", "")
    body = record.get("content_body") or record.get("text") or ""
    category = str(record.get("category") or record.get("category_name") or "").lower()
    headline_ok = category in {"headline", "headlines", "sarcasm"} or (
        str(title).strip() == str(body).strip() and len(str(title).strip()) >= MIN_TITLE_LENGTH
    )
    if not is_valid_title(title):
        errors.append("title_too_short")
    if not is_valid_body(body, headline_ok=headline_ok):
        errors.append("body_too_short")
    status = (record.get("status") or "draft").lower()
    if not is_allowed_content_status(status):
        errors.append("invalid_status")
    return errors
